# F5B — Balcão, débito com limite e recarga por Pix — Implementation Plan

> **For agentic workers:** REQUIRED SUB-SKILL: Use superpowers:subagent-driven-development (recommended) or superpowers:executing-plans to implement this plan task-by-task. Steps use checkbox (`- [ ]`) syntax for tracking.

**Goal:** A cozinha (no tablet, inclusive offline) e a secretaria (no admin) lançam o lanche de cada dia direto na carteira do responsável financeiro do aluno; o saldo pode ficar negativo até o limite da escola; o débito e os créditos são pagos por Pix (pelo app do responsável ou por um QR na tela da secretaria), e o responsável recebe avisos por WhatsApp.

**Architecture:** Na API, um módulo novo `counter` grava cada lançamento em `counter_sales` (+ `counter_sale_items`) com preço do servidor e lança `counter_charge` na carteira (append-only, `FOR UPDATE`), aceitando o lote offline do tablet sempre (marcando `over_limit`) e recusando o da secretaria acima do limite sem confirmação; estornos lançam `counter_void`. Um módulo novo `topups` cria recargas com cobrança em `payments` (coluna nova `topup_id`), e a única `confirmPayment` ganha o ramo de recarga (`topup_credit`). Avisos saem pela outbox (`wallet.debt_notice`, `wallet.payment_requested`, `wallet.topup_confirmed`); o n8n pede um magic link novo de escopo `wallet` (7 dias). A agenda existente (`list_due_actions()`, recriada com `CREATE OR REPLACE`) passa a listar `send_debt_notices` (n8n) e `reconcile_topups` (job do Cloud Scheduler). Na web: aba "Lançar" no KDS (Dexie versão 2 com `roster` e `counterSales`, outbox com `kind`), página "Contas" no admin e a carteira do responsável com Pix.

**Tech Stack:** Fastify 5, Kysely, PostgreSQL 16 (RLS, índice único parcial, `GRANT UPDATE (coluna)`), zod 4, React 19, react-router 8, reactstrap (admin/KDS), Dexie 4, Vitest + Testcontainers, Vitest + jsdom + Testing Library, Playwright. Nenhuma dependência nova.

**Spec:** `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-11-19-balcao-carteira-design.md` (autoritativa para esta fase; decisões do usuário), com `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-11-16-app-lanche-design.md` (seções 4.1–4.4, 5.5, 5.7, 5.8, 6.1–6.3, 7) e `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-11-19-ui-architectui-design.md` (telas novas).

## Global Constraints

- CLAUDE.md inteiro vale. Em especial: todo acesso ao banco dentro de `withTenant(schoolId, …)`; `school_id` só da sessão, do caminho HMAC (rotas internas) ou da agenda `list_due_actions()` (jobs); **nenhuma função `SECURITY DEFINER` nova** — `list_due_actions()` é recriada com `CREATE OR REPLACE` e a mesma assinatura; `getDb` não entra em nenhum arquivo novo (a allowlist do ESLint não muda).
- Toda tabela nova (`counter_sales`, `counter_sale_items`, `topups`): `school_id uuid NOT NULL`, RLS `tenant_isolation` (`school_id = app_school_id()`), FKs compostas `(school_id, …)`, entra em `TENANT_TABLES` de `apps/api/src/db/rls.test.ts` (ordem de bytes) e em `seedTenant`.
- Dinheiro em centavos inteiros (`bigint` / `number` inteiro, sufixo `Cents`/`_cents`); preço e total sempre calculados no servidor a partir de `menu_items.price_cents` atual; `wallet_entries` append-only; todo lançamento passa por `wallet.postEntry` (trava `wallets` `FOR UPDATE`).
- Pagamento só é confirmado por `payments.confirmPayment` (webhook e conciliação chamam a mesma função); gateway fora de transação, com o `payment.id` como chave de idempotência do provedor; webhook direto na API.
- Ordem única de travas numa transação: `service_days → checkouts → topups → payments → guardian_students → counter_sales → wallets`. Lotes que travam várias carteiras as travam juntas, ordenadas por `guardian_id` (`wallet.lockBalances`).
- `Idempotency-Key` (helper `idempotent` + `requestHash` + `idempotencyKeyFrom`) em: lançamento da secretaria, recarga (responsável e equipe) e pedido de pagamento por WhatsApp. Naturalmente idempotentes, sem chave: sincronização do tablet (`counter-sales`, `counter-voids`, pelo id do cliente), estorno da secretaria (`…/void`), `PUT` do responsável financeiro e do limite, `POST /api/v1/topups/:id/charge` e as rotas internas/de job.
- Transições de estado com `UPDATE … WHERE status = '<anterior>'` (zero linhas = já feito, sem efeito colateral); dedup por constraint (`UNIQUE` do ledger, PK de `counter_sales`).
- Efeito externo só pela outbox (`enqueueEvent`) na transação da mudança. Eventos em `snake_case`, só primeiro nome e telefone; o link nunca vai no evento: o n8n pede o magic link de escopo `wallet` em `POST /internal/v1/schools/{school_id}/magic-links`.
- LGPD: nada de telefone, nome ou valor por aluno em log; `audit_log.detail` só com ids e booleanos.
- KDS (regras 32–36): componentes de `areas/kds` só leem e escrevem no IndexedDB; só `sync.ts` chama a API; lançamento e estorno são uma transação Dexie que grava a outbox e ajusta o saldo local; nada sai da outbox antes de o servidor devolver o id em `accepted` ou `rejected`; sem Background Sync API; ids gerados no cliente.
- Toda rota declara `config.auth`. Papéis: `OFFICE = ["admin", "secretaria"]`; `ACCOUNTS = ["admin", "secretaria", "cozinha"]`; `ADMIN = ["admin"]`. Responsável: `GUARDIAN_WALLET = { kind: "guardian", scope: ["order", "wallet"] }`.
- Contratos novos em `packages/contracts/src/{counter,topups}.ts` e nos arquivos existentes; corpos de `/api` em `camelCase`. Mudou contrato: schema, exemplo da spec e fixture do teste de contrato no mesmo commit (regra 23).
- Telas novas do admin seguem a spec de UI: `PageTitle`, `Card`, `Table responsive hover striped`, `Alert color="danger"` para erros, `StatusBadge`, `announce` + `StatusNotice` para sucesso. A área do responsável continua sem reactstrap/tema (ESLint barra).
- Migração aplicada nunca é editada; esta fase cria só a `0010_counter_wallet.sql`.
- O `CamelCasePlugin` converte nomes de coluna também em `sql\`…\``; `bigint` volta como `number` (parser de `apps/api/src/db/index.ts`).
- Texto de interface em português do Brasil exatamente como o plano dá; código, nomes e commits em inglês. Mensagens de commit terminam com:
  ```
  Co-Authored-By: Claude Opus 5.5 <noreply@anthropic.com>
  Claude-Session: https://claude.ai/code/session_01K86CbkJPwmS7fPKomVgzip
  ```
- O arquivo não rastreado `prompt.txt` na raiz é do usuário: nunca o commite (use caminhos explícitos no `git add`). Nada de `.superpowers/`, `test-results/` nem de build.
- Comandos focados: API `pnpm --filter @app-lanche/api exec vitest run <caminho relativo a apps/api>`; contratos `pnpm --filter @app-lanche/contracts test`; web `pnpm --filter @app-lanche/web exec vitest run <caminho relativo a apps/web>`.
- Regenerar os tipos Kysely depois da migração (da raiz; espere o Postgres aceitar conexões; desligue depois):
  `pnpm db:up; DATABASE_ADMIN_URL=postgres://postgres:postgres@localhost:5432/app_lanche APP_DB_PASSWORD=app_user pnpm db:migrate; DATABASE_URL=postgres://postgres:postgres@localhost:5432/app_lanche pnpm db:types; docker compose down`
- Nesta máquina WSL os testes de API (Testcontainers) só passam com o compose desligado: `pnpm app:down; docker compose down` antes de `pnpm test`.
- Antes de cada commit: `pnpm typecheck && pnpm lint` e os testes do pacote tocado.

## Decisões da fase (o executor não reabre)

1. **Saldo negativo e piso.** A migração remove `CHECK (balance_cents >= 0)` de `wallets`; o piso é regra do serviço. `wallet.postEntry` ganha `minBalanceCents?: number | null` (padrão `0`; `null` = sem piso) e só recusa **débito** que deixaria o saldo abaixo do piso — crédito sempre entra, mesmo com a carteira negativa. Pisos: checkout e ajuste manual `0` (débito só nasce no balcão); tablet offline `null`; secretaria `-debt_limit_cents`, ou `null` com `allowOverLimit: true`. O checkout passa a aplicar `max(0, min(saldo, total))` (carteira negativa não vira desconto nem aumenta o Pix). `postEntry` aceita `id` (o lançamento do balcão aponta para a entrada) e devolve `{ posted, entryId, previousBalanceCents, balanceCents }`.
2. **Responsável financeiro.** `guardian_students.financial`, índice único parcial `guardian_students_one_financial (school_id, student_id) WHERE financial`. A migração marca, por aluno, o vínculo do responsável mais antigo (`guardians.created_at`, depois `id`), porque `guardian_students` não guarda a data do vínculo. Importação CSV grava `financial = NOT EXISTS (outro financeiro do aluno)` com `ON CONFLICT (school_id, guardian_id, student_id) DO NOTHING` (alvo explícito: o índice parcial nunca é engolido em silêncio). Troca por `PUT /api/admin/v1/students/:id/financial-guardian` (`OFFICE`, 204, naturalmente idempotente, auditada), que trava os vínculos do aluno `FOR UPDATE` em ordem de `guardian_id`; o lançamento trava o vínculo financeiro `FOR SHARE` antes da carteira. Aluno sem responsável financeiro: lançamento recusado (tablet: `rejected`; secretaria: 422 `invalid_order`); no tablet aparece "Sem responsável financeiro" e o botão fica desligado.
3. **Lançamento.** `id` do cliente (tablet) ou `uuidv7()` (secretaria). `occurred_at = least(cliente, now() + 5 min)` pelo relógio do banco — limitar em vez de recusar, porque recusar depois de o aluno comer seria perder a venda (mesmo motivo do design para aplicar acima do limite). Preço = `menu_items.price_cents` atual de item **ativo** (o balcão não depende do cardápio do dia). Ordem no serviço: ids já existentes → `duplicate` (aceito); validação (aluno ativo, itens ativos e sem repetição, responsável financeiro) → `rejected`; trava das carteiras do lote; `counter_charge` com `id` gerado; se o ledger já tinha a entrada (corrida), `duplicate`; grava `counter_sales` com `wallet_entry_id` e os itens. `over_limit = saldo depois < -limite`, em qualquer origem. Lançamento não passa por corte nem romaneio.
4. **Estorno.** `counter_void` positivo na carteira do `guardian_id` gravado no lançamento (não no financeiro atual), `UPDATE counter_sales SET voided_at, void_entry_id WHERE voided_at IS NULL`; `app_user` só tem `UPDATE (voided_at, void_entry_id)` em `counter_sales`. Tablet: só lançamentos `source = 'kitchen'` do mesmo dia no fuso da escola (data local do lançamento = data local de `least(occurredAt do estorno, now())`); secretaria (`OFFICE`): qualquer lançamento. Já estornado = aceito sem efeito. Lotes de estorno travam os lançamentos em ordem de id e depois as carteiras.
5. **Avisos de débito.** Na transação do lançamento que leva o saldo de `>= 0` para `< 0`: `wallets.debt_notice_at = now()` e evento `wallet.debt_notice`. Repetição a cada 3 dias enquanto negativo: `list_due_actions()` (recriada) lista `send_debt_notices` para a escola com alguma carteira negativa e `debt_notice_at` nulo ou de 3+ dias, **só entre 09:00 e 19:00 no fuso da escola** (sem WhatsApp de madrugada; o primeiro aviso, na hora do lançamento, não tem janela). O n8n chama `POST /internal/v1/schools/{school_id}/debt-notices` (sem corpo), que faz `UPDATE wallets SET debt_notice_at = now() WHERE balance_cents < 0 AND (debt_notice_at IS NULL OR debt_notice_at <= now() - 3 dias) RETURNING …` e grava um evento por carteira (repetir não duplica).
6. **Escopo `wallet`.** `magic_links.scope` aceita `wallet`; validade `now() + 7 dias` (banco), sem `service_date`. Aceitam `["order", "wallet"]`: `GET /api/v1/wallet`, `POST /api/v1/topups`, `GET /api/v1/topups/:id`, `POST /api/v1/topups/:id/charge`. `GET /api/v1/me` continua aceitando qualquer escopo. Pedido, cardápio e documentos não aceitam `wallet`. A PWA leva `wallet` para `/carteira`.
7. **Recarga.** `topups` + `payments.topup_id` (`checkout_id` vira anulável; `CONSTRAINT payments_one_target CHECK ((checkout_id IS NULL) <> (topup_id IS NULL))`; `payments_one_per_topup` único parcial). Pix vale 60 min (`expires_at = now() + 60 min` do banco). Uma recarga pendente por responsável: criar outra cancela as pendentes (`topups → cancelled`, `payments → expired`). `confirmPayment` ganha o ramo de recarga (trava `topups → payments → wallets`): `topups` de `pending_payment|expired|cancelled` para `paid`, `topup_credit` (fonte `topup`, id da recarga) e `wallet.topup_confirmed` — recarga não tem corte, então pago depois de expirada ou cancelada também credita. Valores: responsável `{ purpose: "debt" }` (o servidor usa o débito atual; sem débito → 409 `wallet_no_debt`) ou `{ purpose: "credit", amountCents }` (R$ 10,00 a R$ 1.000,00); equipe `{ amountCents }` de R$ 0,01 a R$ 10.000,00 (a tela sugere o débito).
8. **Conciliação e expiração de recarga.** `list_due_actions()` lista `reconcile_topups` para escolas com recarga `pending_payment`; só o job novo `POST /jobs/v1/topups/reconcile` (Cloud Scheduler a cada 5 min) consome essa ação: consulta no gateway as cobranças pendentes e chama `confirmPayment`; depois expira as recargas cujo Pix venceu. `GET /internal/v1/schedule/due` não lista `reconcile_topups` (o n8n não toca pagamento, regra 21). Recargas canceladas não são conciliadas; o webhook as cobre.
9. **QR no balcão e papéis.** O QR é gerado no admin (página "Contas"), também por usuários de papel `cozinha`: o tablet não gera QR porque os componentes do KDS não chamam a API (regra 32). Contas, extrato, QR e link por WhatsApp: `ACCOUNTS`; lançar no admin, estornar e trocar o responsável financeiro: `OFFICE`; limite: lê `ACCOUNTS`, altera `ADMIN`.
10. **Limite da escola.** Não há tela de configuração da escola; o limite fica num cartão "Limite de débito" da página "Contas" (`GET`/`PUT /api/admin/v1/school/settings`). `GRANT UPDATE (debt_limit_cents) ON schools TO app_user`.
11. **Link por WhatsApp a pedido da secretaria.** `POST /api/admin/v1/guardians/:guardianId/payment-requests` (`ACCOUNTS`, `Idempotency-Key`), só com débito (senão 409 `wallet_no_debt`): evento `wallet.payment_requested` com o valor devido.
12. **Aviso de ultrapassagem na "Contas".** Lista os lançamentos `over_limit` não estornados recebidos nos últimos 7 dias (relógio do banco), até 50, mais recentes primeiro; no extrato cada lançamento `over_limit` tem o selo "acima do limite".
13. **Tablet (Dexie versão 2).** Mantém `outbox` e a chave `eventId`; o índice ganha `kind` e o upgrade marca as linhas antigas `kind: "delivery"`. Linhas: `delivery` (como antes), `counter_sale` (`eventId` = id do lançamento) e `counter_void` (`eventId` próprio, `saleId`). Stores novos `roster` (`studentId`, índice `guardianId`; aluno com o saldo local do responsável financeiro) e `counterSales` (`saleId`, índice `serviceDate`; lista do dia com "Desfazer"). Limite e itens em `meta` (`roster:limit`, `roster:items`, `roster:fetchedAt`). Saldo local = saldo do servidor − lançamentos pendentes + estornos pendentes, recalculado a cada download; lançar e estornar ajustam o saldo local na mesma transação. Sincronização: entregas, lançamentos e estornos, cada um em lotes de até 100 e nessa ordem; depois romaneio, mudanças e roster. Recusado: sai da outbox, o lançamento fica "recusado" na lista e o saldo local volta. `authLost`, `pair` e `unpair` apagam `roster`, `counterSales` e `roster:*`; a limpeza apaga `counterSales` de dias antigos e o roster baixado há mais de 2 dias.
14. **Contratos com saldo negativo.** `MeResponse.walletBalanceCents`, `WalletResponse.balanceCents`, `WalletAdjustmentResponse.balanceCents`, `ReminderRecipient.wallet_balance_cents` e `payment.late_credited.wallet_balance_cents` passam a inteiro com sinal.
15. **`counter_sale_items` sem coluna `id`.** Chave `(school_id, sale_id, menu_item_id)`, como `service_day_items`; o design lista exatamente essas colunas.
16. **Erro novo:** `wallet_no_debt` (409). Acima do limite na secretaria sem confirmação: 409 `insufficient_balance` (já existe).
17. **Seed de demonstração:** um responsável financeiro por aluno; Ana com +R$ 30,00 (ajuste), Bruno com −R$ 15,00 (lançamento da secretaria para Caio: misto quente + suco), Carla zerada, e um segundo responsável, Diego (`+5511900000004`), vinculado a Léo sem ser financeiro.

### Task 1: Migração `0010_counter_wallet.sql`, tipos e semente de teste

**Files:**
- Create: `db/migrations/0010_counter_wallet.sql`
- Regenerate: `apps/api/src/db/types.ts`
- Modify: `apps/api/src/db/rls.test.ts`, `apps/api/src/db/schema.test.ts`, `apps/api/test/helpers.ts`
- Modify: `apps/api/src/modules/payments/service.ts` (nulidade de `checkoutId` em `paymentViews`)

**Interfaces:**
- Produces (banco): `schools.debt_limit_cents`; `guardian_students.financial` + índice `guardian_students_one_financial`; `wallets.debt_notice_at` (sem `CHECK` de saldo); `wallet_entries` com `kind` `counter_charge|counter_void|topup_credit`, `source_type` `counter_sale|topup` e `CONSTRAINT wallet_entries_sign_check`; tabelas `counter_sales`, `counter_sale_items`, `topups`; `payments.topup_id`, `payments.checkout_id` anulável, `payments_one_target`, `payments_one_per_topup`; `magic_links.scope` com `wallet`; `list_due_actions()` com as ações `send_debt_notices` e `reconcile_topups`.
- Produces (Kysely): `DB.counterSales`, `DB.counterSaleItems`, `DB.topups`; `Schools.debtLimitCents`, `GuardianStudents.financial`, `Wallets.debtNoticeAt`, `Payments.topupId`.
- Produces (testes): `SeedIds.counterSaleId`, `SeedIds.topupId`; `seedFamily`/`seedTenant` gravam o vínculo com `financial: true`; `zoneAtLocalHour(hour: number): string` em `apps/api/test/helpers.ts`.

- [ ] **Step 1: Teste de esquema (falha)**

`apps/api/src/db/schema.test.ts`: apague o caso `it("rejects a negative wallet balance", …)` do `describe("F2 schema")` e acrescente no fim do arquivo:

```ts
describe("F5B schema", () => {
  let schoolId: string;
  let ids: SeedIds;

  beforeAll(async () => {
    schoolId = await createSchool();
    ids = await seedTenant(schoolId);
  });

  it("allows a negative wallet balance", async () => {
    await withTenant(schoolId, (trx) => trx.updateTable("wallets").set({ balanceCents: -1 }).where("id", "=", ids.walletId).execute());
    await withTenant(schoolId, (trx) => trx.updateTable("wallets").set({ balanceCents: 50 }).where("id", "=", ids.walletId).execute());
  });

  it("enforces the sign of the counter and top-up entry kinds", async () => {
    const entry = (kind: string, amountCents: number, sourceType: string) =>
      withTenant(schoolId, (trx) =>
        trx.insertInto("walletEntries").values({ id: uuidv7(), schoolId, walletId: ids.walletId, kind, amountCents, sourceType, sourceId: uuidv7() }).execute(),
      );
    await expect(entry("counter_charge", 100, "counter_sale")).rejects.toThrow(/wallet_entries_sign_check/);
    await expect(entry("counter_void", -100, "counter_sale")).rejects.toThrow(/wallet_entries_sign_check/);
    await expect(entry("topup_credit", -100, "topup")).rejects.toThrow(/wallet_entries_sign_check/);
    await expect(entry("topup_credit", 100, "nope")).rejects.toThrow(/check constraint/);
  });

  it("keeps at most one financial guardian per student", async () => {
    const other = uuidv7();
    await adminDb().insertInto("guardians").values({ id: other, schoolId, phoneE164: "+5511999990777", firstName: "Rui" }).execute();
    await expect(
      withTenant(schoolId, (trx) =>
        trx.insertInto("guardianStudents").values({ schoolId, guardianId: other, studentId: ids.studentId, financial: true }).execute(),
      ),
    ).rejects.toThrow(/guardian_students_one_financial/);
    await withTenant(schoolId, (trx) =>
      trx.insertInto("guardianStudents").values({ schoolId, guardianId: other, studentId: ids.studentId, financial: false }).execute(),
    );
  });

  it("lets the application update only the void columns of a counter sale, and never delete", async () => {
    await expect(
      withTenant(schoolId, (trx) => sql`UPDATE counter_sales SET total_cents = 1`.execute(trx)),
    ).rejects.toThrow(/permission denied/);
    await withTenant(schoolId, (trx) => sql`UPDATE counter_sales SET voided_at = voided_at`.execute(trx));
    await expect(withTenant(schoolId, (trx) => sql`DELETE FROM counter_sales`.execute(trx))).rejects.toThrow(/permission denied/);
  });

  it("counter_sale_items is append-only for the application role", async () => {
    await expect(
      withTenant(schoolId, (trx) => sql`UPDATE counter_sale_items SET qty = qty`.execute(trx)),
    ).rejects.toThrow(/permission denied/);
    await expect(withTenant(schoolId, (trx) => sql`DELETE FROM counter_sale_items`.execute(trx))).rejects.toThrow(/permission denied/);
  });

  it("ties a payment to exactly one checkout or top-up", async () => {
    const payment = (checkoutId: string | null, topupId: string | null) =>
      withTenant(schoolId, (trx) =>
        trx
          .insertInto("payments")
          .values({ id: uuidv7(), schoolId, checkoutId, topupId, provider: "fake", method: "pix", amountCents: 100, expiresAt: new Date() })
          .execute(),
      );
    await expect(payment(null, null)).rejects.toThrow(/payments_one_target/);
    await expect(payment(ids.checkoutId, ids.topupId)).rejects.toThrow(/payments_one_target/);
    await payment(null, ids.topupId);
    await expect(payment(null, ids.topupId)).rejects.toThrow(/payments_one_per_topup/);
  });

  it("accepts the wallet magic-link scope and lets staff change only the debt limit of the school", async () => {
    await withTenant(schoolId, (trx) =>
      trx
        .insertInto("magicLinks")
        .values({ id: uuidv7(), schoolId, guardianId: ids.guardianId, tokenHash: `w-${uuidv7()}`, scope: "wallet", expiresAt: new Date(Date.now() + 60_000) })
        .execute(),
    );
    await withTenant(schoolId, (trx) => trx.updateTable("schools").set({ debtLimitCents: 7000 }).execute());
    await expect(withTenant(schoolId, (trx) => trx.updateTable("schools").set({ name: "x" }).execute())).rejects.toThrow(/permission denied/);
    await expect(withTenant(schoolId, (trx) => trx.updateTable("schools").set({ debtLimitCents: -1 }).execute())).rejects.toThrow(/check constraint/);
  });
});
```

Run: `pnpm --filter @app-lanche/api exec vitest run src/db/schema.test.ts` → FAIL (colunas e tabelas não existem; o TypeScript do Vitest não bloqueia, o banco recusa).

- [ ] **Step 2: Migração**

Antes de escrever, confira os nomes das constraints antigas num banco migrado (`pnpm db:up`, migre como no Global Constraints e rode):
`docker compose exec -T db psql -U postgres app_lanche -c "SELECT conrelid::regclass, conname, pg_get_constraintdef(oid) FROM pg_constraint WHERE conrelid IN ('wallets'::regclass, 'wallet_entries'::regclass, 'magic_links'::regclass) AND contype = 'c'"`. Os nomes esperados são `wallets_balance_cents_check`, `wallet_entries_kind_check`, `wallet_entries_source_type_check`, `wallet_entries_check` (a regra de sinal, constraint de tabela sem nome) e `magic_links_scope_check`; se algum diferir, use o nome real no `DROP CONSTRAINT`.

`db/migrations/0010_counter_wallet.sql`:

```sql
-- F5B: lançamento no balcão, débito com limite, recarga por Pix e avisos de débito
-- (spec 2026-11-19-balcao-carteira-design.md).

-- Limite de débito por escola (padrão R$ 50,00). A equipe só altera esta coluna de schools.
ALTER TABLE schools ADD COLUMN debt_limit_cents bigint NOT NULL DEFAULT 5000 CHECK (debt_limit_cents >= 0);
GRANT UPDATE (debt_limit_cents) ON schools TO app_user;

-- Responsável financeiro: no máximo um por aluno. guardian_students não guarda a data do vínculo:
-- o mais antigo é o do responsável cadastrado primeiro (created_at, depois o id UUID v7).
ALTER TABLE guardian_students ADD COLUMN financial boolean NOT NULL DEFAULT false;
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX guardian_students_one_financial ON guardian_students (school_id, student_id) WHERE financial;
UPDATE guardian_students gs SET financial = true
FROM (
  SELECT DISTINCT ON (l.school_id, l.student_id) l.school_id, l.student_id, l.guardian_id
  FROM guardian_students l JOIN guardians g ON g.id = l.guardian_id
  ORDER BY l.school_id, l.student_id, g.created_at, g.id
) first
WHERE gs.school_id = first.school_id AND gs.student_id = first.student_id AND gs.guardian_id = first.guardian_id;

-- O saldo pode ficar negativo (débito com limite); o piso é regra do serviço (wallet.postEntry).
ALTER TABLE wallets DROP CONSTRAINT wallets_balance_cents_check;
-- Último aviso de débito (wallet.debt_notice); repetido a cada 3 dias enquanto o saldo seguir negativo.
ALTER TABLE wallets ADD COLUMN debt_notice_at timestamptz;

ALTER TABLE wallet_entries DROP CONSTRAINT wallet_entries_kind_check;
ALTER TABLE wallet_entries ADD CONSTRAINT wallet_entries_kind_check CHECK (kind IN (
  'checkout_hold', 'hold_release', 'late_payment_credit', 'cancel_credit', 'refund', 'manual_adjustment',
  'counter_charge', 'counter_void', 'topup_credit'));
ALTER TABLE wallet_entries DROP CONSTRAINT wallet_entries_source_type_check;
ALTER TABLE wallet_entries ADD CONSTRAINT wallet_entries_source_type_check
  CHECK (source_type IN ('checkout', 'payment', 'adjustment', 'counter_sale', 'topup'));
ALTER TABLE wallet_entries DROP CONSTRAINT wallet_entries_check;
ALTER TABLE wallet_entries ADD CONSTRAINT wallet_entries_sign_check CHECK (
  (kind IN ('checkout_hold', 'refund', 'counter_charge') AND amount_cents < 0)
  OR (kind IN ('hold_release', 'late_payment_credit', 'cancel_credit', 'counter_void', 'topup_credit') AND amount_cents > 0)
  OR kind = 'manual_adjustment'
);

-- Um registro por lançamento no balcão. O id vem do tablet (regra 36) ou da API (secretaria).
CREATE TABLE counter_sales (
  id uuid PRIMARY KEY,
  school_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES schools (id),
  student_id uuid NOT NULL,
  guardian_id uuid NOT NULL,
  source text NOT NULL CHECK (source IN ('kitchen', 'staff')),
  device_id uuid,
  staff_user_id uuid,
  occurred_at timestamptz NOT NULL,
  received_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  total_cents bigint NOT NULL CHECK (total_cents > 0),
  over_limit boolean NOT NULL,
  wallet_entry_id uuid NOT NULL,
  voided_at timestamptz,
  void_entry_id uuid,
  UNIQUE (school_id, id),
  FOREIGN KEY (school_id, student_id) REFERENCES students (school_id, id),
  FOREIGN KEY (school_id, guardian_id) REFERENCES guardians (school_id, id),
  FOREIGN KEY (school_id, device_id) REFERENCES devices (school_id, id),
  FOREIGN KEY (school_id, staff_user_id) REFERENCES staff_users (school_id, id),
  FOREIGN KEY (school_id, wallet_entry_id) REFERENCES wallet_entries (school_id, id),
  FOREIGN KEY (school_id, void_entry_id) REFERENCES wallet_entries (school_id, id),
  CHECK ((source = 'kitchen') = (device_id IS NOT NULL)),
  CHECK ((source = 'staff') = (staff_user_id IS NOT NULL)),
  CHECK ((voided_at IS NULL) = (void_entry_id IS NULL))
);
CREATE INDEX counter_sales_guardian ON counter_sales (school_id, guardian_id, occurred_at DESC);
CREATE INDEX counter_sales_student ON counter_sales (school_id, student_id, occurred_at DESC);
CREATE INDEX counter_sales_over_limit ON counter_sales (school_id, received_at) WHERE over_limit AND voided_at IS NULL;

CREATE TABLE counter_sale_items (
  school_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES schools (id),
  sale_id uuid NOT NULL,
  menu_item_id uuid NOT NULL,
  qty integer NOT NULL CHECK (qty BETWEEN 1 AND 10),
  unit_price_cents bigint NOT NULL CHECK (unit_price_cents > 0),
  PRIMARY KEY (school_id, sale_id, menu_item_id),
  FOREIGN KEY (school_id, sale_id) REFERENCES counter_sales (school_id, id),
  FOREIGN KEY (school_id, menu_item_id) REFERENCES menu_items (school_id, id)
);

-- Recarga por Pix (pagar débito ou colocar créditos). A cobrança fica em payments.topup_id.
CREATE TABLE topups (
  id uuid PRIMARY KEY,
  school_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES schools (id),
  guardian_id uuid NOT NULL,
  amount_cents bigint NOT NULL CHECK (amount_cents > 0 AND amount_cents <= 1000000),
  status text NOT NULL DEFAULT 'pending_payment' CHECK (status IN ('pending_payment', 'paid', 'expired', 'cancelled')),
  requested_by text NOT NULL CHECK (requested_by IN ('guardian', 'staff')),
  created_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  paid_at timestamptz,
  UNIQUE (school_id, id),
  FOREIGN KEY (school_id, guardian_id) REFERENCES guardians (school_id, id),
  CHECK ((status = 'paid') = (paid_at IS NOT NULL))
);
CREATE INDEX topups_guardian ON topups (school_id, guardian_id, created_at DESC);
CREATE INDEX topups_pending ON topups (school_id) WHERE status = 'pending_payment';

ALTER TABLE payments ALTER COLUMN checkout_id DROP NOT NULL;
ALTER TABLE payments ADD COLUMN topup_id uuid;
ALTER TABLE payments ADD CONSTRAINT payments_topup_fk FOREIGN KEY (school_id, topup_id) REFERENCES topups (school_id, id);
ALTER TABLE payments ADD CONSTRAINT payments_one_target CHECK ((checkout_id IS NULL) <> (topup_id IS NULL));
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX payments_one_per_topup ON payments (school_id, topup_id) WHERE topup_id IS NOT NULL;

ALTER TABLE magic_links DROP CONSTRAINT magic_links_scope_check;
ALTER TABLE magic_links ADD CONSTRAINT magic_links_scope_check CHECK (scope IN ('order', 'documents', 'wallet'));

DO $$
DECLARE t text;
BEGIN
  FOREACH t IN ARRAY ARRAY['counter_sales', 'counter_sale_items', 'topups']
  LOOP
    EXECUTE format('ALTER TABLE %I ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY', t);
    EXECUTE format('CREATE POLICY tenant_isolation ON %I USING (school_id = app_school_id())', t);
  END LOOP;
END $$;

-- Lançamento é imutável; só o estorno marca voided_at/void_entry_id. Itens são append-only.
GRANT SELECT, INSERT ON counter_sales, counter_sale_items TO app_user;
GRANT UPDATE (voided_at, void_entry_id) ON counter_sales TO app_user;
GRANT SELECT, INSERT, UPDATE ON topups TO app_user;

-- Agenda (spec 4.4): mesma função e assinatura, com duas ações novas. Nenhuma função SECURITY DEFINER nova.
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION list_due_actions()
RETURNS TABLE (action text, school_id uuid, service_date date, overdue boolean)
LANGUAGE sql STABLE SECURITY DEFINER SET search_path = pg_catalog, public, pg_temp
AS $$
  SELECT 'send_reminders'::text, d.school_id, d.service_date, false
  FROM service_days d JOIN schools s ON s.id = d.school_id
  WHERE d.status = 'open'
    AND now() >= d.cutoff_at - make_interval(mins => s.reminder_lead_minutes)
    AND now() < d.cutoff_at
    AND NOT EXISTS (
      SELECT 1 FROM reminder_batches b
      WHERE b.school_id = d.school_id AND b.service_date = d.service_date AND b.completed_at IS NOT NULL)
  UNION ALL
  SELECT 'close_service_day'::text, d.school_id, d.service_date, now() >= d.cutoff_at + interval '10 minutes'
  FROM service_days d
  WHERE d.status = 'open' AND now() >= d.freeze_at
  UNION ALL
  -- Aviso de débito: no máximo a cada 3 dias enquanto negativo, só das 09:00 às 19:00 no fuso da escola.
  SELECT 'send_debt_notices'::text, s.id, (now() AT TIME ZONE s.timezone)::date, false
  FROM schools s
  WHERE (now() AT TIME ZONE s.timezone)::time >= time '09:00'
    AND (now() AT TIME ZONE s.timezone)::time < time '19:00'
    AND EXISTS (
      SELECT 1 FROM wallets w
      WHERE w.school_id = s.id AND w.balance_cents < 0
        AND (w.debt_notice_at IS NULL OR w.debt_notice_at <= now() - interval '3 days'))
  UNION ALL
  -- Recargas pendentes: conciliação e expiração pelo job do Cloud Scheduler, nunca pelo n8n (regra 21).
  SELECT 'reconcile_topups'::text, s.id, (now() AT TIME ZONE s.timezone)::date, false
  FROM schools s
  WHERE EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM topups t WHERE t.school_id = s.id AND t.status = 'pending_payment')
  ORDER BY 3, 2, 1
$$;
```

(`CREATE OR REPLACE` mantém o dono, o `REVOKE … FROM PUBLIC` e o `GRANT EXECUTE` da 0007.)

- Acrescente no fim:

```ts
/**
 * Fuso `Etc/GMT±N` em que agora são `hour` horas (janela 09:00–19:00 dos avisos de débito). Nos nomes Etc/GMT o sinal
 * é invertido: Etc/GMT-3 é UTC+3.
 */
export function zoneAtLocalHour(hour: number): string {
  const diff = (((hour - new Date().getUTCHours()) % 24) + 24) % 24;
  const offset = diff >= 12 ? diff - 24 : diff; // -12..11
  if (offset === 0) return "Etc/GMT";
  return offset > 0 ? `Etc/GMT-${offset}` : `Etc/GMT+${-offset}`;
}
```

`packages/contracts/src/counter.test.ts`:

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { CounterSaleItems, StaffCounterSaleRequest } from "./counter";
import { KdsCounterSalesRequest, KdsCounterVoidsRequest } from "./kds";

const A = "0193a3b0-0000-7000-8000-000000000001";
const B = "0193a3b0-0000-7000-8000-000000000002";

describe("counter contracts", () => {
  it("accepts items and quantities only, without prices", () => {
    expect(CounterSaleItems.parse([{ menuItemId: A, qty: 2 }])).toEqual([{ menuItemId: A, qty: 2 }]);
    expect(() => CounterSaleItems.parse([{ menuItemId: A, qty: 2, priceCents: 1 }])).not.toThrow();
    expect(CounterSaleItems.parse([{ menuItemId: A, qty: 2, priceCents: 1 }])).toEqual([{ menuItemId: A, qty: 2 }]);
  });

  it("rejects repeated items, empty sales and quantities out of range", () => {
    expect(() => CounterSaleItems.parse([{ menuItemId: A, qty: 1 }, { menuItemId: A, qty: 1 }])).toThrow();
    expect(() => CounterSaleItems.parse([])).toThrow();
    expect(() => CounterSaleItems.parse([{ menuItemId: A, qty: 11 }])).toThrow();
  });

  it("takes the over-limit confirmation only from staff", () => {
    const body = { studentId: B, items: [{ menuItemId: A, qty: 1 }], allowOverLimit: true };
    expect(StaffCounterSaleRequest.parse(body)).toEqual(body);
    expect(() => StaffCounterSaleRequest.parse({ ...body, guardianId: A })).toThrow();
  });

  it("caps tablet batches at 100", () => {
    const sale = { id: A, studentId: B, items: [{ menuItemId: A, qty: 1 }], occurredAt: "2026-11-19T10:05:00-03:00" };
    expect(KdsCounterSalesRequest.parse({ sales: [sale] })).toEqual({ sales: [sale] });
    expect(() => KdsCounterSalesRequest.parse({ sales: Array.from({ length: 101 }, () => sale) })).toThrow();
    const v = { saleId: A, occurredAt: "2026-11-19T10:06:00-03:00" };
    expect(KdsCounterVoidsRequest.parse({ voids: [v] })).toEqual({ voids: [v] });
    expect(() => KdsCounterVoidsRequest.parse({ voids: [] })).toThrow();
  });
});
```

`packages/contracts/src/topups.test.ts`:

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { GuardianTopupRequest, StaffTopupRequest, TOPUP_MAX_CENTS, TOPUP_MIN_CENTS } from "./topups";

describe("topup contracts", () => {
  it("lets the guardian pay the debt without an amount", () => {
    expect(GuardianTopupRequest.parse({ purpose: "debt" })).toEqual({ purpose: "debt" });
    expect(() => GuardianTopupRequest.parse({ purpose: "debt", amountCents: 500 })).toThrow();
  });

  it("limits free credits to R$ 10,00 – R$ 1.000,00", () => {
    expect(GuardianTopupRequest.parse({ purpose: "credit", amountCents: TOPUP_MIN_CENTS })).toEqual({ purpose: "credit", amountCents: 1000 });
    expect(GuardianTopupRequest.parse({ purpose: "credit", amountCents: TOPUP_MAX_CENTS })).toEqual({ purpose: "credit", amountCents: 100000 });
    expect(() => GuardianTopupRequest.parse({ purpose: "credit", amountCents: 999 })).toThrow();
    expect(() => GuardianTopupRequest.parse({ purpose: "credit", amountCents: 100001 })).toThrow();
    expect(() => GuardianTopupRequest.parse({ purpose: "credit", amountCents: 1500.5 })).toThrow();
  });

  it("lets staff charge any positive amount up to R$ 10.000,00", () => {
    expect(StaffTopupRequest.parse({ amountCents: 1 })).toEqual({ amountCents: 1 });
    expect(() => StaffTopupRequest.parse({ amountCents: 0 })).toThrow();
    expect(() => StaffTopupRequest.parse({ amountCents: 1_000_001 })).toThrow();
  });
});
```

```ts
  it("accepts the wallet event examples from spec 6.3", () => {
    const base = { school_id: "0193a3b0-0000-7000-8000-000000000002", occurred_at: "2026-11-19T10:05:00-03:00" };
    const contact = { guardian_id: "0193a3b0-0000-7000-8000-000000000003", phone: "+5511999990000", first_name: "Ana" };

      { ...base, id: "0193a3b0-0000-7000-8000-000000000021", type: "wallet.debt_notice", data: { ...contact, amount_due_cents: 1350 } },
      { ...base, id: "0193a3b0-0000-7000-8000-000000000022", type: "wallet.payment_requested", data: { ...contact, amount_due_cents: 1350 } },
      { ...base, id: "0193a3b0-0000-7000-8000-000000000023", type: "wallet.topup_confirmed", data: { ...contact, amount_cents: 5000, wallet_balance_cents: 3650 } },
    ];
    for (const e of examples) {

    }

    expect(
      OutboxEvent.parse({ type: "payment.late_credited", data: { ...contact, amount_cents: 900, wallet_balance_cents: -300 } }).type,
    ).toBe("payment.late_credited");
  });
```

```ts
import { z } from "zod";
import { Cents, Uuid } from "./common";

/** O cliente envia só itens e quantidades; preço e total vêm do servidor (CLAUDE.md regra 7). */
export const CounterSaleItems = z
  .array(z.object({ menuItemId: Uuid, qty: z.number().int().min(1).max(10) }))
  .min(1)
  .max(10)
  .refine((items) => new Set(items.map((i) => i.menuItemId)).size === items.length, "duplicate item");
export type CounterSaleItems = z.infer<typeof CounterSaleItems>;

/** POST /api/admin/v1/counter-sales — `allowOverLimit` é a confirmação explícita da secretaria. */
export const StaffCounterSaleRequest = z
  .object({ studentId: Uuid, items: CounterSaleItems, allowOverLimit: z.boolean().optional() })
  .strict();
export type StaffCounterSaleRequest = z.infer<typeof StaffCounterSaleRequest>;

export const CounterSaleResult = z.object({ saleId: Uuid, totalCents: Cents, balanceCents: z.number().int(), overLimit: z.boolean() });
export type CounterSaleResult = z.infer<typeof CounterSaleResult>;

/** POST /api/admin/v1/counter-sales/:id/void */
export const CounterVoidResult = z.object({ saleId: Uuid, balanceCents: z.number().int() });
export type CounterVoidResult = z.infer<typeof CounterVoidResult>;

/** GET /api/kds/v1/roster e GET /api/admin/v1/counter/roster: alunos ativos, responsável financeiro com saldo, limite e itens ativos. */
export const CounterRosterResponse = z.object({
  debtLimitCents: z.number().int().nonnegative(),
  items: z.array(z.object({ id: Uuid, name: z.string(), priceCents: Cents })),
  students: z.array(
    z.object({
      id: Uuid,
      firstName: z.string(),
      className: z.string(),
      restrictions: z.string().nullable(),
      guardian: z.object({ id: Uuid, firstName: z.string(), balanceCents: z.number().int() }).nullable(),
    }),
  ),
});
export type CounterRosterResponse = z.infer<typeof CounterRosterResponse>;
```

```ts
import { z } from "zod";
import { PaymentView } from "./checkout";
import { Cents, IsoDateTime, Uuid } from "./common";

export const TopupStatus = z.enum(["pending_payment", "paid", "expired", "cancelled"]);
export type TopupStatus = z.infer<typeof TopupStatus>;

export const TOPUP_MIN_CENTS = 1000;
export const TOPUP_MAX_CENTS = 100_000;

/** POST /api/v1/topups — "Pagar débito" (o servidor usa o débito atual) ou "Adicionar créditos" (valor livre). */
export const GuardianTopupRequest = z.discriminatedUnion("purpose", [
  z.object({ purpose: z.literal("debt") }).strict(),
  z.object({ purpose: z.literal("credit"), amountCents: z.number().int().min(TOPUP_MIN_CENTS).max(TOPUP_MAX_CENTS) }).strict(),
]);
export type GuardianTopupRequest = z.infer<typeof GuardianTopupRequest>;

/** POST /api/admin/v1/guardians/:guardianId/topups — QR na tela do balcão. */
export const StaffTopupRequest = z.object({ amountCents: Cents }).strict();
export type StaffTopupRequest = z.infer<typeof StaffTopupRequest>;

export const TopupView = z.object({
  id: Uuid,
  status: TopupStatus,
  amountCents: Cents,
  createdAt: IsoDateTime,
  paidAt: IsoDateTime.nullable(),
  /** Saldo atual da carteira do responsável da recarga. */
  balanceCents: z.number().int(),
  payment: PaymentView.nullable(),
});
export type TopupView = z.infer<typeof TopupView>;
```

```ts
import { z } from "zod";
import { Cents, IsoDateTime, Uuid } from "./common";

export const WalletEntryKind = z.enum([
  "checkout_hold",
  "hold_release",
  "late_payment_credit",
  "cancel_credit",
  "refund",
  "manual_adjustment",
  "counter_charge",
  "counter_void",
  "topup_credit",
]);
export type WalletEntryKind = z.infer<typeof WalletEntryKind>;

export const WalletResponse = z.object({
  balanceCents: z.number().int(),
  entries: z.array(z.object({ id: Uuid, kind: WalletEntryKind, amountCents: z.number().int(), createdAt: IsoDateTime })),
});
export type WalletResponse = z.infer<typeof WalletResponse>;

export const WalletAdjustmentRequest = z.object({
  amountCents: z
    .number()
    .int()
    .min(-1_000_000)
    .max(1_000_000)
    .refine((v) => v !== 0, "zero adjustment"),
  reason: z.string().trim().min(1).max(200),
});
export type WalletAdjustmentRequest = z.infer<typeof WalletAdjustmentRequest>;

export const WalletAdjustmentResponse = z.object({ balanceCents: z.number().int() });
export type WalletAdjustmentResponse = z.infer<typeof WalletAdjustmentResponse>;

/** Linha de extrato: lançamento do balcão traz o aluno, o selo de ultrapassagem e se foi estornado. */
export const StatementEntry = z.object({
  id: Uuid,
  kind: WalletEntryKind,
  amountCents: z.number().int(),
  createdAt: IsoDateTime,
  saleId: Uuid.nullable(),
  studentFirstName: z.string().nullable(),
  overLimit: z.boolean(),
  voided: z.boolean(),
});
export type StatementEntry = z.infer<typeof StatementEntry>;

/** GET /api/admin/v1/accounts — débitos primeiro; `students` = alunos de quem ele é o financeiro. */
export const AccountListResponse = z.object({
  debtLimitCents: z.number().int().nonnegative(),
  accounts: z.array(z.object({ guardianId: Uuid, firstName: z.string(), students: z.array(z.string()), balanceCents: z.number().int() })),
  overLimitSales: z.array(
    z.object({
      saleId: Uuid,
      guardianId: Uuid,
      guardianFirstName: z.string(),
      studentFirstName: z.string(),
      occurredAt: IsoDateTime,
      totalCents: Cents,
    }),
  ),
});
export type AccountListResponse = z.infer<typeof AccountListResponse>;

/** GET /api/admin/v1/guardians/:guardianId/statement */
export const AccountStatementResponse = z.object({
  guardian: z.object({ id: Uuid, firstName: z.string() }),
  balanceCents: z.number().int(),
  debtLimitCents: z.number().int().nonnegative(),
  entries: z.array(StatementEntry),
});
export type AccountStatementResponse = z.infer<typeof AccountStatementResponse>;

/** GET/PUT /api/admin/v1/school/settings */
export const SchoolSettings = z.object({ debtLimitCents: z.number().int().min(0).max(1_000_000) }).strict();
export type SchoolSettings = z.infer<typeof SchoolSettings>;

/** POST /api/admin/v1/guardians/:guardianId/payment-requests */
export const PaymentRequestResponse = z.object({ amountDueCents: Cents });
export type PaymentRequestResponse = z.infer<typeof PaymentRequestResponse>;
```

```ts
describe("wallet debt", () => {
  type Kind = "counter_charge" | "manual_adjustment" | "cancel_credit";
  const source = (kind: Kind) => (kind === "counter_charge" ? "counter_sale" : kind === "cancel_credit" ? "checkout" : "adjustment");

  it("credits a negative wallet and refuses only debits below the floor", async () => {
    const schoolId = await createSchool();
    const { guardianId } = await seedFamily(schoolId);
    const post = (kind: Kind, amountCents: number, minBalanceCents?: number | null) =>
      withTenant(schoolId, (trx) =>
        postEntry(trx, {
          schoolId,
          guardianId,
          kind,
          amountCents,
          sourceType: source(kind),
          sourceId: uuidv7(),
          ...(minBalanceCents === undefined ? {} : { minBalanceCents }),
        }),
      );
    expect(await post("counter_charge", -1000, null)).toMatchObject({ posted: true, previousBalanceCents: 0, balanceCents: -1000 });
    expect(await post("cancel_credit", 300)).toMatchObject({ posted: true, previousBalanceCents: -1000, balanceCents: -700 });
    await expect(post("manual_adjustment", -1)).rejects.toMatchObject({ code: "insufficient_balance" });
    await expect(post("counter_charge", -400, -1000)).rejects.toMatchObject({ code: "insufficient_balance" });
    expect(await post("counter_charge", -300, -1000)).toMatchObject({ balanceCents: -1000 });
    expect(await ledgerMatches(schoolId, guardianId)).toBe(true);
  });

  it("records the entry under the given id once", async () => {
    const schoolId = await createSchool();
    const { guardianId } = await seedFamily(schoolId);
    const id = uuidv7();
    const sourceId = uuidv7();
    const post = () =>
      withTenant(schoolId, (trx) =>
        postEntry(trx, { id, schoolId, guardianId, kind: "counter_charge", amountCents: -500, sourceType: "counter_sale", sourceId, minBalanceCents: null }),
      );
    expect(await post()).toEqual({ posted: true, entryId: id, previousBalanceCents: 0, balanceCents: -500 });
    expect(await post()).toEqual({ posted: false, entryId: null, previousBalanceCents: -500, balanceCents: -500 });
    const rows = await adminDb().selectFrom("walletEntries").select("id").where("schoolId", "=", schoolId).execute();
    expect(rows).toEqual([{ id }]);
  });

  it("notices only the start of a debt, with the amount due", async () => {
    const schoolId = await createSchool();
    const { guardianId } = await seedFamily(schoolId);
    await withTenant(schoolId, async (trx) => {
      await postEntry(trx, { schoolId, guardianId, kind: "counter_charge", amountCents: -300, sourceType: "counter_sale", sourceId: uuidv7(), minBalanceCents: null });
      await noticeDebtStart(trx, schoolId, guardianId, 200, -300);
      await noticeDebtStart(trx, schoolId, guardianId, -300, -500);
      await noticeDebtStart(trx, schoolId, guardianId, -500, 100);
    });
    const events = await adminDb()
      .selectFrom("outboxEvents")
      .select(sql<string>`data::text`.as("data"))
      .where("schoolId", "=", schoolId)
      .where("type", "=", "wallet.debt_notice")
      .execute();
    expect(events.map((e) => JSON.parse(e.data))).toEqual([
      { guardian_id: guardianId, phone: expect.stringMatching(/^\+55/), first_name: "Ana", amount_due_cents: 300 },
    ]);
    const wallet = await adminDb().selectFrom("wallets").select("debtNoticeAt").where("guardianId", "=", guardianId).executeTakeFirstOrThrow();
    expect(wallet.debtNoticeAt).not.toBeNull();
  });
});
```

`apps/api/src/modules/wallet/repo.ts`:

```ts
/** Trava as carteiras em ordem de guardian_id: lotes de balcão de dois tablets nunca se travam em ordens opostas. */
export async function lockWallets(trx: Trx, guardianIds: string[]): Promise<void> {
  if (guardianIds.length === 0) return;
  await trx.selectFrom("wallets").select("id").where("guardianId", "in", guardianIds).orderBy("guardianId").forUpdate().execute();
}

export async function markDebtNotice(trx: Trx, guardianId: string): Promise<void> {
  await trx.updateTable("wallets").set({ debtNoticeAt: sql<Date>`now()` }).where("guardianId", "=", guardianId).execute();
}

export async function guardianContact(trx: Trx, guardianId: string) {
  return trx.selectFrom("guardians").select(["phoneE164", "firstName"]).where("id", "=", guardianId).executeTakeFirstOrThrow();
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Serviço da carteira**

`apps/api/src/modules/wallet/service.ts`: importe `enqueueEvent` de `../../platform/outbox` e substitua `WalletSourceType` e `postEntry`:

```ts
export type WalletSourceType = "checkout" | "payment" | "adjustment" | "counter_sale" | "topup";
export type PostedEntry = { posted: boolean; entryId: string | null; previousBalanceCents: number; balanceCents: number };

/** Cria as carteiras que faltam e trava todas, em ordem de guardian_id (lotes do balcão). */
export async function lockBalances(trx: Trx, schoolId: string, guardianIds: string[]): Promise<void> {
  const ids = [...new Set(guardianIds)].sort();
  for (const id of ids) await repo.ensureWallet(trx, schoolId, id);
  await repo.lockWallets(trx, ids);
}

/**
 * Lança uma entrada no ledger. A unicidade (origem, tipo) torna a chamada idempotente: se a entrada já existe,
 * nada muda e `posted` é false.
 *
 * Piso (`minBalanceCents`, padrão 0; null = sem piso): só um débito que deixaria o saldo abaixo dele é recusado.
 * Crédito sempre entra, mesmo com a carteira negativa (spec F5B).
 */
export async function postEntry(
  trx: Trx,
  entry: {
    schoolId: string;
    guardianId: string;
    kind: WalletEntryKind;
    amountCents: number;
    sourceType: WalletSourceType;
    sourceId: string;
    note?: string;
    id?: string;
    minBalanceCents?: number | null;
  },
): Promise<PostedEntry> {
  if (!Number.isSafeInteger(entry.amountCents) || entry.amountCents === 0) throw new Error("invalid wallet amount");
  const wallet = await lockBalance(trx, entry.schoolId, entry.guardianId);
  const entryId = entry.id ?? uuidv7();
  const posted = await repo.insertEntry(trx, {
    id: entryId,
    schoolId: entry.schoolId,
    walletId: wallet.walletId,
    kind: entry.kind,
    amountCents: entry.amountCents,
    sourceType: entry.sourceType,
    sourceId: entry.sourceId,
    note: entry.note ?? null,
  });
  // Repetição: a entrada já existe e o saldo já a contém.
  if (!posted) return { posted: false, entryId: null, previousBalanceCents: wallet.balanceCents, balanceCents: wallet.balanceCents };
  const balanceCents = wallet.balanceCents + entry.amountCents;
  const floor = entry.minBalanceCents === undefined ? 0 : entry.minBalanceCents;
  // Lançar aqui desfaz a transação inteira, inclusive a entrada recém-inserida.
  if (entry.amountCents < 0 && floor !== null && balanceCents < floor) throw new DomainError("insufficient_balance");
  await repo.setBalance(trx, wallet.walletId, balanceCents);
  return { posted: true, entryId, previousBalanceCents: wallet.balanceCents, balanceCents };
}

/** Grava o aviso de débito (ou o pedido de pagamento da secretaria) para o responsável. Só primeiro nome (regra 29). */
export async function enqueueDebtNotice(
  trx: Trx,
  schoolId: string,
  guardianId: string,
  amountDueCents: number,
  type: "wallet.debt_notice" | "wallet.payment_requested" = "wallet.debt_notice",
): Promise<void> {
  const guardian = await repo.guardianContact(trx, guardianId);
  await enqueueEvent(trx, schoolId, {
    type,
    data: { guardian_id: guardianId, phone: guardian.phoneE164, first_name: guardian.firstName, amount_due_cents: amountDueCents },
  });
}

/** Saldo passou de >= 0 para < 0: marca o aviso (a agenda repete a cada 3 dias) e grava wallet.debt_notice. */
export async function noticeDebtStart(
  trx: Trx,
  schoolId: string,
  guardianId: string,
  previousBalanceCents: number,
  balanceCents: number,
): Promise<void> {
  if (!(previousBalanceCents >= 0 && balanceCents < 0)) return;
  await repo.markDebtNotice(trx, guardianId);
  await enqueueDebtNotice(trx, schoolId, guardianId, -balanceCents);
}
```

`adjustBalance` continua chamando `postEntry` sem `minBalanceCents` (piso 0: o ajuste manual não cria débito, mas um ajuste positivo numa carteira negativa entra).

```ts
import { sql } from "kysely";
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { adminDb, createSchool, staffCookie } from "../../../test/helpers";
import { buildApp } from "../../app";

const HEADER = "codigo_aluno,primeiro_nome,turma,responsavel_nome,responsavel_telefone";
const CSV = [HEADER, "F1,Léo,Infantil 3A,Ana,(11) 97777-0001", "F1,Léo,Infantil 3A,Rui,(11) 97777-0002", "F2,Bia,Infantil 3A,Rui,(11) 97777-0002"].join("\n");

function importCsv(cookies: Record<string, string>, csv: string) {
  return buildApp().inject({ method: "POST", url: "/api/admin/v1/import/students", cookies, payload: { csv } });
}

async function financialMap(schoolId: string): Promise<Record<string, string[]>> {
  const { rows } = await sql<{ student: string; guardian: string }>`
    SELECT s.first_name AS student, g.first_name AS guardian
    FROM guardian_students gs JOIN students s ON s.id = gs.student_id JOIN guardians g ON g.id = gs.guardian_id
    WHERE gs.school_id = ${schoolId} AND gs.financial ORDER BY 1, 2`.execute(adminDb());
  const out: Record<string, string[]> = {};
  for (const r of rows) (out[r.student] ??= []).push(r.guardian);
  return out;
}

async function ids(schoolId: string) {
  const students = await adminDb().selectFrom("students").select(["id", "firstName"]).where("schoolId", "=", schoolId).execute();
  const guardians = await adminDb().selectFrom("guardians").select(["id", "firstName"]).where("schoolId", "=", schoolId).execute();
  const by = (rows: { id: string; firstName: string }[], name: string) => rows.find((r) => r.firstName === name)!.id;
  return { leo: by(students, "Léo"), ana: by(guardians, "Ana"), rui: by(guardians, "Rui") };
}

describe("financial guardian", () => {
  it("marks the first imported guardian of each student as financial and keeps it on re-import", async () => {
    const schoolId = await createSchool();
    const { cookies } = await staffCookie(schoolId, "secretaria");
    expect((await importCsv(cookies, CSV)).json()).toMatchObject({ applied: true });
    expect(await financialMap(schoolId)).toEqual({ Bia: ["Rui"], "Léo": ["Ana"] });
    const reordered = [HEADER, "F1,Léo,Infantil 3A,Rui,(11) 97777-0002", "F1,Léo,Infantil 3A,Ana,(11) 97777-0001", "F2,Bia,Infantil 3A,Ana,(11) 97777-0001"].join("\n");
    expect((await importCsv(cookies, reordered)).json()).toMatchObject({ applied: true });
    expect(await financialMap(schoolId)).toEqual({ Bia: ["Rui"], "Léo": ["Ana"] });
  });

  it("changes the financial guardian idempotently, with audit, and lists it", async () => {
    const schoolId = await createSchool();
    const { cookies } = await staffCookie(schoolId, "secretaria");
    await importCsv(cookies, CSV);
    const { leo, rui } = await ids(schoolId);
    const put = () =>
      buildApp().inject({ method: "PUT", url: `/api/admin/v1/students/${leo}/financial-guardian`, cookies, payload: { guardianId: rui } });
    expect((await put()).statusCode).toBe(204);
    expect((await put()).statusCode).toBe(204);
    expect(await financialMap(schoolId)).toEqual({ Bia: ["Rui"], "Léo": ["Rui"] });
    const audit = await adminDb().selectFrom("auditLog").select("action").where("schoolId", "=", schoolId).where("action", "=", "student.financial_guardian_changed").execute();
    expect(audit).toHaveLength(1);
    const list = await buildApp().inject({ method: "GET", url: "/api/admin/v1/students", cookies });
    const student = list.json().students.find((s: { id: string }) => s.id === leo);
    expect(student.guardians.map((g: { firstName: string; financial: boolean }) => [g.firstName, g.financial])).toEqual([
      ["Ana", false],
      ["Rui", true],
    ]);
  });

  it("refuses a guardian not linked to the student and non-office roles", async () => {
    const schoolId = await createSchool();
    const office = await staffCookie(schoolId, "secretaria");
    await importCsv(office.cookies, CSV);
    const { leo } = await ids(schoolId);
    const bia = (await adminDb().selectFrom("students").select("id").where("schoolId", "=", schoolId).where("firstName", "=", "Bia").executeTakeFirstOrThrow()).id;
    const anaId = (await ids(schoolId)).ana;
    const put = (cookies: Record<string, string>, studentId: string, guardianId: string) =>
      buildApp().inject({ method: "PUT", url: `/api/admin/v1/students/${studentId}/financial-guardian`, cookies, payload: { guardianId } });
    // Ana é responsável de Léo, não de Bia: responsável de outro aluno não vira financeiro.
    expect((await put(office.cookies, bia, anaId)).statusCode).toBe(404);
    expect((await put(office.cookies, leo, crypto.randomUUID())).statusCode).toBe(404);
    const kitchen = await staffCookie(schoolId, "cozinha");
    expect((await put(kitchen.cookies, leo, anaId)).statusCode).toBe(403);
  });
});
```

```ts
/** Novo vínculo é o financeiro se o aluno ainda não tem um. O alvo explícito do ON CONFLICT não engole o índice parcial. */
export async function linkGuardianStudent(
  trx: Trx,
  row: { schoolId: string; guardianId: string; studentId: string },
): Promise<void> {
  await trx
    .insertInto("guardianStudents")
    .values({
      ...row,
      financial: sql<boolean>`NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM guardian_students f WHERE f.student_id = ${row.studentId} AND f.financial)`,
    })
    .onConflict((oc) => oc.columns(["schoolId", "guardianId", "studentId"]).doNothing())
    .execute();
}
```

Em `listGuardianLinks`, acrescente `"guardianStudents.financial"` ao `select`. Acrescente:

```ts
/** Vínculos do aluno, travados FOR UPDATE em ordem de responsável. Ordem de travas: guardian_students → wallets. */
export async function lockStudentLinks(trx: Trx, studentId: string) {
  return trx
    .selectFrom("guardianStudents")
    .select(["guardianId", "financial"])
    .where("studentId", "=", studentId)
    .orderBy("guardianId")
    .forUpdate()
    .execute();
}

export async function setFinancial(trx: Trx, studentId: string, guardianId: string): Promise<void> {
  // Primeiro desmarca o atual: o índice único parcial é conferido a cada comando.
  await trx
    .updateTable("guardianStudents")
    .set({ financial: false })
    .where("studentId", "=", studentId)
    .where("financial", "=", true)
    .where("guardianId", "<>", guardianId)
    .execute();
  await trx.updateTable("guardianStudents").set({ financial: true }).where("studentId", "=", studentId).where("guardianId", "=", guardianId).execute();
}
```

```ts
export async function setFinancialGuardian(session: SessionClaims, studentId: string, body: SetFinancialGuardianRequest): Promise<void> {
  await withTenant(session.schoolId, async (trx) => {
    const links = await repo.lockStudentLinks(trx, studentId);
    const target = links.find((l) => l.guardianId === body.guardianId);
    if (!target) throw new DomainError("not_found");
    if (target.financial) return; // já é: nada a fazer
    await repo.setFinancial(trx, studentId, body.guardianId);
    await audit(trx, {
      schoolId: session.schoolId,
      actorKind: "staff",
      actorId: session.sub,
      action: "student.financial_guardian_changed",
      detail: { student_id: studentId, guardian_id: body.guardianId },
    });
  });
}
```

```ts
import { sql } from "kysely";
import { uuidv7 } from "uuidv7";
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { adminDb, createSchool, seedFamily, staffCookie, walletLedgerMatches } from "../../../test/helpers";
import { buildApp } from "../../app";

async function setup(limitCents?: number) {
  const schoolId = await createSchool();
  if (limitCents !== undefined) await adminDb().updateTable("schools").set({ debtLimitCents: limitCents }).where("id", "=", schoolId).execute();
  const family = await seedFamily(schoolId, ["Léo", "Bia"]);
  const misto = uuidv7();
  const suco = uuidv7();
  await adminDb()
    .insertInto("menuItems")
    .values([
      { id: misto, schoolId, name: `Misto ${misto.slice(-6)}`, priceCents: 900 },
      { id: suco, schoolId, name: `Suco ${suco.slice(-6)}`, priceCents: 450 },
    ])
    .execute();
  const office = await staffCookie(schoolId, "secretaria");
  return { schoolId, guardianId: family.guardianId, leo: family.studentIds[0]!, bia: family.studentIds[1]!, misto, suco, office };
}

type Setup = Awaited<ReturnType<typeof setup>>;
const sell = (s: Setup, payload: object, key: string = uuidv7()) =>
  buildApp().inject({ method: "POST", url: "/api/admin/v1/counter-sales", cookies: s.office.cookies, headers: { "idempotency-key": key }, payload });
const voidSale = (s: Setup, saleId: string) =>
  buildApp().inject({ method: "POST", url: `/api/admin/v1/counter-sales/${saleId}/void`, cookies: s.office.cookies });

async function balance(guardianId: string): Promise<number> {
  const row = await adminDb().selectFrom("wallets").select("balanceCents").where("guardianId", "=", guardianId).executeTakeFirst();
  return row?.balanceCents ?? 0;
}

async function debtNotices(schoolId: string) {
  const rows = await adminDb()
    .selectFrom("outboxEvents")
    .select(sql<string>`data::text`.as("data"))
    .where("schoolId", "=", schoolId)
    .where("type", "=", "wallet.debt_notice")
    .execute();
  return rows.map((r) => JSON.parse(r.data) as { amount_due_cents: number; first_name: string });
}

describe("staff counter sales", () => {
  it("charges the financial guardian at the current server price and notices the start of the debt once", async () => {
    const s = await setup();
    await adminDb().updateTable("menuItems").set({ priceCents: 1000 }).where("id", "=", s.misto).execute();
    const res = await sell(s, { studentId: s.leo, items: [{ menuItemId: s.misto, qty: 2 }, { menuItemId: s.suco, qty: 1 }] });
    expect(res.statusCode).toBe(200);
    expect(res.json()).toMatchObject({ totalCents: 2450, balanceCents: -2450, overLimit: false });
    const saleId = res.json().saleId as string;
    const sale = await adminDb().selectFrom("counterSales").selectAll().where("id", "=", saleId).executeTakeFirstOrThrow();
    expect(sale).toMatchObject({ guardianId: s.guardianId, source: "staff", staffUserId: s.office.staffUserId, deviceId: null, totalCents: 2450, overLimit: false });
    const items = await adminDb().selectFrom("counterSaleItems").select(["menuItemId", "qty", "unitPriceCents"]).where("saleId", "=", saleId).orderBy("unitPriceCents").execute();
    expect(items).toEqual([
      { menuItemId: s.suco, qty: 1, unitPriceCents: 450 },
      { menuItemId: s.misto, qty: 2, unitPriceCents: 1000 },
    ]);
    const entry = await adminDb().selectFrom("walletEntries").select(["id", "kind", "amountCents"]).where("id", "=", sale.walletEntryId).executeTakeFirstOrThrow();
    expect(entry).toMatchObject({ kind: "counter_charge", amountCents: -2450 });
    await sell(s, { studentId: s.bia, items: [{ menuItemId: s.suco, qty: 1 }] });
    expect(await debtNotices(s.schoolId)).toEqual([expect.objectContaining({ amount_due_cents: 2450, first_name: "Ana" })]);
    expect(await walletLedgerMatches(s.schoolId, s.guardianId)).toBe(true);
  });

  it("refuses over the limit without confirmation and records the sale flagged with it", async () => {
    const s = await setup(1000);
    const body = { studentId: s.leo, items: [{ menuItemId: s.misto, qty: 2 }] };
    const refused = await sell(s, body);
    expect(refused.statusCode).toBe(409);
    expect(refused.json().error.code).toBe("insufficient_balance");
    expect(await adminDb().selectFrom("counterSales").select("id").where("schoolId", "=", s.schoolId).execute()).toEqual([]);
    const forced = await sell(s, { ...body, allowOverLimit: true });
    expect(forced.json()).toMatchObject({ totalCents: 1800, balanceCents: -1800, overLimit: true });
  });

  it("rejects inactive items, unknown students and students without a financial guardian", async () => {
    const s = await setup();
    await adminDb().updateTable("menuItems").set({ active: false }).where("id", "=", s.suco).execute();
    expect((await sell(s, { studentId: s.leo, items: [{ menuItemId: s.suco, qty: 1 }] })).json().error.code).toBe("invalid_order");
    expect((await sell(s, { studentId: uuidv7(), items: [{ menuItemId: s.misto, qty: 1 }] })).statusCode).toBe(422);
    await adminDb().updateTable("guardianStudents").set({ financial: false }).where("studentId", "=", s.bia).execute();
    expect((await sell(s, { studentId: s.bia, items: [{ menuItemId: s.misto, qty: 1 }] })).statusCode).toBe(422);
    expect(await balance(s.guardianId)).toBe(0);
  });

  it("replays the same Idempotency-Key without a second sale", async () => {
    const s = await setup();
    const key = uuidv7();
    const body = { studentId: s.leo, items: [{ menuItemId: s.misto, qty: 1 }] };
    const first = await sell(s, body, key);
    const again = await sell(s, body, key);
    expect(again.json()).toEqual(first.json());
    expect(await balance(s.guardianId)).toBe(-900);
  });

  it("voids once, crediting the sale's guardian, with audit", async () => {
    const s = await setup();
    const saleId = (await sell(s, { studentId: s.leo, items: [{ menuItemId: s.misto, qty: 1 }] })).json().saleId as string;
    const first = await voidSale(s, saleId);
    expect(first.json()).toEqual({ saleId, balanceCents: 0 });
    expect((await voidSale(s, saleId)).json()).toEqual({ saleId, balanceCents: 0 });
    const voids = await adminDb().selectFrom("walletEntries").select("amountCents").where("schoolId", "=", s.schoolId).where("kind", "=", "counter_void").execute();
    expect(voids).toEqual([{ amountCents: 900 }]);
    const sale = await adminDb().selectFrom("counterSales").select(["voidedAt", "voidEntryId"]).where("id", "=", saleId).executeTakeFirstOrThrow();
    expect(sale.voidedAt).not.toBeNull();
    const audit = await adminDb().selectFrom("auditLog").select("action").where("schoolId", "=", s.schoolId).where("action", "like", "counter.%").orderBy("createdAt").execute();
    expect(audit.map((a) => a.action)).toEqual(["counter.sale", "counter.void"]);
    expect((await voidSale(s, uuidv7())).statusCode).toBe(404);
    expect(await walletLedgerMatches(s.schoolId, s.guardianId)).toBe(true);
  });

  it("serves the roster to the office and keeps the kitchen role out of sales", async () => {
    const s = await setup();
    await adminDb().updateTable("menuItems").set({ active: false }).where("id", "=", s.suco).execute();
    const roster = await buildApp().inject({ method: "GET", url: "/api/admin/v1/counter/roster", cookies: s.office.cookies });
    expect(roster.json()).toMatchObject({ debtLimitCents: 5000, items: [{ id: s.misto, priceCents: 900 }] });
    expect(roster.json().students).toHaveLength(2);
    expect(roster.json().students[0].guardian).toMatchObject({ id: s.guardianId, firstName: "Ana", balanceCents: 0 });
    const kitchen = await staffCookie(s.schoolId, "cozinha");
    const res = await buildApp().inject({
      method: "POST",
      url: "/api/admin/v1/counter-sales",
      cookies: kitchen.cookies,
      headers: { "idempotency-key": uuidv7() },
      payload: { studentId: s.leo, items: [{ menuItemId: s.misto, qty: 1 }] },
    });
    expect(res.statusCode).toBe(403);
  });
});
```
