- [ ] **Step 2: Repo**

`apps/api/src/modules/counter/repo.ts`:

```ts
import { sql } from "kysely";
import type { Trx } from "../../db/index";

export async function existingSaleIds(trx: Trx, ids: string[]): Promise<Set<string>> {
  if (ids.length === 0) return new Set();
  const rows = await trx.selectFrom("counterSales").select("id").where("id", "in", ids).execute();
  return new Set(rows.map((r) => r.id));
}

export async function activeStudentIds(trx: Trx, ids: string[]): Promise<Set<string>> {
  if (ids.length === 0) return new Set();
  const rows = await trx.selectFrom("students").select("id").where("id", "in", ids).where("active", "=", true).execute();
  return new Set(rows.map((r) => r.id));
}

/** Preço atual dos itens ativos do cardápio: o servidor calcula o total (CLAUDE.md regra 7). */
export async function activeItemPrices(trx: Trx, ids: string[]): Promise<Map<string, number>> {
  if (ids.length === 0) return new Map();
  const rows = await trx.selectFrom("menuItems").select(["id", "priceCents"]).where("id", "in", ids).where("active", "=", true).execute();
  return new Map(rows.map((r) => [r.id, r.priceCents]));
}

/** Responsável financeiro de cada aluno, FOR SHARE (a troca trava FOR UPDATE). Ordem: guardian_students → wallets. */
export async function lockFinancialGuardians(trx: Trx, studentIds: string[]): Promise<Map<string, string>> {
  if (studentIds.length === 0) return new Map();
  const { rows } = await sql<{ studentId: string; guardianId: string }>`
    SELECT student_id, guardian_id FROM guardian_students
    WHERE student_id = ANY(${studentIds}::uuid[]) AND financial
    ORDER BY student_id FOR SHARE`.execute(trx);
  return new Map(rows.map((r) => [r.studentId, r.guardianId]));
}

export async function debtLimit(trx: Trx): Promise<number> {
  return (await trx.selectFrom("schools").select("debtLimitCents").executeTakeFirstOrThrow()).debtLimitCents;
}

/** occurred_at limitado a 5 min à frente do relógio do banco; sem instante do cliente (secretaria), now(). */
export async function insertSale(
  trx: Trx,
  row: {
    id: string;
    schoolId: string;
    studentId: string;
    guardianId: string;
    source: "kitchen" | "staff";
    deviceId: string | null;
    staffUserId: string | null;
    occurredAt: string | null;
    totalCents: number;
    overLimit: boolean;
    walletEntryId: string;
  },
): Promise<void> {
  await sql`
    INSERT INTO counter_sales (id, school_id, student_id, guardian_id, source, device_id, staff_user_id, occurred_at,
                               total_cents, over_limit, wallet_entry_id)
    VALUES (${row.id}, ${row.schoolId}, ${row.studentId}, ${row.guardianId}, ${row.source}, ${row.deviceId}, ${row.staffUserId},
            least(coalesce(${row.occurredAt}::timestamptz, now()), now() + interval '5 minutes'),
            ${row.totalCents}, ${row.overLimit}, ${row.walletEntryId})`.execute(trx);
}

export async function insertSaleItems(
  trx: Trx,
  rows: { schoolId: string; saleId: string; menuItemId: string; qty: number; unitPriceCents: number }[],
): Promise<void> {
  if (rows.length > 0) await trx.insertInto("counterSaleItems").values(rows).execute();
}

export type LockedSale = { id: string; guardianId: string; totalCents: number; source: "kitchen" | "staff"; voidedAt: Date | null; occurredAt: Date };

/** Lançamentos travados FOR UPDATE em ordem de id (antes das carteiras). */
export async function lockSales(trx: Trx, ids: string[]): Promise<Map<string, LockedSale>> {
  if (ids.length === 0) return new Map();
  const { rows } = await sql<LockedSale>`
    SELECT id, guardian_id, total_cents, source, voided_at, occurred_at FROM counter_sales
    WHERE id = ANY(${ids}::uuid[]) ORDER BY id FOR UPDATE`.execute(trx);
  return new Map(rows.map((r) => [r.id, r]));
}

/** Mesmo dia no fuso da escola: o do lançamento e o do estorno (limitado ao relógio do banco). */
export async function sameSchoolDay(trx: Trx, saleAt: Date, voidAt: string): Promise<boolean> {
  const { rows } = await sql<{ same: boolean }>`
    SELECT (${saleAt}::timestamptz AT TIME ZONE s.timezone)::date
         = (least(${voidAt}::timestamptz, now()) AT TIME ZONE s.timezone)::date AS same
    FROM schools s WHERE s.id = app_school_id()`.execute(trx);
  return rows[0]?.same ?? false;
}

export async function markVoided(trx: Trx, saleId: string, entryId: string): Promise<boolean> {
  const res = await sql`
    UPDATE counter_sales SET voided_at = now(), void_entry_id = ${entryId}
    WHERE id = ${saleId} AND voided_at IS NULL`.execute(trx);
  return Number(res.numAffectedRows ?? 0) === 1;
}

/** Restrição alimentar só com consentimento de um responsável (spec 4.5), como no romaneio. */
export async function rosterStudents(trx: Trx) {
  const { rows } = await sql<{
    id: string;
    firstName: string;
    className: string;
    restrictions: string | null;
    guardianId: string | null;
    guardianFirstName: string | null;
    balanceCents: number;
  }>`
    SELECT s.id, s.first_name, c.name AS class_name,
      CASE WHEN EXISTS (
        SELECT 1 FROM guardian_students x WHERE x.student_id = s.id AND x.restriction_consent_at IS NOT NULL
      ) THEN s.dietary_restrictions END AS restrictions,
      g.id AS guardian_id, g.first_name AS guardian_first_name, coalesce(w.balance_cents, 0) AS balance_cents
    FROM students s
    JOIN classes c ON c.id = s.class_id
    LEFT JOIN guardian_students gs ON gs.student_id = s.id AND gs.financial
    LEFT JOIN guardians g ON g.id = gs.guardian_id
    LEFT JOIN wallets w ON w.guardian_id = g.id
    WHERE s.active
    ORDER BY c.name, s.first_name, s.id`.execute(trx);
  return rows;
}

export async function rosterItems(trx: Trx) {
  return trx.selectFrom("menuItems").select(["id", "name", "priceCents"]).where("active", "=", true).orderBy("name").execute();
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Serviço**

`apps/api/src/modules/counter/service.ts`:

```ts
import type {
  CounterRosterResponse,
  CounterSaleResult,
  CounterVoidResult,
  KdsCounterSalesRequest,
  KdsCounterVoidsRequest,
  KdsEventsResponse,
  StaffCounterSaleRequest,
} from "@app-lanche/contracts";
import { uuidv7 } from "uuidv7";
import { withTenant, type Trx } from "../../db/index";
import { audit } from "../../platform/audit";
import { DomainError } from "../../platform/errors";
import { idempotent, requestHash } from "../../platform/idempotency";
import type { SessionClaims } from "../../platform/session";
import * as wallet from "../wallet/service";
import * as repo from "./repo";

export type SaleActor = { source: "kitchen"; deviceId: string } | { source: "staff"; staffUserId: string };
/** record: o tablet offline sempre grava (spec F5B); refuse: secretaria sem confirmação; allow: secretaria confirmou. */
export type LimitPolicy = "record" | "refuse" | "allow";
export type SaleInput = { id: string; studentId: string; items: { menuItemId: string; qty: number }[]; occurredAt: string | null };
export type SaleOutcome =
  | { status: "applied"; totalCents: number; balanceCents: number; overLimit: boolean }
  | { status: "duplicate" }
  | { status: "rejected" };
export type VoidOutcome = { status: "voided" | "already"; balanceCents: number } | { status: "rejected" };

/**
 * Aplica lançamentos de balcão na carteira do responsável financeiro. Id já gravado = `duplicate` (aceito sem efeito);
 * aluno ou item inexistente/inativo, item repetido ou aluno sem financeiro = `rejected`. As carteiras do lote são
 * travadas juntas em ordem de guardian_id. Lançamento não passa por corte nem romaneio (é venda já entregue).
 */
export async function applySales(trx: Trx, schoolId: string, actor: SaleActor, sales: SaleInput[], policy: LimitPolicy): Promise<Map<string, SaleOutcome>> {
  const out = new Map<string, SaleOutcome>();
  const existing = await repo.existingSaleIds(trx, sales.map((s) => s.id));
  const seen = new Set<string>();
  const fresh: SaleInput[] = [];
  for (const s of sales) {
    if (existing.has(s.id) || seen.has(s.id)) out.set(s.id, { status: "duplicate" });
    else fresh.push(s);
    seen.add(s.id);
  }
  const students = await repo.activeStudentIds(trx, [...new Set(fresh.map((s) => s.studentId))]);
  const prices = await repo.activeItemPrices(trx, [...new Set(fresh.flatMap((s) => s.items.map((i) => i.menuItemId)))]);
  const valid = fresh.filter((s) => {
    const itemIds = s.items.map((i) => i.menuItemId);
    return students.has(s.studentId) && itemIds.length > 0 && new Set(itemIds).size === itemIds.length && itemIds.every((id) => prices.has(id));
  });
  const financial = await repo.lockFinancialGuardians(trx, [...new Set(valid.map((s) => s.studentId))]);
  await wallet.lockBalances(trx, schoolId, [...financial.values()]);
  const limit = await repo.debtLimit(trx);

  for (const s of fresh) {
    const guardianId = financial.get(s.studentId);
    if (!valid.includes(s) || !guardianId) {
      out.set(s.id, { status: "rejected" });
      continue;
    }
    const totalCents = s.items.reduce((sum, i) => sum + prices.get(i.menuItemId)! * i.qty, 0);
    const entryId = uuidv7();
    const posted = await wallet.postEntry(trx, {
      id: entryId,
      schoolId,
      guardianId,
      kind: "counter_charge",
      amountCents: -totalCents,
      sourceType: "counter_sale",
      sourceId: s.id,
      minBalanceCents: policy === "refuse" ? -limit : null,
    });
    if (!posted.posted) {
      out.set(s.id, { status: "duplicate" }); // outro envio do mesmo id ganhou a corrida
      continue;
    }
    const overLimit = posted.balanceCents < -limit;
    await repo.insertSale(trx, {
      id: s.id,
      schoolId,
      studentId: s.studentId,
      guardianId,
      source: actor.source,
      deviceId: actor.source === "kitchen" ? actor.deviceId : null,
      staffUserId: actor.source === "staff" ? actor.staffUserId : null,
      occurredAt: s.occurredAt,
      totalCents,
      overLimit,
      walletEntryId: entryId,
    });
    await repo.insertSaleItems(
      trx,
      s.items.map((i) => ({ schoolId, saleId: s.id, menuItemId: i.menuItemId, qty: i.qty, unitPriceCents: prices.get(i.menuItemId)! })),
    );
    await wallet.noticeDebtStart(trx, schoolId, guardianId, posted.previousBalanceCents, posted.balanceCents);
    out.set(s.id, { status: "applied", totalCents, balanceCents: posted.balanceCents, overLimit });
  }
  return out;
}

/**
 * Estorna lançamentos: `counter_void` positivo na carteira gravada no lançamento. O tablet só estorna lançamentos do
 * tablet (`source = 'kitchen'`) do mesmo dia no fuso da escola. Já estornado = `already` (aceito sem efeito).
 */
export async function voidSales(
  trx: Trx,
  schoolId: string,
  actor: SaleActor,
  voids: { saleId: string; occurredAt?: string }[],
): Promise<Map<string, VoidOutcome>> {
  const out = new Map<string, VoidOutcome>();
  const sales = await repo.lockSales(trx, [...new Set(voids.map((v) => v.saleId))]);
  await wallet.lockBalances(trx, schoolId, [...sales.values()].map((s) => s.guardianId));
  for (const v of voids) {
    if (out.has(v.saleId)) continue;
    const sale = sales.get(v.saleId);
    if (!sale) {
      out.set(v.saleId, { status: "rejected" });
      continue;
    }
    if (sale.voidedAt) {
      out.set(v.saleId, { status: "already", balanceCents: await wallet.balanceOf(trx, sale.guardianId) });
      continue;
    }
    if (actor.source === "kitchen" && (sale.source !== "kitchen" || !v.occurredAt || !(await repo.sameSchoolDay(trx, sale.occurredAt, v.occurredAt)))) {
      out.set(v.saleId, { status: "rejected" });
      continue;
    }
    const entryId = uuidv7();
    const posted = await wallet.postEntry(trx, {
      id: entryId,
      schoolId,
      guardianId: sale.guardianId,
      kind: "counter_void",
      amountCents: sale.totalCents,
      sourceType: "counter_sale",
      sourceId: v.saleId,
    });
    if (posted.posted) await repo.markVoided(trx, v.saleId, entryId);
    await audit(trx, {
      schoolId,
      actorKind: actor.source === "kitchen" ? "kitchen" : "staff",
      actorId: actor.source === "kitchen" ? actor.deviceId : actor.staffUserId,
      action: "counter.void",
      detail: { sale_id: v.saleId },
    });
    out.set(v.saleId, { status: "voided", balanceCents: posted.balanceCents });
  }
  return out;
}

function split(ids: string[], rejected: (id: string) => boolean): KdsEventsResponse {
  return { accepted: ids.filter((id) => !rejected(id)), rejected: ids.filter(rejected) };
}

export async function recordKitchenSales(schoolId: string, deviceId: string, body: KdsCounterSalesRequest): Promise<KdsEventsResponse> {
  const outcomes = await withTenant(schoolId, (trx) =>
    applySales(trx, schoolId, { source: "kitchen", deviceId }, body.sales.map((s) => ({ ...s, occurredAt: s.occurredAt })), "record"),
  );
  return split(body.sales.map((s) => s.id), (id) => outcomes.get(id)?.status === "rejected");
}

export async function recordKitchenVoids(schoolId: string, deviceId: string, body: KdsCounterVoidsRequest): Promise<KdsEventsResponse> {
  const outcomes = await withTenant(schoolId, (trx) => voidSales(trx, schoolId, { source: "kitchen", deviceId }, body.voids));
  return split(body.voids.map((v) => v.saleId), (id) => outcomes.get(id)?.status === "rejected");
}

export async function createStaffSale(session: SessionClaims, key: string, body: StaffCounterSaleRequest): Promise<CounterSaleResult> {
  const schoolId = session.schoolId;
  const hash = requestHash({ route: "POST /api/admin/v1/counter-sales", actorId: session.sub, body });
  return withTenant(schoolId, async (trx) => {
    const { response } = await idempotent(trx, { schoolId, key, requestHash: hash }, async () => {
      const saleId = uuidv7();
      const outcomes = await applySales(
        trx,
        schoolId,
        { source: "staff", staffUserId: session.sub },
        [{ id: saleId, studentId: body.studentId, items: body.items, occurredAt: null }],
        body.allowOverLimit ? "allow" : "refuse",
      );
      const outcome = outcomes.get(saleId)!;
      if (outcome.status !== "applied") throw new DomainError("invalid_order");
      await audit(trx, { schoolId, actorKind: "staff", actorId: session.sub, action: "counter.sale", detail: { sale_id: saleId, over_limit: outcome.overLimit } });
      return { saleId, totalCents: outcome.totalCents, balanceCents: outcome.balanceCents, overLimit: outcome.overLimit };
    });
    return response;
  });
}

export async function voidStaffSale(session: SessionClaims, saleId: string): Promise<CounterVoidResult> {
  const outcome = await withTenant(session.schoolId, async (trx) =>
    (await voidSales(trx, session.schoolId, { source: "staff", staffUserId: session.sub }, [{ saleId }])).get(saleId)!,
  );
  if (outcome.status === "rejected") throw new DomainError("not_found");
  return { saleId, balanceCents: outcome.balanceCents };
}

export async function roster(schoolId: string): Promise<CounterRosterResponse> {
  return withTenant(schoolId, async (trx) => ({
    debtLimitCents: await repo.debtLimit(trx),
    items: await repo.rosterItems(trx),
    students: (await repo.rosterStudents(trx)).map((r) => ({
      id: r.id,
      firstName: r.firstName,
      className: r.className,
      restrictions: r.restrictions,
      guardian: r.guardianId ? { id: r.guardianId, firstName: r.guardianFirstName!, balanceCents: r.balanceCents } : null,
    })),
  }));
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Rotas**

`apps/api/src/modules/counter/routes.ts`:

```ts
import { StaffCounterSaleRequest, Uuid } from "@app-lanche/contracts";
import type { FastifyInstance } from "fastify";
import { z } from "zod";
import type { RouteAuth } from "../../app";
import { idempotencyKeyFrom } from "../../platform/idempotency";
import * as service from "./service";

const OFFICE: RouteAuth = { kind: "staff", roles: ["admin", "secretaria"] };
const IdParams = z.object({ id: Uuid });

export async function counterRoutes(app: FastifyInstance): Promise<void> {
  app.get("/api/admin/v1/counter/roster", { config: { auth: OFFICE } }, async (req) => service.roster(req.session!.schoolId));

  app.post("/api/admin/v1/counter-sales", { config: { auth: OFFICE } }, async (req) =>
    service.createStaffSale(req.session!, idempotencyKeyFrom(req), StaffCounterSaleRequest.parse(req.body)),
  );

  // Naturalmente idempotente: estornar de novo não lança outra vez (unicidade do ledger e voided_at).
  app.post("/api/admin/v1/counter-sales/:id/void", { config: { auth: OFFICE } }, async (req) =>
    service.voidStaffSale(req.session!, IdParams.parse(req.params).id),
  );
}
```

- [ ] **Step 1: Testes (falham)**

`apps/api/src/modules/kds/counterSync.test.ts`:

```ts
import { sql } from "kysely";
import { uuidv7 } from "uuidv7";
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { adminDb, createSchool, kitchenCookie, seedFamily, staffCookie, walletLedgerMatches } from "../../../test/helpers";
import { buildApp } from "../../app";
import { SESSION_COOKIE } from "../../platform/session";

async function setup(limitCents?: number) {
  const schoolId = await createSchool();
  if (limitCents !== undefined) await adminDb().updateTable("schools").set({ debtLimitCents: limitCents }).where("id", "=", schoolId).execute();
  const fam = await seedFamily(schoolId, ["Léo", "Bia"]);
  const misto = uuidv7();
  const suco = uuidv7();
  await adminDb()
    .insertInto("menuItems")
    .values([
      { id: misto, schoolId, name: `Misto ${misto.slice(-6)}`, priceCents: 900 },
      { id: suco, schoolId, name: `Suco ${suco.slice(-6)}`, priceCents: 450 },
    ])
    .execute();
  const tablet = await kitchenCookie(schoolId);
  return { schoolId, guardianId: fam.guardianId, leo: fam.studentIds[0]!, bia: fam.studentIds[1]!, misto, suco, cookies: tablet.cookies };
}

type Setup = Awaited<ReturnType<typeof setup>>;
// Instantes do tablet sempre no passado recente (o servidor limita a 5 min à frente).
const at = (secondsAgo = 1) => new Date(Date.now() - secondsAgo * 1000).toISOString();
const sale = (studentId: string, menuItemId: string, qty = 1, id: string = uuidv7()) => ({ id, studentId, items: [{ menuItemId, qty }], occurredAt: at() });
const push = (s: Setup, sales: object[], cookies = s.cookies) =>
  buildApp().inject({ method: "POST", url: "/api/kds/v1/counter-sales", cookies, payload: { sales } });
const pushVoids = (s: Setup, voids: object[]) => buildApp().inject({ method: "POST", url: "/api/kds/v1/counter-voids", cookies: s.cookies, payload: { voids } });

async function balance(guardianId: string) {
  return (await adminDb().selectFrom("wallets").select("balanceCents").where("guardianId", "=", guardianId).executeTakeFirst())?.balanceCents ?? 0;
}

describe("kds roster", () => {
  it("lists active students with consented restrictions, the financial guardian's balance, the limit and active items", async () => {
    const s = await setup(3000);
    await adminDb().updateTable("students").set({ dietaryRestrictions: "sem lactose" }).where("id", "in", [s.leo, s.bia]).execute();
    await adminDb().updateTable("guardianStudents").set({ restrictionConsentAt: new Date() }).where("studentId", "=", s.leo).execute();
    await adminDb().updateTable("guardianStudents").set({ financial: false }).where("studentId", "=", s.bia).execute();
    await adminDb().updateTable("menuItems").set({ active: false }).where("id", "=", s.suco).execute();
    const res = await buildApp().inject({ method: "GET", url: "/api/kds/v1/roster", cookies: s.cookies });
    expect(res.statusCode).toBe(200);
    const body = res.json();
    expect(body.debtLimitCents).toBe(3000);
    expect(body.items).toEqual([{ id: s.misto, name: expect.any(String), priceCents: 900 }]);
    const byId = Object.fromEntries(body.students.map((st: { id: string }) => [st.id, st]));
    expect(byId[s.leo]).toMatchObject({ firstName: "Léo", restrictions: "sem lactose", guardian: { id: s.guardianId, firstName: "Ana", balanceCents: 0 } });
    expect(byId[s.bia]).toMatchObject({ firstName: "Bia", restrictions: null, guardian: null });
    expect(res.cookies.find((c) => c.name === SESSION_COOKIE)?.maxAge).toBe((30 + 90) * 86400);
  });
});

describe("kds counter sync", () => {
  it("charges once per sale id, also on replay", async () => {
    const s = await setup();
    const batch = [sale(s.leo, s.misto), sale(s.bia, s.suco, 2)];
    const first = await push(s, batch);
    expect(first.json()).toEqual({ accepted: batch.map((b) => b.id), rejected: [] });
    expect((await push(s, batch)).json()).toEqual({ accepted: batch.map((b) => b.id), rejected: [] });
    expect(await balance(s.guardianId)).toBe(-1800);
    const charges = await adminDb().selectFrom("walletEntries").select("id").where("schoolId", "=", s.schoolId).where("kind", "=", "counter_charge").execute();
    expect(charges).toHaveLength(2);
    expect(await walletLedgerMatches(s.schoolId, s.guardianId)).toBe(true);
  });

  it("rejects unknown or inactive students and items, and students without a financial guardian", async () => {
    const s = await setup();
    await adminDb().updateTable("menuItems").set({ active: false }).where("id", "=", s.suco).execute();
    await adminDb().updateTable("guardianStudents").set({ financial: false }).where("studentId", "=", s.bia).execute();
    const bad = [sale(uuidv7(), s.misto), sale(s.leo, s.suco), sale(s.leo, uuidv7()), sale(s.bia, s.misto)];
    const ok = sale(s.leo, s.misto);
    const res = await push(s, [...bad, ok]);
    expect(res.json()).toEqual({ accepted: [ok.id], rejected: bad.map((b) => b.id) });
    expect(await balance(s.guardianId)).toBe(-900);
  });

  it("always records an offline sale, flagging it over the limit", async () => {
    const s = await setup(1000);
    const big = sale(s.leo, s.misto, 2);
    expect((await push(s, [big])).json().accepted).toEqual([big.id]);
    const row = await adminDb().selectFrom("counterSales").select(["overLimit", "source"]).where("id", "=", big.id).executeTakeFirstOrThrow();
    expect(row).toEqual({ overLimit: true, source: "kitchen" });
    expect(await balance(s.guardianId)).toBe(-1800);
  });

  it("limits occurred_at to 5 minutes ahead of the database clock", async () => {
    const s = await setup();
    const future = { ...sale(s.leo, s.misto), occurredAt: new Date(Date.now() + 3_600_000).toISOString() };
    await push(s, [future]);
    const { rows } = await sql<{ ok: boolean }>`SELECT occurred_at <= now() + interval '5 minutes' AS ok FROM counter_sales WHERE id = ${future.id}`.execute(adminDb());
    expect(rows[0]!.ok).toBe(true);
  });

  it("voids the tablet's own sales of the same day once, and nothing else", async () => {
    const s = await setup();
    const mine = sale(s.leo, s.misto);
    const old = sale(s.bia, s.misto);
    await push(s, [mine, old]);
    await adminDb().updateTable("counterSales").set({ occurredAt: sql<Date>`now() - interval '2 days'` }).where("id", "=", old.id).execute();
    const office = await staffCookie(s.schoolId, "secretaria");
    const staffSale = await buildApp().inject({
      method: "POST",
      url: "/api/admin/v1/counter-sales",
      cookies: office.cookies,
      headers: { "idempotency-key": uuidv7() },
      payload: { studentId: s.leo, items: [{ menuItemId: s.suco, qty: 1 }] },
    });
    const staffSaleId = staffSale.json().saleId as string;
    const unknown = uuidv7();
    const voids = [mine.id, old.id, staffSaleId, unknown].map((saleId) => ({ saleId, occurredAt: at() }));
    expect((await pushVoids(s, voids)).json()).toEqual({ accepted: [mine.id], rejected: [old.id, staffSaleId, unknown] });
    expect((await pushVoids(s, [{ saleId: mine.id, occurredAt: at() }])).json()).toEqual({ accepted: [mine.id], rejected: [] });
    const voidEntries = await adminDb().selectFrom("walletEntries").select("amountCents").where("schoolId", "=", s.schoolId).where("kind", "=", "counter_void").execute();
    expect(voidEntries).toEqual([{ amountCents: 900 }]);
    expect(await walletLedgerMatches(s.schoolId, s.guardianId)).toBe(true);
  });

  it("applies batches from two tablets on the same wallets concurrently", async () => {
    const s = await setup();
    const other = await seedFamily(s.schoolId, ["Caio"]);
    const second = await kitchenCookie(s.schoolId);
    const caio = other.studentIds[0]!;
    const a = [sale(s.leo, s.misto), sale(caio, s.misto)];
    const b = [sale(caio, s.suco), sale(s.bia, s.suco)];
    const [ra, rb] = await Promise.all([push(s, a), push(s, b, second.cookies)]);
    expect(ra.json().rejected).toEqual([]);
    expect(rb.json().rejected).toEqual([]);
    expect(await balance(s.guardianId)).toBe(-1350);
    expect(await balance(other.guardianId)).toBe(-1350);
    expect(await walletLedgerMatches(s.schoolId, s.guardianId)).toBe(true);
    expect(await walletLedgerMatches(s.schoolId, other.guardianId)).toBe(true);
  });
});
```

```ts
import { uuidv7 } from "uuidv7";
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { adminDb, createSchool, kitchenCookie, seedFamily, staffCookie } from "../../../test/helpers";
import { buildApp } from "../../app";
import { withTenant } from "../../db/index";
import { applySales } from "../counter/service";

async function setup() {
  const schoolId = await createSchool();
  await adminDb().updateTable("schools").set({ debtLimitCents: 1000 }).where("id", "=", schoolId).execute();
  const ana = await seedFamily(schoolId, ["Léo", "Bia"]);
  const bruno = await seedFamily(schoolId, ["Caio"]);
  await adminDb().updateTable("guardians").set({ firstName: "Bruno" }).where("id", "=", bruno.guardianId).execute();
  const carla = await seedFamily(schoolId, ["Duda"]);
  await adminDb().updateTable("guardians").set({ firstName: "Carla" }).where("id", "=", carla.guardianId).execute();
  const misto = uuidv7();
  await adminDb().insertInto("menuItems").values({ id: misto, schoolId, name: `Misto ${misto.slice(-6)}`, priceCents: 900 }).execute();
  const { deviceId } = await kitchenCookie(schoolId);
  const sell = (studentId: string, qty = 1) =>
    withTenant(schoolId, async (trx) => {
      const id = uuidv7();
      await applySales(trx, schoolId, { source: "kitchen", deviceId }, [{ id, studentId, items: [{ menuItemId: misto, qty }], occurredAt: null }], "record");
      return id;
    });
  return { schoolId, ana, bruno, carla, sell, deviceId };
}

const get = (cookies: Record<string, string>, url: string) => buildApp().inject({ method: "GET", url, cookies });

describe("accounts", () => {
  it("lists every guardian with debts first, the students they pay for and recent over-limit sales", async () => {
    const s = await setup();
    await s.sell(s.ana.studentIds[0]!);
    const biaSale = await s.sell(s.ana.studentIds[1]!, 2); // -2700 < -1000: over_limit
    await s.sell(s.bruno.studentIds[0]!);
    const { cookies } = await staffCookie(s.schoolId, "cozinha");
    const res = await get(cookies, "/api/admin/v1/accounts");
    expect(res.statusCode).toBe(200);
    expect(res.json().debtLimitCents).toBe(1000);
    expect(res.json().accounts).toEqual([
      { guardianId: s.ana.guardianId, firstName: "Ana", students: ["Bia", "Léo"], balanceCents: -2700 },
      { guardianId: s.bruno.guardianId, firstName: "Bruno", students: ["Caio"], balanceCents: -900 },
      { guardianId: s.carla.guardianId, firstName: "Carla", students: ["Duda"], balanceCents: 0 },
    ]);
    expect(res.json().overLimitSales).toEqual([
      expect.objectContaining({ saleId: biaSale, guardianId: s.ana.guardianId, guardianFirstName: "Ana", studentFirstName: "Bia", totalCents: 1800 }),
    ]);
  });

  it("shows the statement with student names, over-limit flags and voids", async () => {
    const s = await setup();
    const leoSale = await s.sell(s.ana.studentIds[0]!);
    await s.sell(s.ana.studentIds[1]!);
    const office = await staffCookie(s.schoolId, "secretaria");
    await buildApp().inject({ method: "POST", url: `/api/admin/v1/counter-sales/${leoSale}/void`, cookies: office.cookies });
    const res = await get(office.cookies, `/api/admin/v1/guardians/${s.ana.guardianId}/statement`);
    expect(res.json()).toMatchObject({ guardian: { id: s.ana.guardianId, firstName: "Ana" }, balanceCents: -900, debtLimitCents: 1000 });
    expect(res.json().entries.map((e: { kind: string; studentFirstName: string | null; overLimit: boolean; voided: boolean; amountCents: number }) =>
      [e.kind, e.studentFirstName, e.amountCents, e.overLimit, e.voided])).toEqual([
      ["counter_void", "Léo", 900, false, true],
      ["counter_charge", "Bia", -900, true, false],
      ["counter_charge", "Léo", -900, false, true],
    ]);
    expect((await get(office.cookies, `/api/admin/v1/guardians/${uuidv7()}/statement`)).statusCode).toBe(404);
  });

  it("lets everyone on staff read the debt limit and only admins change it", async () => {
    const s = await setup();
    const admin = await staffCookie(s.schoolId, "admin");
    const office = await staffCookie(s.schoolId, "secretaria");
    const put = (cookies: Record<string, string>, payload: object) =>
      buildApp().inject({ method: "PUT", url: "/api/admin/v1/school/settings", cookies, payload });
    expect((await get(office.cookies, "/api/admin/v1/school/settings")).json()).toEqual({ debtLimitCents: 1000 });
    expect((await put(office.cookies, { debtLimitCents: 8000 })).statusCode).toBe(403);
    expect((await put(admin.cookies, { debtLimitCents: -1 })).statusCode).toBe(400);
    expect((await put(admin.cookies, { debtLimitCents: 8000 })).json()).toEqual({ debtLimitCents: 8000 });
    expect((await get(office.cookies, "/api/admin/v1/school/settings")).json()).toEqual({ debtLimitCents: 8000 });
    const audit = await adminDb().selectFrom("auditLog").select("action").where("schoolId", "=", s.schoolId).where("action", "=", "school.debt_limit_changed").execute();
    expect(audit).toHaveLength(1);
  });
});
```

`apps/api/src/modules/wallet/repo.ts` (o `listEntries` antigo sai na Task 8, quando `getWallet` deixa de usá-lo):

```ts
export async function debtLimit(trx: Trx): Promise<number> {
  return (await trx.selectFrom("schools").select("debtLimitCents").executeTakeFirstOrThrow()).debtLimitCents;
}

export async function setDebtLimit(trx: Trx, debtLimitCents: number): Promise<void> {
  await trx.updateTable("schools").set({ debtLimitCents }).execute();
}

export async function guardianName(trx: Trx, guardianId: string) {
  return trx.selectFrom("guardians").select(["id", "firstName"]).where("id", "=", guardianId).executeTakeFirst();
}

/** Todos os responsáveis, débitos primeiro; `students` = alunos ativos de quem ele é o financeiro. */
export async function listAccounts(trx: Trx) {
  const { rows } = await sql<{ guardianId: string; firstName: string; balanceCents: number; students: string[] }>`
    SELECT g.id AS guardian_id, g.first_name, coalesce(w.balance_cents, 0) AS balance_cents,
      coalesce(array_agg(s.first_name ORDER BY s.first_name) FILTER (WHERE s.id IS NOT NULL), '{}') AS students
    FROM guardians g
    LEFT JOIN wallets w ON w.guardian_id = g.id
    LEFT JOIN guardian_students gs ON gs.guardian_id = g.id AND gs.financial
    LEFT JOIN students s ON s.id = gs.student_id AND s.active
    GROUP BY g.id, g.first_name, w.balance_cents
    ORDER BY coalesce(w.balance_cents, 0), g.first_name, g.id`.execute(trx);
  return rows;
}

/** Lançamentos acima do limite, não estornados, recebidos nos últimos 7 dias (relógio do banco). */
export async function recentOverLimitSales(trx: Trx) {
  const { rows } = await sql<{
    saleId: string;
    guardianId: string;
    guardianFirstName: string;
    studentFirstName: string;
    occurredAt: Date;
    totalCents: number;
  }>`
    SELECT cs.id AS sale_id, cs.guardian_id, g.first_name AS guardian_first_name, s.first_name AS student_first_name,
      cs.occurred_at, cs.total_cents
    FROM counter_sales cs
    JOIN guardians g ON g.id = cs.guardian_id
    JOIN students s ON s.id = cs.student_id
    WHERE cs.over_limit AND cs.voided_at IS NULL AND cs.received_at > now() - interval '7 days'
    ORDER BY cs.occurred_at DESC, cs.id DESC
    LIMIT 50`.execute(trx);
  return rows;
}

/** Extrato: lançamentos do balcão trazem o aluno, o selo de ultrapassagem e se foram estornados. */
export async function statementEntries(trx: Trx, guardianId: string, limit: number) {
  const { rows } = await sql<{
    id: string;
    kind: string;
    amountCents: number;
    createdAt: Date;
    saleId: string | null;
    studentFirstName: string | null;
    overLimit: boolean;
    voided: boolean;
  }>`
    SELECT e.id, e.kind, e.amount_cents, e.created_at, cs.id AS sale_id, st.first_name AS student_first_name,
      coalesce(e.kind = 'counter_charge' AND cs.over_limit, false) AS over_limit,
      coalesce(cs.voided_at IS NOT NULL, false) AS voided
    FROM wallet_entries e
    JOIN wallets w ON w.id = e.wallet_id
    LEFT JOIN counter_sales cs ON e.source_type = 'counter_sale' AND cs.id = e.source_id
    LEFT JOIN students st ON st.id = cs.student_id
    WHERE w.guardian_id = ${guardianId}
    ORDER BY e.created_at DESC, e.id DESC
    LIMIT ${limit}`.execute(trx);
  return rows;
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Serviço e rotas**

`apps/api/src/modules/wallet/service.ts`:

```ts
export function toStatement(rows: Awaited<ReturnType<typeof repo.statementEntries>>): StatementEntry[] {
  return rows.map((r) => ({
    id: r.id,
    kind: r.kind as WalletEntryKind,
    amountCents: r.amountCents,
    createdAt: r.createdAt.toISOString(),
    saleId: r.saleId,
    studentFirstName: r.studentFirstName,
    overLimit: r.overLimit,
    voided: r.voided,
  }));
}

export async function listAccounts(session: SessionClaims): Promise<AccountListResponse> {
  return withTenant(session.schoolId, async (trx) => ({
    debtLimitCents: await repo.debtLimit(trx),
    accounts: await repo.listAccounts(trx),
    overLimitSales: (await repo.recentOverLimitSales(trx)).map((r) => ({ ...r, occurredAt: r.occurredAt.toISOString() })),
  }));
}

export async function accountStatement(session: SessionClaims, guardianId: string): Promise<AccountStatementResponse> {
  return withTenant(session.schoolId, async (trx) => {
    const guardian = await repo.guardianName(trx, guardianId);
    if (!guardian) throw new DomainError("not_found");
    return {
      guardian,
      balanceCents: await repo.readBalance(trx, guardianId),
      debtLimitCents: await repo.debtLimit(trx),
      entries: toStatement(await repo.statementEntries(trx, guardianId, 100)),
    };
  });
}

export async function getSettings(session: SessionClaims): Promise<SchoolSettings> {
  return withTenant(session.schoolId, async (trx) => ({ debtLimitCents: await repo.debtLimit(trx) }));
}

export async function updateSettings(session: SessionClaims, body: SchoolSettings): Promise<SchoolSettings> {
  return withTenant(session.schoolId, async (trx) => {
    if ((await repo.debtLimit(trx)) !== body.debtLimitCents) {
      await repo.setDebtLimit(trx, body.debtLimitCents);
      await audit(trx, { schoolId: session.schoolId, actorKind: "staff", actorId: session.sub, action: "school.debt_limit_changed", detail: { debt_limit_cents: body.debtLimitCents } });
    }
    return { debtLimitCents: body.debtLimitCents };
  });
}
```

(importe os tipos `AccountListResponse`, `AccountStatementResponse`, `SchoolSettings`, `StatementEntry` de `@app-lanche/contracts`).

`apps/api/src/modules/wallet/routes.ts`:

```ts
import { SchoolSettings, Uuid, WalletAdjustmentRequest } from "@app-lanche/contracts";
import type { FastifyInstance } from "fastify";
import { z } from "zod";
import type { RouteAuth } from "../../app";
import { idempotencyKeyFrom } from "../../platform/idempotency";
import * as service from "./service";

const GuardianParams = z.object({ guardianId: Uuid });
const ACCOUNTS: RouteAuth = { kind: "staff", roles: ["admin", "secretaria", "cozinha"] };
const ADMIN: RouteAuth = { kind: "staff", roles: ["admin"] };

export async function walletRoutes(app: FastifyInstance): Promise<void> {
  app.get("/api/v1/wallet", { config: { auth: { kind: "guardian", scope: "order" } } }, async (req) => service.getWallet(req.session!));

  app.post("/api/admin/v1/guardians/:guardianId/wallet/adjustments", { config: { auth: ADMIN } }, async (req) =>
    service.adjustBalance(req.session!, idempotencyKeyFrom(req), GuardianParams.parse(req.params).guardianId, WalletAdjustmentRequest.parse(req.body)),
  );

  app.get("/api/admin/v1/accounts", { config: { auth: ACCOUNTS } }, async (req) => service.listAccounts(req.session!));

  app.get("/api/admin/v1/guardians/:guardianId/statement", { config: { auth: ACCOUNTS } }, async (req) =>
    service.accountStatement(req.session!, GuardianParams.parse(req.params).guardianId),
  );

  app.get("/api/admin/v1/school/settings", { config: { auth: ACCOUNTS } }, async (req) => service.getSettings(req.session!));

  // PUT naturalmente idempotente (sem Idempotency-Key).
  app.put("/api/admin/v1/school/settings", { config: { auth: ADMIN } }, async (req) =>
    service.updateSettings(req.session!, SchoolSettings.parse(req.body)),
  );
}
```

```ts
import { sql } from "kysely";
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { adminDb, createSchool, guardianCookie, internalRequest, seedFamily } from "../../../test/helpers";
import { buildApp } from "../../app";

describe("wallet magic-link scope", () => {
  it("issues a 7-day wallet link and exchanges it for a wallet session", async () => {
    const schoolId = await createSchool();
    const fam = await seedFamily(schoolId, ["Léo"]);
    const app = buildApp();
    const res = await app.inject(internalRequest("POST", `/internal/v1/schools/${schoolId}/magic-links`, { guardian_id: fam.guardianId, scope: "wallet" }));
    expect(res.statusCode).toBe(200);
    const { rows } = await sql<{ days: number; scope: string; serviceDate: string | null }>`
      SELECT round(extract(epoch FROM expires_at - created_at) / 86400)::int AS days, scope, service_date
      FROM magic_links WHERE guardian_id = ${fam.guardianId}`.execute(adminDb());
    expect(rows).toEqual([{ days: 7, scope: "wallet", serviceDate: null }]);
    const token = (res.json().link_url as string).split("#")[1]!;
    const exchange = await app.inject({ method: "POST", url: "/api/v1/auth/exchange", payload: { token, deviceSecret: "d".repeat(43) } });
    expect(exchange.json()).toEqual({ status: "ok", scope: "wallet" });
    const withDate = await app.inject(
      internalRequest("POST", `/internal/v1/schools/${schoolId}/magic-links`, { guardian_id: fam.guardianId, scope: "wallet", service_date: "2031-01-02" }),
    );
    expect(withDate.statusCode).toBe(400);
  });

  it("lets a wallet session use only the wallet and /me", async () => {
    const schoolId = await createSchool();
    const fam = await seedFamily(schoolId, ["Léo"]);
    const app = buildApp();
    const wallet = await guardianCookie(schoolId, fam.guardianId, "wallet");
    const get = (url: string, cookies = wallet) => app.inject({ method: "GET", url, cookies });
    expect((await get("/api/v1/wallet")).statusCode).toBe(200);
    expect((await get("/api/v1/me")).statusCode).toBe(200);
    expect((await get("/api/v1/service-days")).statusCode).toBe(403);
    expect((await get("/api/v1/documents")).statusCode).toBe(403);
    expect((await get("/api/v1/wallet", await guardianCookie(schoolId, fam.guardianId, "documents"))).statusCode).toBe(403);
  });
});
```

```ts
import { uuidv7 } from "uuidv7";
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { adminDb, createSchool, guardianCookie, kitchenCookie, seedFamily } from "../../../test/helpers";
import { buildApp } from "../../app";
import { withTenant } from "../../db/index";
import { applySales, voidSales } from "../counter/service";

describe("guardian wallet", () => {
  it("shows the debt, the counter entries and, to another guardian, only the sales as information", async () => {
    const schoolId = await createSchool();
    await adminDb().updateTable("schools").set({ debtLimitCents: 1000 }).where("id", "=", schoolId).execute();
    const ana = await seedFamily(schoolId, ["Léo", "Bia"]);
    const [leo, bia] = ana.studentIds as [string, string];
    const rui = uuidv7();
    await adminDb().insertInto("guardians").values({ id: rui, schoolId, phoneE164: "+5511955550001", firstName: "Rui" }).execute();
    await adminDb().insertInto("guardianStudents").values({ schoolId, guardianId: rui, studentId: leo, financial: false }).execute();
    const misto = uuidv7();
    await adminDb().insertInto("menuItems").values({ id: misto, schoolId, name: `Misto ${misto.slice(-6)}`, priceCents: 900 }).execute();
    const { deviceId } = await kitchenCookie(schoolId);
    const kitchen = { source: "kitchen" as const, deviceId };
    const leoSale = uuidv7();
    await withTenant(schoolId, (trx) => applySales(trx, schoolId, kitchen, [{ id: leoSale, studentId: leo, items: [{ menuItemId: misto, qty: 1 }], occurredAt: null }], "record"));
    await withTenant(schoolId, (trx) => applySales(trx, schoolId, kitchen, [{ id: uuidv7(), studentId: bia, items: [{ menuItemId: misto, qty: 2 }], occurredAt: null }], "record"));
    await withTenant(schoolId, (trx) => voidSales(trx, schoolId, kitchen, [{ saleId: leoSale, occurredAt: new Date().toISOString() }]));

    const mine = await buildApp().inject({ method: "GET", url: "/api/v1/wallet", cookies: await guardianCookie(schoolId, ana.guardianId, "wallet") });
    expect(mine.json()).toMatchObject({ balanceCents: -1800, debtLimitCents: 1000, financialFor: ["Bia", "Léo"], otherSales: [] });
    expect(mine.json().entries.map((e: { kind: string; studentFirstName: string; overLimit: boolean; voided: boolean }) => [e.kind, e.studentFirstName, e.overLimit, e.voided])).toEqual([
      ["counter_void", "Léo", false, true],
      ["counter_charge", "Bia", true, false],
      ["counter_charge", "Léo", false, true],
    ]);

    const other = await buildApp().inject({ method: "GET", url: "/api/v1/wallet", cookies: await guardianCookie(schoolId, rui) });

    expect(other.json().otherSales).toEqual([{ id: leoSale, studentFirstName: "Léo", occurredAt: expect.any(String), totalCents: 900, voided: true }]);

});
```

```ts
export async function financialStudentNames(trx: Trx, guardianId: string): Promise<string[]> {
  const rows = await trx
    .selectFrom("guardianStudents")
    .innerJoin("students", "students.id", "guardianStudents.studentId")
    .select("students.firstName")
    .where("guardianStudents.guardianId", "=", guardianId)
    .where("guardianStudents.financial", "=", true)
    .where("students.active", "=", true)
    .orderBy("students.firstName")
    .execute();
  return rows.map((r) => r.firstName);
}

/** Lanches dos alunos do responsável lançados na carteira de outro (30 dias). */
export async function salesChargedToOthers(trx: Trx, guardianId: string) {
  const { rows } = await sql<{ id: string; studentFirstName: string; occurredAt: Date; totalCents: number; voided: boolean }>`
    SELECT cs.id, st.first_name AS student_first_name, cs.occurred_at, cs.total_cents, (cs.voided_at IS NOT NULL) AS voided
    FROM counter_sales cs
    JOIN guardian_students gs ON gs.student_id = cs.student_id AND gs.guardian_id = ${guardianId}
    JOIN students st ON st.id = cs.student_id
    WHERE cs.guardian_id <> ${guardianId} AND cs.occurred_at > now() - interval '30 days'
    ORDER BY cs.occurred_at DESC, cs.id DESC
    LIMIT 50`.execute(trx);
  return rows;
}
```

```ts
import { sql } from "kysely";
import { uuidv7 } from "uuidv7";
import { afterEach, describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { adminDb, createSchool, guardianCookie, seedFamily, staffCookie, walletLedgerMatches } from "../../../test/helpers";
import { buildApp } from "../../app";
import { withTenant } from "../../db/index";
import { fakeFailNextCreate, fakeReset } from "../payments/fakeProvider";
import { postEntry } from "../wallet/service";

const SECRET = process.env.PAYMENT_WEBHOOK_SECRET_TEST!;
afterEach(() => fakeReset());

async function setup() {
  const schoolId = await createSchool();
  await adminDb().updateTable("schools").set({ paymentWebhookSecretRef: "PAYMENT_WEBHOOK_SECRET_TEST" }).where("id", "=", schoolId).execute();
  const fam = await seedFamily(schoolId, ["Léo"]);
  const cookies = await guardianCookie(schoolId, fam.guardianId, "wallet");
  return { schoolId, guardianId: fam.guardianId, cookies };
}

type Setup = Awaited<ReturnType<typeof setup>>;
const create = (s: Setup, payload: object, key: string = uuidv7()) =>
  buildApp().inject({ method: "POST", url: "/api/v1/topups", cookies: s.cookies, headers: { "idempotency-key": key }, payload });

async function debt(s: Setup, cents: number) {
  await withTenant(s.schoolId, (trx) =>
    postEntry(trx, { schoolId: s.schoolId, guardianId: s.guardianId, kind: "counter_charge", amountCents: -cents, sourceType: "counter_sale", sourceId: uuidv7(), minBalanceCents: null }),
  );
}

async function paymentOf(topupId: string) {
  return adminDb().selectFrom("payments").select(["id", "status", "providerChargeId", "amountCents"]).where("topupId", "=", topupId).executeTakeFirstOrThrow();
}

function webhook(schoolId: string, chargeId: string, amountCents: number, eventId: string = uuidv7()) {
  return buildApp().inject({
    method: "POST",
    url: `/webhooks/payments/fake/${schoolId}`,
    headers: { "content-type": "application/json", "x-fake-token": SECRET },
    payload: JSON.stringify({ id: eventId, event: "charge.paid", charge_id: chargeId, amount_cents: amountCents, paid_at: new Date(Date.now() - 1000).toISOString() }),
  });
}

describe("guardian top-ups", () => {
  it("creates a credit top-up with a Pix and cancels the previous pending one", async () => {
    const s = await setup();
    const first = await create(s, { purpose: "credit", amountCents: 2000 });
    expect(first.statusCode).toBe(200);
    expect(first.json()).toMatchObject({ status: "pending_payment", amountCents: 2000, balanceCents: 0, payment: { status: "pending", amountCents: 2000 } });
    expect(first.json().payment.pixCopyPaste).toMatch(/FAKE/);
    const second = await create(s, { purpose: "credit", amountCents: 3000 });
    expect(second.json().status).toBe("pending_payment");
    const old = await adminDb().selectFrom("topups").select(["status", "requestedBy"]).where("id", "=", first.json().id).executeTakeFirstOrThrow();
    expect(old).toEqual({ status: "cancelled", requestedBy: "guardian" });
    expect((await paymentOf(first.json().id)).status).toBe("expired");
    const { rows } = await sql<{ minutes: number }>`
      SELECT round(extract(epoch FROM expires_at - created_at) / 60)::int AS minutes FROM payments WHERE topup_id = ${second.json().id}`.execute(adminDb());
    expect(rows[0]!.minutes).toBe(60);
  });

  it("charges the current debt and refuses without one", async () => {
    const s = await setup();
    const none = await create(s, { purpose: "debt" });
    expect(none.statusCode).toBe(409);
    expect(none.json().error.code).toBe("wallet_no_debt");
    await debt(s, 1350);
    expect((await create(s, { purpose: "debt" })).json()).toMatchObject({ amountCents: 1350, balanceCents: -1350 });
    expect((await create(s, { purpose: "credit", amountCents: 999 })).statusCode).toBe(400);
  });

  it("credits the wallet once when the webhook confirms, even after the top-up was cancelled", async () => {
    const s = await setup();
    await debt(s, 500);
    const a = (await create(s, { purpose: "credit", amountCents: 2000 })).json();
    const b = (await create(s, { purpose: "credit", amountCents: 1000 })).json(); // cancela a
    const pa = await paymentOf(a.id);
    const pb = await paymentOf(b.id);
    const eventId = uuidv7();
    expect((await webhook(s.schoolId, pb.providerChargeId!, 1000, eventId)).statusCode).toBe(200);
    expect((await webhook(s.schoolId, pb.providerChargeId!, 1000, eventId)).statusCode).toBe(200);
    expect((await webhook(s.schoolId, pb.providerChargeId!, 1000)).statusCode).toBe(200);
    expect((await webhook(s.schoolId, pa.providerChargeId!, 2000)).statusCode).toBe(200); // pago depois de cancelada
    const topups = await adminDb().selectFrom("topups").select(["id", "status"]).where("guardianId", "=", s.guardianId).execute();
    expect(new Map(topups.map((t) => [t.id, t.status]))).toEqual(new Map([[a.id, "paid"], [b.id, "paid"]]));
    const credits = await adminDb().selectFrom("walletEntries").select("amountCents").where("schoolId", "=", s.schoolId).where("kind", "=", "topup_credit").orderBy("amountCents").execute();
    expect(credits).toEqual([{ amountCents: 1000 }, { amountCents: 2000 }]);
    const view = await buildApp().inject({ method: "GET", url: `/api/v1/topups/${b.id}`, cookies: s.cookies });
    expect(view.json()).toMatchObject({ status: "paid", balanceCents: 2500, payment: { status: "paid" } });
    const events = await adminDb()
      .selectFrom("outboxEvents")
      .select(sql<string>`data::text`.as("data"))
      .where("schoolId", "=", s.schoolId)
      .where("type", "=", "wallet.topup_confirmed")
      .orderBy("occurredAt")
      .execute();
    expect(events.map((e) => JSON.parse(e.data))).toEqual([
      expect.objectContaining({ amount_cents: 1000, wallet_balance_cents: 500, first_name: "Ana" }),
      expect.objectContaining({ amount_cents: 2000, wallet_balance_cents: 2500 }),
    ]);
    expect(await walletLedgerMatches(s.schoolId, s.guardianId)).toBe(true);
  });

  it("retries the charge when the gateway was down", async () => {
    const s = await setup();
    fakeFailNextCreate(1);
    const t = (await create(s, { purpose: "credit", amountCents: 2000 })).json();
    expect(t.payment.pixCopyPaste).toBeNull();
    const retried = await buildApp().inject({ method: "POST", url: `/api/v1/topups/${t.id}/charge`, cookies: s.cookies });
    expect(retried.json().payment.pixCopyPaste).toMatch(/FAKE/);
  });

  it("keeps top-ups private to their guardian and scope", async () => {
    const s = await setup();
    const t = (await create(s, { purpose: "credit", amountCents: 2000 })).json();
    const other = await seedFamily(s.schoolId, ["Bia"]);
    const get = async (cookies: Record<string, string>) => (await buildApp().inject({ method: "GET", url: `/api/v1/topups/${t.id}`, cookies })).statusCode;
    expect(await get(await guardianCookie(s.schoolId, other.guardianId))).toBe(404);
    expect(await get(await guardianCookie(s.schoolId, s.guardianId, "documents"))).toBe(403);
    expect(await get(await guardianCookie(s.schoolId, s.guardianId, "order"))).toBe(200);
  });
});

describe("staff top-ups", () => {

});
```

`apps/api/src/modules/payments/repo.ts`:

```ts
export type PaymentRef = { checkoutId: string } | { topupId: string };

/** Pix de recarga: vale 60 min pelo relógio do banco. */
export async function insertTopupPayment(
  trx: Trx,
  row: { schoolId: string; topupId: string; provider: string; amountCents: number },
): Promise<string> {
  const id = uuidv7();
  await trx.insertInto("payments").values({ id, method: "pix", ...row, expiresAt: sql<Date>`now() + interval '60 minutes'` }).execute();
  return id;
}
```

`unchargedPendingPayment(trx, ref: PaymentRef)`: mesmo corpo, com o filtro por alvo:

```ts
export async function unchargedPendingPayment(trx: Trx, ref: PaymentRef) {
  const base = trx
    .selectFrom("payments")
    .select(["id", "provider", "amountCents", "expiresAt"])
    .where("status", "=", "pending")
    .where("providerChargeId", "is", null)
    // Depois do vencimento não se cria cobrança viva (CLAUDE.md regra 15: o tempo vem do banco).
    .where("expiresAt", ">", sql<Date>`now()`);
  return ("checkoutId" in ref ? base.where("checkoutId", "=", ref.checkoutId) : base.where("topupId", "=", ref.topupId)).executeTakeFirst();
}
```

Troque `paymentContext` por:

```ts
/** Alvo do pagamento: checkout (com o dia de serviço) ou recarga. */
export async function paymentTarget(trx: Trx, paymentId: string) {
  return trx
    .selectFrom("payments")
    .leftJoin("checkouts", "checkouts.id", "payments.checkoutId")
    .select(["payments.id", "payments.checkoutId", "payments.topupId", "payments.amountCents", "checkouts.serviceDayId"])
    .where("payments.id", "=", paymentId)
    .executeTakeFirst();
}

export async function paymentsForTopups(trx: Trx, topupIds: string[]) {
  if (topupIds.length === 0) return [];
  return trx
    .selectFrom("payments")
    .select(["topupId", "status", "amountCents", "pixCopyPaste", "pixQrPngBase64", "expiresAt"])
    .where("topupId", "in", topupIds)
    .execute();
}

export async function expireTopupPayment(trx: Trx, topupId: string): Promise<void> {
  await trx.updateTable("payments").set({ status: "expired" }).where("topupId", "=", topupId).where("status", "=", "pending").execute();
}
```

```ts
export async function createTopupPayment(trx: Trx, input: { schoolId: string; topupId: string; amountCents: number }): Promise<string> {

export async function topupPaymentViews(trx: Trx, topupIds: string[]): Promise<Map<string, PaymentView>> {
  const rows = await repo.paymentsForTopups(trx, topupIds);
  return new Map(
    rows.map((p) => [
      p.topupId!,
      { status: p.status as PaymentStatus, amountCents: p.amountCents, pixCopyPaste: p.pixCopyPaste, pixQrPngBase64: p.pixQrPngBase64, expiresAt: p.expiresAt.toISOString() },
    ]),
  );
}

export async function expireTopupPayment(trx: Trx, topupId: string): Promise<void> {
  await repo.expireTopupPayment(trx, topupId);
}
```

- `confirmPayment`:

```ts
export type ConfirmOutcome = "confirmed" | "late_credited" | "already_processed" | "topup_credited";

/** Única função que confirma pagamento (CLAUDE.md regra 10). Webhook e conciliação chamam esta. */
export async function confirmPayment(
  trx: Trx,
  input: { schoolId: string; paymentId: string; paidAt: Date },
): Promise<ConfirmOutcome> {
  const payment = await repo.paymentTarget(trx, input.paymentId);
  if (!payment) throw new Error(`payment ${input.paymentId} not found`);
  if (payment.topupId) {
    // Recarga: travas topups → payments → wallets. Sem corte: paga depois de expirada ou cancelada também credita.
    await topups.lockTopup(trx, payment.topupId);
    if (!(await repo.markPaid(trx, payment.id, input.paidAt))) return "already_processed";
    await topups.creditTopup(trx, { schoolId: input.schoolId, topupId: payment.topupId, paymentId: payment.id, amountCents: payment.amountCents, paidAt: input.paidAt });
    return "topup_credited";
  }
  const checkoutId = payment.checkoutId!;
  // Travas na ordem service_days → checkouts → payments → wallets, a mesma do cancelamento e do fechamento.
  const day = await catalog.lockServiceDayById(trx, payment.serviceDayId!, "share");
  await orders.lockCheckout(trx, checkoutId);
  if (!(await repo.markPaid(trx, payment.id, input.paidAt))) return "already_processed";

  const onTime = day.status === "open" && input.paidAt.getTime() <= day.cutoffAt.getTime();
  if (onTime && (await orders.confirmCheckout(trx, input.schoolId, checkoutId))) return "confirmed";

  await orders.creditLatePayment(trx, { schoolId: input.schoolId, checkoutId, paymentId: payment.id, amountCents: payment.amountCents });
  return "late_credited";
}
```

`apps/api/src/modules/topups/repo.ts`:

```ts
import { sql } from "kysely";
import type { Trx } from "../../db/index";

export async function insertTopup(
  trx: Trx,
  row: { id: string; schoolId: string; guardianId: string; amountCents: number; requestedBy: "guardian" | "staff" },
): Promise<void> {
  await trx.insertInto("topups").values(row).execute();
}

/** Uma recarga pendente por responsável: as anteriores viram `cancelled`. */
export async function cancelPendingTopups(trx: Trx, guardianId: string): Promise<string[]> {
  const rows = await trx
    .updateTable("topups")
    .set({ status: "cancelled" })
    .where("guardianId", "=", guardianId)
    .where("status", "=", "pending_payment")
    .returning("id")
    .execute();
  return rows.map((r) => r.id);
}

export async function lockTopup(trx: Trx, id: string): Promise<void> {
  await trx.selectFrom("topups").select("id").where("id", "=", id).forUpdate().executeTakeFirst();
}

/** pending_payment | expired | cancelled → paid. Recarga não tem corte. */
export async function markTopupPaid(trx: Trx, id: string, paidAt: Date) {
  return trx
    .updateTable("topups")
    .set({ status: "paid", paidAt })
    .where("id", "=", id)
    .where("status", "in", ["pending_payment", "expired", "cancelled"])
    .returning(["guardianId"])
    .executeTakeFirst();
}

export async function topupHeader(trx: Trx, id: string) {
  return trx
    .selectFrom("topups")
    .select(["id", "guardianId", "status", "amountCents", "createdAt", "paidAt"])
    .where("id", "=", id)
    .executeTakeFirst();
}

export async function guardianExists(trx: Trx, id: string): Promise<boolean> {
  return (await trx.selectFrom("guardians").select("id").where("id", "=", id).executeTakeFirst()) !== undefined;
}

/** Recargas pendentes cujo Pix venceu (relógio do banco). */
export async function overdueTopupIds(trx: Trx): Promise<string[]> {
  const { rows } = await sql<{ id: string }>`
    SELECT t.id FROM topups t JOIN payments p ON p.topup_id = t.id
    WHERE t.status = 'pending_payment' AND p.expires_at <= now()
    ORDER BY t.id`.execute(trx);
  return rows.map((r) => r.id);
}

export async function expireTopup(trx: Trx, id: string): Promise<boolean> {
  const row = await trx.updateTable("topups").set({ status: "expired" }).where("id", "=", id).where("status", "=", "pending_payment").returning("id").executeTakeFirst();
  return row !== undefined;
}
```

`apps/api/src/modules/topups/service.ts`:

```ts
import type { GuardianTopupRequest, StaffTopupRequest, TopupStatus, TopupView } from "@app-lanche/contracts";
import type { FastifyBaseLogger } from "fastify";
import { uuidv7 } from "uuidv7";
import { withTenant, type Trx } from "../../db/index";
import { audit } from "../../platform/audit";
import { DomainError } from "../../platform/errors";
import { idempotent, requestHash } from "../../platform/idempotency";
import type { SessionClaims } from "../../platform/session";
import * as payments from "../payments/service";
import * as wallet from "../wallet/service";
import * as repo from "./repo";

const MAX_TOPUP_CENTS = 1_000_000;

async function placeTopup(trx: Trx, schoolId: string, guardianId: string, amountCents: number, requestedBy: "guardian" | "staff"): Promise<string> {
  // Travas topups → payments: cancela as pendentes e expira as cobranças delas.
  for (const id of await repo.cancelPendingTopups(trx, guardianId)) await payments.expireTopupPayment(trx, id);
  const id = uuidv7();
  await repo.insertTopup(trx, { id, schoolId, guardianId, amountCents, requestedBy });
  await payments.createTopupPayment(trx, { schoolId, topupId: id, amountCents });
  return id;
}

async function view(schoolId: string, topupId: string, allowed: (guardianId: string) => boolean): Promise<TopupView> {
  return withTenant(schoolId, async (trx) => {
    const t = await repo.topupHeader(trx, topupId);
    if (!t || !allowed(t.guardianId)) throw new DomainError("not_found");
    return {
      id: t.id,
      status: t.status as TopupStatus,
      amountCents: t.amountCents,
      createdAt: t.createdAt.toISOString(),
      paidAt: t.paidAt ? t.paidAt.toISOString() : null,
      balanceCents: await wallet.balanceOf(trx, t.guardianId),
      payment: (await payments.topupPaymentViews(trx, [t.id])).get(t.id) ?? null,
    };
  });
}

export async function createGuardianTopup(session: SessionClaims, key: string, body: GuardianTopupRequest, log: FastifyBaseLogger): Promise<TopupView> {
  const schoolId = session.schoolId;
  const hash = requestHash({ route: "POST /api/v1/topups", actorId: session.sub, body });
  const topupId = await withTenant(schoolId, async (trx) => {
    const { response } = await idempotent(trx, { schoolId, key, requestHash: hash }, async () => {
      let amountCents: number;
      if (body.purpose === "debt") {
        const balance = await wallet.balanceOf(trx, session.sub);
        if (balance >= 0) throw new DomainError("wallet_no_debt");
        amountCents = Math.min(-balance, MAX_TOPUP_CENTS);
      } else {
        amountCents = body.amountCents;
      }
      return { topupId: await placeTopup(trx, schoolId, session.sub, amountCents, "guardian") };
    });
    return response.topupId;
  });
  await payments.ensureCharge(schoolId, { topupId }, log);
  return view(schoolId, topupId, (g) => g === session.sub);
}

export async function getGuardianTopup(session: SessionClaims, topupId: string): Promise<TopupView> {
  return view(session.schoolId, topupId, (g) => g === session.sub);
}

export async function retryGuardianCharge(session: SessionClaims, topupId: string, log: FastifyBaseLogger): Promise<TopupView> {
  await getGuardianTopup(session, topupId);
  await payments.ensureCharge(session.schoolId, { topupId }, log);
  return getGuardianTopup(session, topupId);
}

export async function createStaffTopup(session: SessionClaims, key: string, guardianId: string, body: StaffTopupRequest, log: FastifyBaseLogger): Promise<TopupView> {
  const schoolId = session.schoolId;
  const hash = requestHash({ route: `POST /api/admin/v1/guardians/${guardianId}/topups`, actorId: session.sub, body });
  const topupId = await withTenant(schoolId, async (trx) => {
    const { response } = await idempotent(trx, { schoolId, key, requestHash: hash }, async () => {
      if (!(await repo.guardianExists(trx, guardianId))) throw new DomainError("not_found");
      const id = await placeTopup(trx, schoolId, guardianId, body.amountCents, "staff");
      await audit(trx, { schoolId, actorKind: "staff", actorId: session.sub, action: "wallet.topup_requested", detail: { guardian_id: guardianId, topup_id: id } });
      return { topupId: id };
    });
    return response.topupId;
  });
  await payments.ensureCharge(schoolId, { topupId }, log);
  return view(schoolId, topupId, () => true);
}

export async function getStaffTopup(session: SessionClaims, topupId: string): Promise<TopupView> {
  return view(session.schoolId, topupId, () => true);
}

export async function retryStaffCharge(session: SessionClaims, topupId: string, log: FastifyBaseLogger): Promise<TopupView> {
  await getStaffTopup(session, topupId);
  await payments.ensureCharge(session.schoolId, { topupId }, log);
  return getStaffTopup(session, topupId);
}

/** Primeira trava do ramo de recarga da confirmPayment (topups → payments → wallets). */
export async function lockTopup(trx: Trx, topupId: string): Promise<void> {
  await repo.lockTopup(trx, topupId);
}

/** Chamado só pela confirmPayment, depois de o pagamento passar a `paid`. */
export async function creditTopup(
  trx: Trx,
  input: { schoolId: string; topupId: string; paymentId: string; amountCents: number; paidAt: Date },
): Promise<void> {
  const paid = await repo.markTopupPaid(trx, input.topupId, input.paidAt);
  if (!paid) return;
  const { balanceCents } = await wallet.postEntry(trx, {
    schoolId: input.schoolId,
    guardianId: paid.guardianId,
    kind: "topup_credit",
    amountCents: input.amountCents,
    sourceType: "topup",
    sourceId: input.topupId,
  });
  await wallet.enqueueTopupConfirmed(trx, input.schoolId, paid.guardianId, input.amountCents, balanceCents);
}

/** Expira a recarga cujo Pix venceu (travas topups → payments). */
export async function expireTopup(trx: Trx, topupId: string): Promise<boolean> {
  await repo.lockTopup(trx, topupId);
  if (!(await repo.expireTopup(trx, topupId))) return false;
  await payments.expireTopupPayment(trx, topupId);
  return true;
}
```

`apps/api/src/modules/topups/routes.ts`:

```ts
import { GuardianTopupRequest, StaffTopupRequest, Uuid } from "@app-lanche/contracts";
import type { FastifyInstance } from "fastify";
import { z } from "zod";
import type { RouteAuth } from "../../app";
import { idempotencyKeyFrom } from "../../platform/idempotency";
import { GUARDIAN_WALLET } from "../wallet/routes";
import * as service from "./service";

const ACCOUNTS: RouteAuth = { kind: "staff", roles: ["admin", "secretaria", "cozinha"] };
const IdParams = z.object({ id: Uuid });
const GuardianParams = z.object({ guardianId: Uuid });

export async function topupRoutes(app: FastifyInstance): Promise<void> {
  app.post("/api/v1/topups", { config: { auth: GUARDIAN_WALLET } }, async (req) =>
    service.createGuardianTopup(req.session!, idempotencyKeyFrom(req), GuardianTopupRequest.parse(req.body), req.log),
  );
  app.get("/api/v1/topups/:id", { config: { auth: GUARDIAN_WALLET } }, async (req) =>
    service.getGuardianTopup(req.session!, IdParams.parse(req.params).id),
  );
  // Sem Idempotency-Key: só tenta de novo criar a cobrança, idempotente pelo id do pagamento.
  app.post("/api/v1/topups/:id/charge", { config: { auth: GUARDIAN_WALLET } }, async (req) =>
    service.retryGuardianCharge(req.session!, IdParams.parse(req.params).id, req.log),
  );

  app.post("/api/admin/v1/guardians/:guardianId/topups", { config: { auth: ACCOUNTS } }, async (req) =>
    service.createStaffTopup(req.session!, idempotencyKeyFrom(req), GuardianParams.parse(req.params).guardianId, StaffTopupRequest.parse(req.body), req.log),
  );
  app.get("/api/admin/v1/topups/:id", { config: { auth: ACCOUNTS } }, async (req) => service.getStaffTopup(req.session!, IdParams.parse(req.params).id));
  app.post("/api/admin/v1/topups/:id/charge", { config: { auth: ACCOUNTS } }, async (req) =>
    service.retryStaffCharge(req.session!, IdParams.parse(req.params).id, req.log),
  );
}
```

```ts
import { sql } from "kysely";
import { uuidv7 } from "uuidv7";
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { adminDb, createSchool, internalRequest, seedFamily, staffCookie, zoneAtLocalHour } from "../../../test/helpers";
import { buildApp } from "../../app";
import { withTenant } from "../../db/index";
import { listDueActions } from "../serviceDays/service";
import { postEntry } from "../wallet/service";

async function indebted(cents = 700) {
  const schoolId = await createSchool();
  await adminDb().updateTable("schools").set({ timezone: zoneAtLocalHour(12) }).where("id", "=", schoolId).execute();
  const fam = await seedFamily(schoolId, ["Léo"]);
  await withTenant(schoolId, (trx) =>
    postEntry(trx, { schoolId, guardianId: fam.guardianId, kind: "counter_charge", amountCents: -cents, sourceType: "counter_sale", sourceId: uuidv7(), minBalanceCents: null }),
  );
  return { schoolId, guardianId: fam.guardianId };
}

const mine = async (schoolId: string) => (await listDueActions()).filter((d) => d.schoolId === schoolId).map((d) => d.action);
const setNoticeAgo = (guardianId: string, interval: string) =>
  adminDb().updateTable("wallets").set({ debtNoticeAt: sql<Date>`now() - ${interval}::interval` }).where("guardianId", "=", guardianId).execute();

describe("debt notices", () => {
  it("are due for a negative wallet not noticed in 3 days, only in local business hours", async () => {
    const s = await indebted();
    expect(await mine(s.schoolId)).toEqual(["send_debt_notices"]);
    await setNoticeAgo(s.guardianId, "2 days");
    expect(await mine(s.schoolId)).toEqual([]);
    await setNoticeAgo(s.guardianId, "3 days 1 minute");
    expect(await mine(s.schoolId)).toEqual(["send_debt_notices"]);
    await adminDb().updateTable("schools").set({ timezone: zoneAtLocalHour(3) }).where("id", "=", s.schoolId).execute();
    expect(await mine(s.schoolId)).toEqual([]);
  });

  it("are listed to n8n and enqueued once per due wallet by the internal route", async () => {
    const s = await indebted(1350);
    const app = buildApp();
    const due = await app.inject(internalRequest("GET", "/internal/v1/schedule/due"));
    expect(due.json().due.filter((d: { school_id: string }) => d.school_id === s.schoolId)).toEqual([
      { action: "send_debt_notices", school_id: s.schoolId, service_date: expect.stringMatching(/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/) },
    ]);
    const url = `/internal/v1/schools/${s.schoolId}/debt-notices`;
    expect((await app.inject(internalRequest("POST", url))).json()).toEqual({ enqueued: 1 });
    expect((await app.inject(internalRequest("POST", url))).json()).toEqual({ enqueued: 0 });
    const events = await adminDb().selectFrom("outboxEvents").select(sql<string>`data::text`.as("data")).where("schoolId", "=", s.schoolId).where("type", "=", "wallet.debt_notice").execute();
    expect(events.map((e) => JSON.parse(e.data))).toEqual([expect.objectContaining({ guardian_id: s.guardianId, amount_due_cents: 1350 })]);
  });

  it("hides the top-up reconciliation from n8n", async () => {
    const schoolId = await createSchool();
    const fam = await seedFamily(schoolId, ["Léo"]);
    await adminDb().insertInto("topups").values({ id: uuidv7(), schoolId, guardianId: fam.guardianId, amountCents: 1000, requestedBy: "guardian" }).execute();
    expect(await mine(schoolId)).toEqual(["reconcile_topups"]);
    const due = await buildApp().inject(internalRequest("GET", "/internal/v1/schedule/due"));
    expect(due.json().due.filter((d: { school_id: string }) => d.school_id === schoolId)).toEqual([]);
  });
});

describe("payment requests", () => {
  it("sends the payment link once per key, only with a debt", async () => {
    const s = await indebted(1350);
    const { cookies } = await staffCookie(s.schoolId, "cozinha");
    const key = uuidv7();
    const ask = (guardianId: string, k: string = key) =>
      buildApp().inject({ method: "POST", url: `/api/admin/v1/guardians/${guardianId}/payment-requests`, cookies, headers: { "idempotency-key": k } });
    expect((await ask(s.guardianId)).json()).toEqual({ amountDueCents: 1350 });
    expect((await ask(s.guardianId)).json()).toEqual({ amountDueCents: 1350 });
    const events = await adminDb().selectFrom("outboxEvents").select("type").where("schoolId", "=", s.schoolId).where("type", "=", "wallet.payment_requested").execute();
    expect(events).toHaveLength(1);
    const other = await seedFamily(s.schoolId, ["Bia"]);
    const none = await ask(other.guardianId, uuidv7());
    expect(none.statusCode).toBe(409);
    expect(none.json().error.code).toBe("wallet_no_debt");
    expect((await ask(uuidv7(), uuidv7())).statusCode).toBe(404);
  });
});
```

`apps/api/src/modules/topups/reconcile.test.ts`:

```ts
import { uuidv7 } from "uuidv7";
import { afterEach, describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { adminDb, createSchool, guardianCookie, seedFamily, walletLedgerMatches } from "../../../test/helpers";
import { buildApp } from "../../app";
import { fakeMarkPaid, fakeReset } from "../payments/fakeProvider";
import { reconcileDue } from "./service";

const log = { warn: () => undefined, info: () => undefined, error: () => undefined } as never;
afterEach(() => fakeReset());

async function topup(schoolId: string, guardianId: string, amountCents: number) {
  const res = await buildApp().inject({
    method: "POST",
    url: "/api/v1/topups",
    cookies: await guardianCookie(schoolId, guardianId, "wallet"),
    headers: { "idempotency-key": uuidv7() },
    payload: { purpose: "credit", amountCents },
  });
  const payment = await adminDb().selectFrom("payments").select(["id", "providerChargeId"]).where("topupId", "=", res.json().id).executeTakeFirstOrThrow();
  return { topupId: res.json().id as string, paymentId: payment.id, chargeId: payment.providerChargeId! };
}

describe("top-up reconciliation job", () => {
  it("confirms paid charges, expires overdue top-ups and then has nothing to do", async () => {
    const schoolId = await createSchool();
    const ana = await seedFamily(schoolId, ["Léo"]);
    const bruno = await seedFamily(schoolId, ["Caio"]);
    const paid = await topup(schoolId, ana.guardianId, 2000);
    const stale = await topup(schoolId, bruno.guardianId, 1500);
    fakeMarkPaid(paid.chargeId, new Date(Date.now() - 60_000));
    await adminDb().updateTable("payments").set({ expiresAt: new Date(Date.now() - 60_000) }).where("id", "=", stale.paymentId).execute();

    expect(await reconcileDue({ log, schoolIds: [schoolId] })).toEqual({ schools: 1, confirmed: 1, expired: 1, failed: 0 });
    const statuses = await adminDb().selectFrom("topups").select(["id", "status"]).where("schoolId", "=", schoolId).execute();
    expect(new Map(statuses.map((t) => [t.id, t.status]))).toEqual(new Map([[paid.topupId, "paid"], [stale.topupId, "expired"]]));
    const stalePayment = await adminDb().selectFrom("payments").select("status").where("id", "=", stale.paymentId).executeTakeFirstOrThrow();
    expect(stalePayment.status).toBe("expired");
    const wallet = await adminDb().selectFrom("wallets").select("balanceCents").where("guardianId", "=", ana.guardianId).executeTakeFirstOrThrow();
    expect(wallet.balanceCents).toBe(2000);
    expect(await walletLedgerMatches(schoolId, ana.guardianId)).toBe(true);
    expect(await reconcileDue({ log, schoolIds: [schoolId] })).toEqual({ schools: 0, confirmed: 0, expired: 0, failed: 0 });
  });
});
```

```ts
type N8nAction = Exclude<serviceDays.DueAction["action"], "reconcile_topups">;
/** A conciliação de recargas é do job do Cloud Scheduler: o n8n não toca pagamento (CLAUDE.md regra 21). */
const forN8n = (d: serviceDays.DueAction): d is serviceDays.DueAction & { action: N8nAction } => d.action !== "reconcile_topups";
```

```ts
/** Marca o aviso das carteiras negativas sem aviso há 3 dias (relógio do banco) e devolve quais eram. */
export async function claimDueDebtNotices(trx: Trx) {
  const { rows } = await sql<{ guardianId: string; balanceCents: number }>`
    UPDATE wallets SET debt_notice_at = now()
    WHERE balance_cents < 0 AND (debt_notice_at IS NULL OR debt_notice_at <= now() - interval '3 days')
    RETURNING guardian_id, balance_cents`.execute(trx);
  return rows;
}
```

`apps/api/src/modules/wallet/service.ts`:

```ts
const MAX_DUE_CENTS = 1_000_000;

export async function sendDueDebtNotices(schoolId: string): Promise<DebtNoticesResponse> {
  return withTenant(schoolId, async (trx) => {
    const due = await repo.claimDueDebtNotices(trx);
    for (const w of due) await enqueueDebtNotice(trx, schoolId, w.guardianId, Math.min(-w.balanceCents, MAX_DUE_CENTS));
    return { enqueued: due.length };
  });
}

/** Link de pagamento por WhatsApp a pedido da equipe (o n8n pede o magic link de escopo `wallet`). */
export async function requestPayment(session: SessionClaims, key: string, guardianId: string): Promise<PaymentRequestResponse> {
  const schoolId = session.schoolId;
  const hash = requestHash({ route: `POST /api/admin/v1/guardians/${guardianId}/payment-requests`, actorId: session.sub, body: {} });
  return withTenant(schoolId, async (trx) => {
    const { response } = await idempotent(trx, { schoolId, key, requestHash: hash }, async () => {
      if (!(await repo.guardianExists(trx, guardianId))) throw new DomainError("not_found");
      const balance = await repo.readBalance(trx, guardianId);
      if (balance >= 0) throw new DomainError("wallet_no_debt");
      const amountDueCents = Math.min(-balance, MAX_DUE_CENTS);
      await enqueueDebtNotice(trx, schoolId, guardianId, amountDueCents, "wallet.payment_requested");
      await audit(trx, { schoolId, actorKind: "staff", actorId: session.sub, action: "wallet.payment_requested", detail: { guardian_id: guardianId } });
      return { amountDueCents };
    });
    return response;
  });
}
```

(importe os tipos `DebtNoticesResponse`, `PaymentRequestResponse`.)

```ts
export async function pendingTopupCharges(trx: Trx) {

    .selectFrom("payments")
    .innerJoin("topups", "topups.id", "payments.topupId")
    .select(["payments.id", "payments.provider", "payments.providerChargeId", "payments.amountCents"])
    .where("topups.status", "=", "pending_payment")
    .where("payments.status", "=", "pending")

    .execute();
}
```

`apps/api/src/modules/payments/service.ts`: extraia o laço de `reconcileServiceDay` para uma função comum e acrescente a de recargas:

```ts
type PendingCharge = { id: string; provider: string; providerChargeId: string | null; amountCents: number };

/** Consulta cada cobrança no gateway (fora de transação) e confirma as pagas pela confirmPayment. Devolve quantas confirmou. */
async function confirmPaidCharges(schoolId: string, pending: PendingCharge[], accountRef: string | null, log: FastifyBaseLogger): Promise<number> {
  let confirmed = 0;
  for (const p of pending) {
    let charge;
    try {
      charge = await getProvider(p.provider).getCharge(p.providerChargeId!, accountRef);
    } catch {
      log.warn({ event: "reconcile_charge_failed", payment_id: p.id }, "reconciliation lookup failed");
      continue;
    }
    if (charge.status !== "paid" || !charge.paidAt) continue;
    if (charge.amountCents !== p.amountCents) {
      log.error({ event: "payment_amount_mismatch", school_id: schoolId, payment_id: p.id }, "payment amount mismatch");
      continue;
    }
    const paidAt = charge.paidAt;
    const outcome = await withTenant(schoolId, (trx) => confirmPayment(trx, { schoolId, paymentId: p.id, paidAt }));
    if (outcome !== "already_processed") confirmed += 1;
  }
  return confirmed;
}

/** Consulta no gateway as cobranças pendentes do dia e confirma as pagas pela mesma confirmPayment. */
export async function reconcileServiceDay(schoolId: string, serviceDate: string, log: FastifyBaseLogger): Promise<void> {
  const { pending, school } = await withTenant(schoolId, async (trx) => ({
    pending: await repo.pendingChargesForDay(trx, serviceDate),
    school: await repo.schoolPaymentConfig(trx),
  }));
  await confirmPaidCharges(schoolId, pending, school.paymentAccountRef, log);
}

export async function reconcileTopupCharges(schoolId: string, log: FastifyBaseLogger): Promise<number> {
  const { pending, school } = await withTenant(schoolId, async (trx) => ({
    pending: await repo.pendingTopupCharges(trx),
    school: await repo.schoolPaymentConfig(trx),
  }));
  return confirmPaidCharges(schoolId, pending, school.paymentAccountRef, log);
}
```

`apps/api/src/modules/topups/service.ts` (importe `* as serviceDays from "../serviceDays/service"` e o tipo `TopupReconcileResponse`):

```ts
/** Confirma recargas pagas (gateway) e expira as que venceram. */
export async function reconcileTopups(schoolId: string, log: FastifyBaseLogger): Promise<{ confirmed: number; expired: number }> {
  const confirmed = await payments.reconcileTopupCharges(schoolId, log);
  const expired = await withTenant(schoolId, async (trx) => {
    let n = 0;
    for (const id of await repo.overdueTopupIds(trx)) if (await expireTopup(trx, id)) n += 1;
    return n;
  });
  return { confirmed, expired };
}

/** Job do Cloud Scheduler: escolas com recarga pendente vêm da agenda (list_due_actions); `schoolIds` restringe (testes). */
export async function reconcileDue(opts: { log: FastifyBaseLogger; schoolIds?: string[] }): Promise<TopupReconcileResponse> {
  const due = (await serviceDays.listDueActions()).filter(
    (d) => d.action === "reconcile_topups" && (!opts.schoolIds || opts.schoolIds.includes(d.schoolId)),
  );
  const result = { schools: due.length, confirmed: 0, expired: 0, failed: 0 };
  for (const d of due) {
    try {
      const r = await reconcileTopups(d.schoolId, opts.log);
      result.confirmed += r.confirmed;
      result.expired += r.expired;
    } catch (err) {
      result.failed += 1;
      opts.log.error({ school_id: d.schoolId, err_name: err instanceof Error ? err.name : "UnknownError" }, "top-up reconciliation failed");
    }
  }
  return result;
}
```

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";

const row = (studentId: string, firstName: string, className: string, guardianId: string | null, balanceCents = 0): RosterRow => ({
```
