`apps/web/src/ui/counter.ts`:

```ts
import type { CounterRosterResponse } from "@app-lanche/contracts";

/** Regras do lançamento no balcão (tablet e admin). Só para a tela: preço e total valem os do servidor (regra 7). */
export type RosterItem = CounterRosterResponse["items"][number];
export type RosterRow = {
  studentId: string;
  firstName: string;
  className: string;
  restrictions: string | null;
  guardianId: string | null;
  guardianFirstName: string | null;
  balanceCents: number;
};
export type Cart = Record<string, number>;
export type SaleLine = { menuItemId: string; name: string; qty: number; unitPriceCents: number };

export const OVER_LIMIT_TEXT = "Saldo insuficiente: peça à secretaria";
export const MAX_QTY = 10;

const fold = (s: string) => s.normalize("NFD").replace(/\p{M}/gu, "").toLowerCase();

export function filterRoster(rows: RosterRow[], filter: { query: string; className: string | null }): RosterRow[] {
  const q = fold(filter.query.trim());
  return rows.filter((r) => (filter.className === null || r.className === filter.className) && (q === "" || fold(r.firstName).includes(q)));
}

export function setQty(cart: Cart, menuItemId: string, qty: number): Cart {
  const next = { ...cart };
  if (qty <= 0) delete next[menuItemId];
  else next[menuItemId] = Math.min(Math.trunc(qty), MAX_QTY);
  return next;
}

export function cartLines(cart: Cart, items: RosterItem[]): SaleLine[] {
  return items
    .filter((i) => (cart[i.id] ?? 0) > 0)
    .map((i) => ({ menuItemId: i.id, name: i.name, qty: cart[i.id]!, unitPriceCents: i.priceCents }));
}

export function cartTotal(cart: Cart, items: RosterItem[]): number {
  return cartLines(cart, items).reduce((sum, l) => sum + l.qty * l.unitPriceCents, 0);
}

/** O saldo depois do lançamento não pode ficar abaixo de -limite (spec F5B). */
export function canSell(balanceCents: number, totalCents: number, limitCents: number): boolean {
  return totalCents > 0 && balanceCents - totalCents >= -limitCents;
}

/** Ajuste do saldo local pelo que ainda está na outbox: lançamento debita, estorno credita. */
export function pendingDelta(rows: { kind: string; guardianId?: string; totalCents?: number }[], guardianId: string): number {
  let delta = 0;
  for (const r of rows) {
    if (r.guardianId !== guardianId || r.totalCents === undefined) continue;
    if (r.kind === "counter_sale") delta -= r.totalCents;
    else if (r.kind === "counter_void") delta += r.totalCents;
  }
  return delta;
}

export function rosterRows(res: CounterRosterResponse, pending: { kind: string; guardianId?: string; totalCents?: number }[]): RosterRow[] {
  return res.students.map((s) => ({
    studentId: s.id,
    firstName: s.firstName,
    className: s.className,
    restrictions: s.restrictions,
    guardianId: s.guardian?.id ?? null,
    guardianFirstName: s.guardian?.firstName ?? null,
    balanceCents: s.guardian ? s.guardian.balanceCents + pendingDelta(pending, s.guardian.id) : 0,
  }));
}
```

`apps/web/src/areas/kds/db.ts` (substitua o arquivo):

```ts
import Dexie, { type EntityTable } from "dexie";
import type { CounterRosterResponse, KdsManifestResponse } from "@app-lanche/contracts";
import { canSell, rosterRows, type RosterRow, type SaleLine } from "../../ui/counter";
import { mergeEvent, nextOccurredAt, type DeliveryEvent, type OrderState } from "./state";

export type ManifestRow = KdsManifestResponse & { fetchedAt: string };
export type DeliveryOutboxRow = DeliveryEvent & { kind: "delivery"; serviceDate: string };
/** `eventId` = id do lançamento (regra 36): é ele que o servidor devolve em accepted/rejected. */
export type CounterSaleOutboxRow = {
  kind: "counter_sale";
  eventId: string;
  occurredAt: string;
  serviceDate: string;
  studentId: string;
  items: { menuItemId: string; qty: number }[];
  guardianId: string;
  totalCents: number;
};
/** Estorno: `eventId` próprio; o servidor responde pelo `saleId`. */
export type CounterVoidOutboxRow = {
  kind: "counter_void";
  eventId: string;
  occurredAt: string;
  serviceDate: string;
  saleId: string;
  guardianId: string;
  totalCents: number;
};
export type OutboxRow = DeliveryOutboxRow | CounterSaleOutboxRow | CounterVoidOutboxRow;
/** Lista do dia na aba "Lançar" (com "Desfazer"); o status vem da resposta do servidor. */
export type CounterSaleRow = {
  saleId: string;
  serviceDate: string;
  occurredAt: string;
  studentId: string;
  studentFirstName: string;
  guardianId: string;
  lines: SaleLine[];
  totalCents: number;
  status: "pending" | "accepted" | "rejected";
  voided: boolean;
};
export type MetaRow = { key: string; value: string };

export const ROSTER_LIMIT = "roster:limit";
export const ROSTER_ITEMS = "roster:items";
export const ROSTER_FETCHED_AT = "roster:fetchedAt";

class KdsDb extends Dexie {
  manifest!: EntityTable<ManifestRow, "serviceDate">;
  orderState!: EntityTable<OrderState, "orderId">;
  outbox!: EntityTable<OutboxRow, "eventId">;
  meta!: EntityTable<MetaRow, "key">;
  roster!: EntityTable<RosterRow, "studentId">;
  counterSales!: EntityTable<CounterSaleRow, "saleId">;

  constructor() {
    super("app-lanche-kds");
    // A future schema version must keep the `outbox` store and its key (eventId): pending deliveries live there.
    this.version(1).stores({
      manifest: "serviceDate",
      orderState: "orderId, serviceDate",
      outbox: "eventId, occurredAt, serviceDate",
      meta: "key",
    });
    // v2 (F5B): same outbox store and key, plus a `kind` index; rows written by v1 are deliveries.
    this.version(2)
      .stores({
        manifest: "serviceDate",
        orderState: "orderId, serviceDate",
        outbox: "eventId, occurredAt, serviceDate, kind",
        meta: "key",
        roster: "studentId, guardianId",
        counterSales: "saleId, serviceDate",
      })
      .upgrade((tx) =>
        tx
          .table("outbox")
          .toCollection()
          .modify((row: { kind?: string }) => {
            row.kind ??= "delivery";
          }),
      );
  }
}

export const kdsDb = new KdsDb();

export async function getMeta(key: string): Promise<string | null> {
  return (await kdsDb.meta.get(key))?.value ?? null;
}

export async function setMeta(key: string, value: string): Promise<void> {
  await kdsDb.meta.put({ key, value });
}

/** CLAUDE.md 33: uma transação atualiza orderState e insere na outbox. */
export async function markOrder(orderId: string, serviceDate: string, delivered: boolean): Promise<void> {
  await kdsDb.transaction("rw", kdsDb.orderState, kdsDb.outbox, async () => {
    const current = await kdsDb.orderState.get(orderId);
    // The tap must supersede the state on screen even if that state came from a tablet with a clock ahead.
    const ev: DeliveryEvent = {
      eventId: crypto.randomUUID(),
      orderId,
      type: delivered ? "delivered" : "undelivered",
      occurredAt: nextOccurredAt(new Date().toISOString(), current?.occurredAt),
    };
    const next = mergeEvent(current, ev, serviceDate);
    if (next) await kdsDb.orderState.put(next);
    await kdsDb.outbox.add({ ...ev, kind: "delivery", serviceDate });
  });
}

/** Eventos vindos do servidor (outros tablets): só alteram o estado se forem mais recentes. */
export async function applyRemoteEvents(serviceDate: string, events: DeliveryEvent[]): Promise<void> {
  await kdsDb.transaction("rw", kdsDb.orderState, async () => {
    for (const ev of events) {
      const next = mergeEvent(await kdsDb.orderState.get(ev.orderId), ev, serviceDate);
      if (next) await kdsDb.orderState.put(next);
    }
  });
}

export class CounterSaleRefused extends Error {
  constructor(public readonly reason: "over_limit" | "no_guardian" | "unknown_student" | "empty") {
    super(reason);
  }
}

/** Soma ao saldo local de todos os alunos do responsável. Chame dentro de uma transação que inclua `roster`. */
export async function adjustRosterBalance(guardianId: string, deltaCents: number): Promise<void> {
  await kdsDb.roster
    .where("guardianId")
    .equals(guardianId)
    .modify((r) => {
      r.balanceCents += deltaCents;
    });
}

/**
 * Lançamento no balcão (regras 32–34 valem também aqui): uma transação confere o limite com o último saldo baixado,
 * grava na outbox e na lista do dia e ajusta o saldo local. Devolve o id do lançamento.
 */
export async function recordCounterSale(input: { serviceDate: string; studentId: string; lines: SaleLine[] }): Promise<string> {
  return kdsDb.transaction("rw", [kdsDb.roster, kdsDb.outbox, kdsDb.counterSales, kdsDb.meta], async () => {
    const student = await kdsDb.roster.get(input.studentId);
    if (!student) throw new CounterSaleRefused("unknown_student");
    if (!student.guardianId) throw new CounterSaleRefused("no_guardian");
    if (input.lines.length === 0) throw new CounterSaleRefused("empty");
    const totalCents = input.lines.reduce((sum, l) => sum + l.qty * l.unitPriceCents, 0);
    const limit = Number((await kdsDb.meta.get(ROSTER_LIMIT))?.value ?? "0");
    if (!canSell(student.balanceCents, totalCents, limit)) throw new CounterSaleRefused("over_limit");
    const saleId = crypto.randomUUID();
    const occurredAt = new Date().toISOString();
    await kdsDb.outbox.add({
      kind: "counter_sale",
      eventId: saleId,
      occurredAt,
      serviceDate: input.serviceDate,
      studentId: student.studentId,
      items: input.lines.map(({ menuItemId, qty }) => ({ menuItemId, qty })),
      guardianId: student.guardianId,
      totalCents,
    });
    await kdsDb.counterSales.add({
      saleId,
      serviceDate: input.serviceDate,
      occurredAt,
      studentId: student.studentId,
      studentFirstName: student.firstName,
      guardianId: student.guardianId,
      lines: input.lines,
      totalCents,
      status: "pending",
      voided: false,
    });
    await adjustRosterBalance(student.guardianId, -totalCents);
    return saleId;
  });
}

/** "Desfazer": estorno na outbox e saldo local devolvido, na mesma transação. */
export async function voidCounterSale(saleId: string): Promise<void> {
  await kdsDb.transaction("rw", kdsDb.roster, kdsDb.outbox, kdsDb.counterSales, async () => {
    const sale = await kdsDb.counterSales.get(saleId);
    if (!sale || sale.voided || sale.status === "rejected") return;
    await kdsDb.counterSales.update(saleId, { voided: true });
    await kdsDb.outbox.add({
      kind: "counter_void",
      eventId: crypto.randomUUID(),
      occurredAt: new Date().toISOString(),
      serviceDate: sale.serviceDate,
      saleId,
      guardianId: sale.guardianId,
      totalCents: sale.totalCents,
    });
    await adjustRosterBalance(sale.guardianId, sale.totalCents);
  });
}

/** Roster novo do servidor; o saldo local reaplica o que ainda está na outbox. */
export async function replaceRoster(res: CounterRosterResponse): Promise<void> {
  await kdsDb.transaction("rw", kdsDb.roster, kdsDb.outbox, kdsDb.meta, async () => {
    const pending = await kdsDb.outbox.where("kind").anyOf("counter_sale", "counter_void").toArray();
    await kdsDb.roster.clear();
    await kdsDb.roster.bulkPut(rosterRows(res, pending));
    await kdsDb.meta.bulkPut([
      { key: ROSTER_LIMIT, value: String(res.debtLimitCents) },
      { key: ROSTER_ITEMS, value: JSON.stringify(res.items) },
      { key: ROSTER_FETCHED_AT, value: new Date().toISOString() },
    ]);
  });
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Sincronização**

`apps/web/src/areas/kds/sync.ts`:
- Imports: `CounterRosterResponse` de `@app-lanche/contracts`; de `./db`: `adjustRosterBalance`, `replaceRoster`, `type CounterSaleOutboxRow`, `type CounterVoidOutboxRow`, `type DeliveryOutboxRow`.
- `DATED_META` não muda; acrescente `const ROSTER_META = "roster:";`.
- Em `pair`, a transação passa a incluir `kdsDb.roster, kdsDb.counterSales` e, além do que já limpa, faz `await kdsDb.roster.clear(); await kdsDb.counterSales.clear(); await kdsDb.meta.where("key").startsWith(ROSTER_META).delete();`.
- Em `unpair`, a transação passa a incluir `kdsDb.roster, kdsDb.counterSales` e limpa os dois depois da checagem da outbox.
- Substitua `pushOutbox` por:

```ts
async function pushDeliveries(): Promise<void> {
  const pending = (await kdsDb.outbox.where("kind").equals("delivery").sortBy("occurredAt")) as DeliveryOutboxRow[];
  for (const batch of chunk(pending, 100)) {
    const res = await apiFetch("/api/kds/v1/events", KdsEventsResponse, {
      method: "POST",
      body: { events: batch.map(({ eventId, orderId, type, occurredAt }) => ({ eventId, orderId, type, occurredAt })) },
    });
    // CLAUDE.md 34: só sai da outbox o que o servidor confirmou (aceito ou recusado em definitivo).
    const rejected = new Set(res.rejected);
    await kdsDb.transaction("rw", kdsDb.outbox, kdsDb.orderState, async () => {
      for (const ev of batch) {
        if (!rejected.has(ev.eventId)) continue;
        // Do not keep showing a delivery the server refused.
        const row = await kdsDb.orderState.get(ev.orderId);
        if (row?.eventId === ev.eventId) await kdsDb.orderState.delete(ev.orderId);
      }
      await kdsDb.outbox.bulkDelete([...res.accepted, ...res.rejected]);
    });
  }
}

/** Lançamentos do balcão em lotes de 100. Recusado: sai da outbox, fica "recusado" na lista e o saldo local volta. */
async function pushCounterSales(): Promise<void> {
  const pending = (await kdsDb.outbox.where("kind").equals("counter_sale").sortBy("occurredAt")) as CounterSaleOutboxRow[];
  for (const batch of chunk(pending, 100)) {
    const res = await apiFetch("/api/kds/v1/counter-sales", KdsEventsResponse, {
      method: "POST",
      body: { sales: batch.map((s) => ({ id: s.eventId, studentId: s.studentId, items: s.items, occurredAt: s.occurredAt })) },
    });
    const accepted = new Set(res.accepted);
    const rejected = new Set(res.rejected);
    await kdsDb.transaction("rw", kdsDb.outbox, kdsDb.counterSales, kdsDb.roster, async () => {
      for (const s of batch) {
        if (rejected.has(s.eventId)) {
          const sale = await kdsDb.counterSales.get(s.eventId);
          // Um lançamento já desfeito localmente teve o saldo devolvido pelo estorno.
          if (!sale?.voided) await adjustRosterBalance(s.guardianId, s.totalCents);
          await kdsDb.counterSales.update(s.eventId, { status: "rejected" });
        } else if (accepted.has(s.eventId)) {
          await kdsDb.counterSales.update(s.eventId, { status: "accepted" });
        }
      }
      await kdsDb.outbox.bulkDelete([...res.accepted, ...res.rejected]);
    });
  }
}

/** Estornos depois dos lançamentos. O servidor responde pelo id do lançamento. */
async function pushCounterVoids(): Promise<void> {
  const pending = (await kdsDb.outbox.where("kind").equals("counter_void").sortBy("occurredAt")) as CounterVoidOutboxRow[];
  for (const batch of chunk(pending, 100)) {
    const res = await apiFetch("/api/kds/v1/counter-voids", KdsEventsResponse, {
      method: "POST",
      body: { voids: batch.map((v) => ({ saleId: v.saleId, occurredAt: v.occurredAt })) },
    });
    const done = new Set([...res.accepted, ...res.rejected]);
    const rejected = new Set(res.rejected);
    await kdsDb.transaction("rw", kdsDb.outbox, kdsDb.counterSales, kdsDb.roster, async () => {
      for (const v of batch) {
        if (!rejected.has(v.saleId)) continue;
        const sale = await kdsDb.counterSales.get(v.saleId);
        // Estorno recusado de um lançamento aceito: o lanche continua cobrado.
        if (sale && sale.status !== "rejected" && sale.voided) {
          await kdsDb.counterSales.update(v.saleId, { voided: false });
          await adjustRosterBalance(v.guardianId, -v.totalCents);
        }
      }
      await kdsDb.outbox.bulkDelete(batch.filter((v) => done.has(v.saleId)).map((v) => v.eventId));
    });
  }
}

async function pushOutbox(): Promise<void> {
  await pushDeliveries();
  await pushCounterSales();
  await pushCounterVoids();
}

async function pullRoster(deviceId: string): Promise<void> {
  const res = await apiFetch("/api/kds/v1/roster", CounterRosterResponse);
  if (!(await stillPaired(deviceId))) return;
  await replaceRoster(res);
}
```

- `pruneOldDays`: a transação passa a incluir `kdsDb.counterSales, kdsDb.roster` e, depois do que já faz:

```ts
    await kdsDb.counterSales.where("serviceDate").below(cutoff).and((s) => old(s.serviceDate)).delete();
    // Roster sem atualização há mais de 2 dias (tablet parado): some, como o romaneio antigo (LGPD).
    const fetchedAt = (await kdsDb.meta.get(ROSTER_FETCHED_AT))?.value;
    if (fetchedAt && localDate(new Date(fetchedAt)) < cutoff) {
      await kdsDb.roster.clear();
      await kdsDb.meta.where("key").startsWith(ROSTER_META).delete();
    }
```

  (importe `ROSTER_FETCHED_AT` de `./db`; o comentário da função passa a "apaga romaneio, estado, cursores e lançamentos do balcão de dias com mais de 2 dias, exceto o dia na tela, e o roster parado há mais de 2 dias. Nunca toca na outbox.").
- `markAuthLost`: a transação passa a incluir `kdsDb.roster, kdsDb.counterSales`; depois de limpar romaneio e estado, `await kdsDb.roster.clear(); await kdsDb.counterSales.clear(); await kdsDb.meta.where("key").startsWith(ROSTER_META).delete();`. O comentário vira "apaga o romaneio, o roster e a lista do balcão (LGPD) e mantém a outbox e o deviceId para o novo pareamento."
- `round`: depois de `await pullChanges(serviceDate, deviceId);`, `await pullRoster(deviceId);`.

```tsx
import { useState } from "react";
import { Alert, Button, Card, CardBody, CardHeader, Input, Label, Nav, NavItem, NavLink, Table } from "reactstrap";
import { StatusBadge } from "../../theme/StatusBadge";
import { canSell, cartLines, cartTotal, filterRoster, MAX_QTY, OVER_LIMIT_TEXT, setQty, type Cart, type RosterItem, type RosterRow, type SaleLine } from "../../ui/counter";
import { formatCents } from "../../ui/money";
import type { CounterSaleRow } from "./db";

const STATUS: Record<CounterSaleRow["status"], { tone: "warning" | "success" | "danger"; label: string }> = {
  pending: { tone: "warning", label: "pendente" },
  accepted: { tone: "success", label: "enviado" },
  rejected: { tone: "danger", label: "recusado" },
};

export type CounterViewProps = {
  roster: RosterRow[];
  items: RosterItem[];
  limitCents: number;
  sales: CounterSaleRow[];
  onSell: (studentId: string, lines: SaleLine[]) => Promise<void>;
  onUndo: (saleId: string) => Promise<void>;
};

export function CounterView({ roster, items, limitCents, sales, onSell, onUndo }: CounterViewProps) {
  const [query, setQuery] = useState("");
  const [className, setClassName] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [studentId, setStudentId] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [cart, setCart] = useState<Cart>({});
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);

  const classes = [...new Set(roster.map((r) => r.className))].sort((a, b) => a.localeCompare(b));
  const visible = filterRoster(roster, { query, className }).sort((a, b) => a.firstName.localeCompare(b.firstName));
  const student = roster.find((r) => r.studentId === studentId) ?? null;
  const lines = cartLines(cart, items);
  const total = cartTotal(cart, items);
  const after = (student?.balanceCents ?? 0) - total;
  const overLimit = !!student?.guardianId && total > 0 && !canSell(student.balanceCents, total, limitCents);
  const allowed = !!student?.guardianId && lines.length > 0 && lines.length <= 10 && canSell(student.balanceCents, total, limitCents);

  function choose(id: string) {
    setStudentId(id);
    setCart({});
  }

  async function sell() {
    if (!student || !allowed) return;
    setBusy(true);
    try {
      await onSell(student.studentId, lines);
      setCart({});
      setStudentId(null);
      setQuery("");
    } finally {
      setBusy(false);
    }
  }

  return (
    <div className="kds-counter">
      <Card className="main-card mb-3">
        <CardHeader tag="h2" className="h6 mb-0">Aluno</CardHeader>
        <CardBody>
          <Label for="kds-counter-search">Buscar aluno</Label>
          <Input id="kds-counter-search" data-testid="kds-counter-search" type="search" value={query} onChange={(e) => setQuery(e.target.value)} />
          <div role="group" aria-label="Filtrar por turma" className="my-2">
            <Nav pills>
              <NavItem>
                <NavLink tag="button" type="button" active={className === null} aria-pressed={className === null} onClick={() => setClassName(null)}>
                  Todas as turmas
                </NavLink>
              </NavItem>
              {classes.map((c) => (
                <NavItem key={c}>
                  <NavLink tag="button" type="button" active={className === c} aria-pressed={className === c} onClick={() => setClassName(c)}>
                    {c}
                  </NavLink>
                </NavItem>
              ))}
            </Nav>
          </div>
          <ul className="kds-grid">
            {visible.map((r) => (
              <li key={r.studentId}>
                <button
                  type="button"
                  className={r.studentId === studentId ? "kds-card is-delivered" : "kds-card"}
                  data-testid={`kds-counter-student-${r.studentId}`}
                  aria-pressed={r.studentId === studentId}
                  onClick={() => choose(r.studentId)}
                >
                  <span className="kds-card__name">{r.firstName}</span>
                  <span className="kds-card__class">{r.className}</span>
                  {r.guardianId ? <span>Saldo: {formatCents(r.balanceCents)}</span> : <span>Sem responsável financeiro</span>}
                </button>
              </li>
            ))}
          </ul>
        </CardBody>
      </Card>

      {student && (
        <Card className="main-card mb-3">
          <CardHeader tag="h2" className="h6 mb-0">Lançamento para {student.firstName}</CardHeader>
          <CardBody>
            <p className="mb-1">{student.guardianFirstName ? `Conta de ${student.guardianFirstName}` : "Sem responsável financeiro: peça à secretaria"}</p>
            {student.restrictions && <p><span className="badge text-bg-warning">Restrição: {student.restrictions}</span></p>}
            <Table responsive className="mb-3">
              <tbody>
                {items.map((i) => {
                  const qty = cart[i.id] ?? 0;
                  return (
                    <tr key={i.id}>
                      <td>{i.name}</td>
                      <td>{formatCents(i.priceCents)}</td>
                      <td className="text-end text-nowrap">
                        <Button color="light" type="button" aria-label={`Menos ${i.name}`} data-testid={`kds-counter-item-${i.id}-minus`} disabled={qty === 0} onClick={() => setCart(setQty(cart, i.id, qty - 1))}>
                          −
                        </Button>{" "}
                        <span className="fs-5 px-2" aria-live="polite">{qty}</span>{" "}
                        <Button color="light" type="button" aria-label={`Mais ${i.name}`} data-testid={`kds-counter-item-${i.id}-plus`} disabled={qty >= MAX_QTY} onClick={() => setCart(setQty(cart, i.id, qty + 1))}>
                          +
                        </Button>
                      </td>
                    </tr>
                  );
                })}
              </tbody>
            </Table>
            <dl className="row mb-3">
              <dt className="col-6">Total</dt>
              <dd className="col-6 text-end">{formatCents(total)}</dd>
              <dt className="col-6">Saldo atual</dt>
              <dd className="col-6 text-end">{formatCents(student.balanceCents)}</dd>
              <dt className="col-6">Saldo depois</dt>
              <dd className="col-6 text-end" data-testid="kds-counter-balance-after">{formatCents(after)}</dd>
            </dl>
            {overLimit && (
              <p role="alert" className="text-danger fw-semibold" data-testid="kds-counter-over-limit">
                {OVER_LIMIT_TEXT}
              </p>
            )}
            <Button color="primary" size="lg" type="button" data-testid="kds-counter-submit" disabled={!allowed || busy} onClick={() => void sell()}>
              Lançar
            </Button>
          </CardBody>
        </Card>
      )}

      <Card className="main-card mb-3">
        <CardHeader tag="h2" className="h6 mb-0">Lançamentos de hoje</CardHeader>
        <CardBody>
          {sales.length === 0 ? (
            <p className="mb-0">Nenhum lançamento hoje neste tablet.</p>
          ) : (
            <Table responsive hover striped className="mb-0">
              <tbody>
                {sales.map((s) => (
                  <tr key={s.saleId} data-testid={`kds-counter-sale-${s.saleId}`}>
                    <td>{s.studentFirstName}</td>
                    <td>{s.lines.map((l) => `${l.qty}x ${l.name}`).join(", ")}</td>
                    <td>{formatCents(s.totalCents)}</td>
                    <td>
                      <StatusBadge tone={s.voided ? "secondary" : STATUS[s.status].tone}>{s.voided ? "estornado" : STATUS[s.status].label}</StatusBadge>
                    </td>
                    <td className="text-end">
                      {!s.voided && s.status !== "rejected" && (
                        <Button
                          size="sm"
                          outline
                          color="danger"
                          type="button"
                          data-testid={`kds-counter-undo-${s.saleId}`}
                          onClick={() => {
                            if (window.confirm(`Desfazer o lançamento de ${s.studentFirstName}?`)) void onUndo(s.saleId);
                          }}
                        >
                          Desfazer
                        </Button>
                      )}
                    </td>
                  </tr>
                ))}
              </tbody>
            </Table>
          )}
        </CardBody>
      </Card>
      {roster.length === 0 && <Alert color="secondary" fade={false}>Sem lista de alunos.</Alert>}
    </div>
  );
}
```

`apps/web/src/areas/kds/CounterPanel.tsx` (lê e escreve só no IndexedDB; regra 32):

```tsx
import type { RosterItem } from "../../ui/counter";
import { formatCents } from "../../ui/money";
import { useState } from "react";
import { Alert } from "reactstrap";
import { CounterView } from "./CounterView";
import { CounterSaleRefused, kdsDb, recordCounterSale, ROSTER_ITEMS, ROSTER_LIMIT, voidCounterSale } from "./db";
import { useLive } from "./useLive";

export function CounterPanel({ serviceDate }: { serviceDate: string }) {
  const roster = useLive(() => kdsDb.roster.toArray(), []);
  const limit = useLive(async () => Number((await kdsDb.meta.get(ROSTER_LIMIT))?.value ?? "0"), []);
  const items = useLive(async () => JSON.parse((await kdsDb.meta.get(ROSTER_ITEMS))?.value ?? "[]") as RosterItem[], []);
  const sales = useLive(async () => (await kdsDb.counterSales.where("serviceDate").equals(serviceDate).sortBy("occurredAt")).reverse(), [serviceDate]);
  const [notice, setNotice] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);

  if (roster === undefined || limit === undefined || items === undefined || sales === undefined) return <p>Carregando…</p>;
  if (roster.length === 0) {
    return (
      <div className="alert alert-secondary" data-testid="kds-counter-empty">
        Carregando a lista de alunos… Conecte o tablet à internet na primeira vez.
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <>
      {notice && <Alert color="success" fade={false} role="status">{notice}</Alert>}
      {error && <Alert color="danger" fade={false}>{error}</Alert>}
      <CounterView
        roster={roster}
        items={items}
        limitCents={limit}
        sales={sales}
        onSell={async (studentId, lines) => {
          try {
            await recordCounterSale({ serviceDate, studentId, lines });
            const name = roster.find((r) => r.studentId === studentId)?.firstName ?? "";
            setNotice(`Lançado para ${name}: ${formatCents(lines.reduce((s, l) => s + l.qty * l.unitPriceCents, 0))}.`);
            setError(null);
          } catch (err) {
            setNotice(null);
            setError(err instanceof CounterSaleRefused && err.reason === "over_limit" ? "Saldo insuficiente: peça à secretaria" : "Não foi possível lançar neste tablet. Tente de novo.");
          }
        }}
        onUndo={async (saleId) => {
          try {
            await voidCounterSale(saleId);
            setNotice("Lançamento desfeito.");
            setError(null);
          } catch {
            setError("Não foi possível desfazer neste tablet. Tente de novo.");
          }
        }}
      />
    </>
  );
}
```

```tsx
import { cleanup, fireEvent, render, screen, waitFor, within } from "@testing-library/react";
import { MemoryRouter, Outlet, Route, Routes } from "react-router";
import { afterEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";
import { AccountPage } from "./AccountPage";
import { AccountsAdmin } from "./AccountsAdmin";
import { CounterSaleAdmin } from "./CounterSaleAdmin";

type Reply = { status: number; body?: unknown };

/** Respostas por "MÉTODO url"; uma lista é consumida em ordem e o último item se repete. */
function mockApi(replies: Record<string, Reply | Reply[]>) {
  const fn = vi.fn<(url: string, init?: RequestInit) => Promise<Response>>(async (url, init) => {
    const key = `${init?.method ?? "GET"} ${url}`;
    const entry = replies[key];
    if (!entry) throw new Error(`unexpected request ${key}`);
    const reply = Array.isArray(entry) ? (entry.length > 1 ? entry.shift()! : entry[0]!) : entry;
    return reply.status === 204 ? new Response(null, { status: 204 }) : new Response(JSON.stringify(reply.body), { status: reply.status });
  });
  vi.stubGlobal("fetch", fn);
  return fn;
}
const calls = (fn: ReturnType<typeof mockApi>, key: string) => fn.mock.calls.filter((c) => `${c[1]?.method ?? "GET"} ${c[0]}` === key);
const bodyOf = (fn: ReturnType<typeof mockApi>, key: string, n = 0) => JSON.parse(calls(fn, key)[n]![1]!.body as string);

const U = (n: number) => `0193a000-0000-7000-8000-${String(n).padStart(12, "0")}`;
const [ANA, BRUNO, CARLA, LEO, MISTO, SALE, TOPUP] = [U(1), U(2), U(3), U(4), U(5), U(6), U(7)];

function renderAt(path: string, role = "secretaria") {
  const me = { id: U(99), email: "equipe@escola.test", role, schoolName: "Escola Sol" };
  render(
    <MemoryRouter initialEntries={[path]}>
      <Routes>
        <Route element={<Outlet context={{ me, meFailed: false }} />}>
          <Route path="/admin/contas" element={<AccountsAdmin />} />
          <Route path="/admin/contas/lancar" element={<CounterSaleAdmin />} />
          <Route path="/admin/contas/:guardianId" element={<AccountPage />} />
        </Route>
      </Routes>
    </MemoryRouter>,
  );
}

    fireEvent.click(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Lançar" }));
    await waitFor(() => expect(calls(fn, "POST /api/admin/v1/counter-sales")).toHaveLength(2));
    expect(bodyOf(fn, "POST /api/admin/v1/counter-sales", 0)).toEqual({ studentId: LEO, items: [{ menuItemId: MISTO, qty: 1 }] });
    expect(bodyOf(fn, "POST /api/admin/v1/counter-sales", 1)).toEqual({ studentId: LEO, items: [{ menuItemId: MISTO, qty: 1 }], allowOverLimit: true });
  });
});
```

`apps/web/src/ui/walletLabels.ts`:

```ts
import type { WalletEntryKind } from "@app-lanche/contracts";

export const ENTRY_LABELS: Record<WalletEntryKind, string> = {
  checkout_hold: "Usado em pedido",
  hold_release: "Devolução de saldo reservado",
  late_payment_credit: "Pagamento após o horário",
  cancel_credit: "Pedido cancelado",
  refund: "Reembolso",
  manual_adjustment: "Ajuste da escola",
  counter_charge: "Lanche no balcão",
  counter_void: "Estorno de lanche no balcão",
  topup_credit: "Recarga por Pix",
};

/** "Lanche no balcão — Bia" quando a entrada é de um aluno. */
export function entryLabel(kind: WalletEntryKind, studentFirstName: string | null): string {
  return studentFirstName ? `${ENTRY_LABELS[kind]} — ${studentFirstName}` : ENTRY_LABELS[kind];
}
```

`apps/web/src/areas/admin/accounts.ts`:

```ts
import type { AccountListResponse } from "@app-lanche/contracts";
import { formatCents, parsePriceToCents } from "../../ui/money";

export type AccountRow = AccountListResponse["accounts"][number];

const fold = (s: string) => s.normalize("NFD").replace(/\p{M}/gu, "").toLowerCase();

export function filterAccounts(rows: AccountRow[], filter: { query: string; onlyDebt: boolean }): AccountRow[] {
  const q = fold(filter.query.trim());
  return rows.filter(
    (a) => (!filter.onlyDebt || a.balanceCents < 0) && (q === "" || fold([a.firstName, ...a.students].join(" ")).includes(q)),
  );
}

/** "50,00" para o campo de valor (sem "R$"). */
export function centsToInput(cents: number): string {
  return formatCents(cents).replace("R$ ", "");
}

/** Limite aceita zero (sem débito); outros valores como em parsePriceToCents. */
export function parseLimitCents(text: string): number | null {
  return /^\s*0+(,0{1,2})?\s*$/.test(text) ? 0 : parsePriceToCents(text);
}

export function formatDayTime(iso: string): string {
  return new Date(iso).toLocaleString("pt-BR", { day: "2-digit", month: "2-digit", hour: "2-digit", minute: "2-digit" });
}
```

`apps/web/src/areas/admin/PixPanel.tsx`:

```tsx
import { TopupView } from "@app-lanche/contracts";
import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from "react";
import { Button, Input, Label } from "reactstrap";
import { apiFetch } from "../../app/api";
import { formatCents } from "../../ui/money";

const POLL_MS = 3000;

/** QR e copia-e-cola de uma recarga, acompanhando o pagamento a cada 3 s (sem cobrança ainda: pede de novo). */
export function PixPanel({ initial, onPaid }: { initial: TopupView; onPaid: () => void }) {
  const [topup, setTopup] = useState(initial);
  const [copied, setCopied] = useState(false);
  const onPaidRef = useRef(onPaid);
  onPaidRef.current = onPaid;

  useEffect(() => {
    setTopup(initial);
    let current = initial;
    let stopped = false;
    let timer: ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | undefined;
    const tick = async () => {
      try {
        const next = current.payment?.pixCopyPaste
          ? await apiFetch(`/api/admin/v1/topups/${initial.id}`, TopupView)
          : await apiFetch(`/api/admin/v1/topups/${initial.id}/charge`, TopupView, { method: "POST" });
        if (stopped) return;
        current = next;
        setTopup(next);
        if (next.status === "paid") onPaidRef.current();
      } catch {
        // Rede ou servidor: tenta de novo no próximo ciclo.
      }
      if (!stopped && current.status === "pending_payment") timer = setTimeout(() => void tick(), POLL_MS);
    };
    if (current.status === "pending_payment") timer = setTimeout(() => void tick(), POLL_MS);
    return () => {
      stopped = true;
      clearTimeout(timer);
    };
  }, [initial]);

  const pix = topup.payment;
  return (
    <div className="border rounded bg-light p-3 my-3" data-testid="pix-panel">
      <p className="fw-semibold">Pix de {formatCents(topup.amountCents)}</p>
      {topup.status === "paid" && <p className="mb-0 text-success fw-semibold">Pagamento recebido. Saldo agora: {formatCents(topup.balanceCents)}.</p>}
      {topup.status === "expired" && <p className="mb-0">Este Pix expirou. Gere um novo.</p>}
      {topup.status === "cancelled" && <p className="mb-0">Este Pix foi substituído por outro mais recente.</p>}
      {topup.status === "pending_payment" &&
        (pix?.pixCopyPaste ? (
          <>
            {pix.pixQrPngBase64 && <img alt="QR Code Pix" className="d-block mb-2 pix-qr" src={`data:image/png;base64,${pix.pixQrPngBase64}`} />}
            <Label for="pix-code">Pix copia e cola</Label>
            <Input id="pix-code" type="textarea" readOnly rows={3} value={pix.pixCopyPaste} />
            <Button
              className="mt-2"
              color="secondary"
              outline
              type="button"
              onClick={() => navigator.clipboard.writeText(pix.pixCopyPaste!).then(() => setCopied(true), () => setCopied(false))}
            >
              {copied ? "Código copiado" : "Copiar código Pix"}
            </Button>
            <p className="mt-2 mb-0">
              Aguardando o pagamento… vale até {new Date(pix.expiresAt).toLocaleTimeString("pt-BR", { hour: "2-digit", minute: "2-digit" })}.
            </p>
          </>
        ) : (
          <p className="mb-0">Gerando o Pix… aguarde alguns segundos.</p>
        ))}
    </div>
  );
}
```

```tsx
import { AccountListResponse, SchoolSettings } from "@app-lanche/contracts";
import { useCallback, useEffect, useState, type FormEvent } from "react";
import { Link } from "react-router";
import { Alert, Button, Card, CardBody, CardHeader, Col, Form, FormGroup, Input, Label, Row, Table } from "reactstrap";
import { apiFetch } from "../../app/api";
import { PageTitle } from "../../theme/layout/PageTitle";
import { announce, StatusNotice } from "../../theme/notify";
import { StatusBadge } from "../../theme/StatusBadge";
import { formatCents } from "../../ui/money";
import { centsToInput, filterAccounts, formatDayTime, parseLimitCents } from "./accounts";
import { useAdminMe } from "./AdminLayout";
import { useStaffErrors } from "./useStaffErrors";

function DebtLimitCard({ canEdit, onSaved }: { canEdit: boolean; onSaved: () => void }) {
  const { error, setError, fail } = useStaffErrors();
  const [limit, setLimit] = useState<number | null>(null);
  const [text, setText] = useState("");
  const [status, setStatus] = useState<string | null>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    apiFetch("/api/admin/v1/school/settings", SchoolSettings).then((s) => {
      setLimit(s.debtLimitCents);
      setText(centsToInput(s.debtLimitCents));
    }, fail);
  }, [fail]);

  async function save(event: FormEvent) {
    event.preventDefault();
    setError(null);
    setStatus(null);
    const cents = parseLimitCents(text);
    if (cents === null) {
      setError("Informe um valor em reais, por exemplo 50,00.");
      return;
    }
    try {
      const saved = await apiFetch("/api/admin/v1/school/settings", SchoolSettings, { method: "PUT", body: { debtLimitCents: cents } });
      setLimit(saved.debtLimitCents);
      setStatus("Limite salvo.");
      announce("Limite salvo.");
      onSaved();
    } catch (err) {
      fail(err);
    }
  }

  return (
    <Card tag="section" className="main-card mb-3">
      <CardHeader tag="h2" className="h6 mb-0">Limite de débito</CardHeader>
      <CardBody>
        <StatusNotice message={status} />
        {error && <Alert color="danger" fade={false}>{error}</Alert>}
        {limit === null ? (
          <p className="mb-0">Carregando…</p>
        ) : canEdit ? (
          <Form onSubmit={save} className="d-flex gap-2 align-items-end flex-wrap">
            <FormGroup className="mb-0">
              <Label for="debt-limit">Cada responsável pode ficar devendo até (R$)</Label>
              <Input id="debt-limit" inputMode="decimal" value={text} onChange={(e) => setText(e.target.value)} />
            </FormGroup>
            <Button color="primary" type="submit">Salvar limite</Button>
          </Form>
        ) : (
          <p className="mb-0">Cada responsável pode ficar devendo até {formatCents(limit)}.</p>
        )}
      </CardBody>
    </Card>
  );
}

export function AccountsAdmin() {
  const { me } = useAdminMe();
  const { error, fail } = useStaffErrors();
  const [data, setData] = useState<AccountListResponse | null>(null);
  const [query, setQuery] = useState("");
  const [onlyDebt, setOnlyDebt] = useState(false);

  const load = useCallback(async () => {
    try {
      setData(await apiFetch("/api/admin/v1/accounts", AccountListResponse));
    } catch (err) {
      fail(err);
    }
  }, [fail]);
  useEffect(() => void load(), [load]);

  const office = me?.role === "admin" || me?.role === "secretaria";
  const rows = data ? filterAccounts(data.accounts, { query, onlyDebt }) : [];

  return (
    <>
      <PageTitle
        heading="Contas"
        subheading="Saldos, débitos e pagamentos por Pix."
        icon="pe-7s-cash"
        actions={office ? <Link className="btn btn-primary" to="/admin/contas/lancar">Lançar no balcão</Link> : undefined}
      />
      {error && <Alert color="danger" fade={false}>{error}</Alert>}
      {data && data.overLimitSales.length > 0 && (
        <Alert color="warning" fade={false} data-testid="over-limit-alert">
          <p className="mb-1 fw-semibold">Lançamentos acima do limite de débito nos últimos 7 dias</p>
          <ul className="mb-0">
            {data.overLimitSales.map((s) => (
              <li key={s.saleId}>
                {formatDayTime(s.occurredAt)} · {s.studentFirstName} (conta de <Link to={`/admin/contas/${s.guardianId}`}>{s.guardianFirstName}</Link>) ·{" "}
                {formatCents(s.totalCents)}
              </li>
            ))}
          </ul>
        </Alert>
      )}
      <DebtLimitCard canEdit={me?.role === "admin"} onSaved={() => void load()} />
      <Card className="main-card mb-3">
        <CardBody>
          <Row className="g-2 mb-3">
            <Col md={8}>
              <Label for="accounts-query">Buscar responsável ou aluno</Label>
              <Input id="accounts-query" type="search" value={query} onChange={(e) => setQuery(e.target.value)} />
            </Col>
            <Col md={4} className="d-flex align-items-end">
              <FormGroup check className="mb-2">
                <Input id="accounts-debt" type="checkbox" checked={onlyDebt} onChange={(e) => setOnlyDebt(e.target.checked)} />
                <Label check for="accounts-debt">Só com débito</Label>
              </FormGroup>
            </Col>
          </Row>
          {!data ? (
            <p className="mb-0">Carregando…</p>
          ) : rows.length === 0 ? (
            <p className="mb-0">Nenhum responsável neste filtro.</p>
          ) : (
            <Table responsive hover striped className="mb-0">
              <thead>
                <tr><th>Responsável</th><th>Alunos</th><th>Saldo</th></tr>
              </thead>
              <tbody>
                {rows.map((a) => (
                  <tr key={a.guardianId}>
                    <td><Link to={`/admin/contas/${a.guardianId}`}>{a.firstName}</Link></td>
                    <td>{a.students.join(", ") || "—"}</td>
                    <td>
                      {a.balanceCents < 0 ? (
                        <StatusBadge tone={a.balanceCents < -data.debtLimitCents ? "danger" : "warning"}>débito {formatCents(-a.balanceCents)}</StatusBadge>
                      ) : (
                        formatCents(a.balanceCents)
                      )}
                    </td>
                  </tr>
                ))}
              </tbody>
            </Table>
          )}
        </CardBody>
      </Card>
    </>
  );
}
```

`apps/web/src/areas/admin/AccountPage.tsx`:

```tsx
import { AccountStatementResponse, CounterVoidResult, PaymentRequestResponse, TopupView } from "@app-lanche/contracts";
import { useCallback, useEffect, useState, type FormEvent } from "react";
import { Link, useParams } from "react-router";
import { Alert, Button, Card, CardBody, CardHeader, Form, FormGroup, Input, Label, Table } from "reactstrap";
import { apiFetch } from "../../app/api";
import { PageTitle } from "../../theme/layout/PageTitle";
import { announce, StatusNotice } from "../../theme/notify";
import { StatusBadge } from "../../theme/StatusBadge";
import { formatCents, parsePriceToCents } from "../../ui/money";
import { entryLabel } from "../../ui/walletLabels";
import { centsToInput, formatDayTime } from "./accounts";
import { useAdminMe } from "./AdminLayout";
import { PixPanel } from "./PixPanel";
import { useRequestKey } from "./useRequestKey";
import { useStaffErrors } from "./useStaffErrors";

export function AccountPage() {
  const { guardianId } = useParams();
  const { me } = useAdminMe();
  const { error, setError, fail } = useStaffErrors();
  const [data, setData] = useState<AccountStatementResponse | null>(null);
  const [amount, setAmount] = useState("");
  const [topup, setTopup] = useState<TopupView | null>(null);
  const [status, setStatus] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const qr = useRequestKey();
  const link = useRequestKey();

  const load = useCallback(async () => {
    try {
      const d = await apiFetch(`/api/admin/v1/guardians/${guardianId}/statement`, AccountStatementResponse);
      setData(d);
      setAmount((current) => current || (d.balanceCents < 0 ? centsToInput(-d.balanceCents) : ""));
    } catch (err) {
      fail(err);
    }
  }, [guardianId, fail]);
  useEffect(() => void load(), [load]);

  const office = me?.role === "admin" || me?.role === "secretaria";
  function done(message: string) {
    setStatus(message);
    announce(message);
  }

  async function generateQr(event: FormEvent) {
    event.preventDefault();
    setError(null);
    const cents = parsePriceToCents(amount);
    if (cents === null) {
      setError("Informe um valor em reais, por exemplo 13,50.");
      return;
    }
    const body = { amountCents: cents };
    await qr.once(async () => {
      try {
        setTopup(await apiFetch(`/api/admin/v1/guardians/${guardianId}/topups`, TopupView, { method: "POST", body, idempotencyKey: qr.keyFor(body) }));
        qr.succeeded();
      } catch (err) {
        fail(err);
      }
    });
  }

  async function sendLink() {
    setError(null);
    await link.once(async () => {
      try {
        const r = await apiFetch(`/api/admin/v1/guardians/${guardianId}/payment-requests`, PaymentRequestResponse, {
          method: "POST",
          idempotencyKey: link.keyFor({ guardianId, balance: data?.balanceCents }),
        });
        link.succeeded();
        done(`Link de pagamento de ${formatCents(r.amountDueCents)} enviado para o WhatsApp do responsável.`);
      } catch (err) {
        fail(err);
      }
    });
  }

  async function voidSale(saleId: string) {
    if (!window.confirm("Estornar este lançamento? O valor volta para a conta.")) return;
    setError(null);
    try {
      await apiFetch(`/api/admin/v1/counter-sales/${saleId}/void`, CounterVoidResult, { method: "POST" });
      done("Lançamento estornado.");
      await load();
    } catch (err) {
      fail(err);
    }
  }

  if (!data) return error ? <Alert color="danger" fade={false}>{error}</Alert> : <p>Carregando…</p>;
  const debt = data.balanceCents < 0;

  return (
    <>
      <PageTitle
        heading={`Conta de ${data.guardian.firstName}`}
        subheading="Extrato e pagamentos por Pix."
        icon="pe-7s-cash"
        actions={<Link className="btn btn-outline-secondary" to="/admin/contas">Voltar</Link>}
      />
      <StatusNotice message={status} />
      {error && <Alert color="danger" fade={false}>{error}</Alert>}
      <Card className="main-card mb-3">
        <CardBody>
          <p className="fs-5 mb-1">
            {debt ? <strong className="text-danger">Débito: {formatCents(-data.balanceCents)}</strong> : <>Saldo: {formatCents(data.balanceCents)}</>}
          </p>
          <p className="mb-3">Limite de débito da escola: {formatCents(data.debtLimitCents)}.</p>
          {debt && (
            <Button color="primary" outline type="button" onClick={() => void sendLink()}>
              Enviar link de pagamento por WhatsApp
            </Button>
          )}
        </CardBody>
      </Card>
      <Card tag="section" className="main-card mb-3">
        <CardHeader tag="h2" className="h6 mb-0">Pagamento por Pix na tela</CardHeader>
        <CardBody>
          <Form onSubmit={generateQr} className="d-flex gap-2 align-items-end flex-wrap">
            <FormGroup className="mb-0">
              <Label for="pix-amount">Valor do Pix (R$)</Label>
              <Input id="pix-amount" inputMode="decimal" value={amount} onChange={(e) => setAmount(e.target.value)} />
            </FormGroup>
            <Button color="primary" type="submit">Gerar QR de pagamento</Button>
          </Form>
          {topup && (
            <PixPanel
              initial={topup}
              onPaid={() => {
                done("Pagamento recebido.");
                void load();
              }}
            />
          )}
        </CardBody>
      </Card>
      <Card tag="section" className="main-card mb-3">
        <CardHeader tag="h2" className="h6 mb-0">Extrato</CardHeader>
        <CardBody>
          {data.entries.length === 0 ? (
            <p className="mb-0">Nenhum lançamento.</p>
          ) : (
            <Table responsive hover striped className="mb-0">
              <thead>
                <tr><th>Data</th><th>Lançamento</th><th>Valor</th><th>Situação</th><th><span className="visually-hidden">Ações</span></th></tr>
              </thead>
              <tbody>
                {data.entries.map((e) => (
                  <tr key={e.id}>
                    <td>{formatDayTime(e.createdAt)}</td>
                    <td>{entryLabel(e.kind, e.studentFirstName)}</td>
                    <td>{e.amountCents > 0 ? "+" : ""}{formatCents(e.amountCents)}</td>
                    <td>
                      {e.overLimit && <StatusBadge tone="danger">acima do limite</StatusBadge>}{" "}
                      {e.kind === "counter_charge" && e.voided && <StatusBadge tone="secondary">estornado</StatusBadge>}
                    </td>
                    <td className="text-end">
                      {office && e.kind === "counter_charge" && !e.voided && e.saleId && (
                        <Button size="sm" outline color="danger" type="button" aria-label={`Estornar lançamento de ${e.studentFirstName ?? ""}`} onClick={() => void voidSale(e.saleId!)}>
                          Estornar
                        </Button>
                      )}
                    </td>
                  </tr>
                ))}
              </tbody>
            </Table>
          )}
        </CardBody>
      </Card>
    </>
  );
}
```

`apps/web/src/areas/admin/CounterSaleAdmin.tsx`:

```tsx
import { CounterRosterResponse, CounterSaleResult } from "@app-lanche/contracts";
import { useCallback, useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { Link } from "react-router";
import { Alert, Button, Card, CardBody, Col, FormGroup, Input, Label, Row, Table } from "reactstrap";
import { ApiError, apiFetch } from "../../app/api";
import { PageTitle } from "../../theme/layout/PageTitle";
import { announce, StatusNotice } from "../../theme/notify";
import { canSell, cartLines, cartTotal, filterRoster, MAX_QTY, rosterRows, setQty, type Cart } from "../../ui/counter";
import { formatCents } from "../../ui/money";
import { useRequestKey } from "./useRequestKey";
import { useStaffErrors } from "./useStaffErrors";

/** Lançamento online da secretaria: acima do limite só com confirmação explícita (spec F5B). */
export function CounterSaleAdmin() {
  const { error, setError, fail } = useStaffErrors();
  const [roster, setRoster] = useState<CounterRosterResponse | null>(null);
  const [query, setQuery] = useState("");
  const [studentId, setStudentId] = useState("");
  const [cart, setCart] = useState<Cart>({});
  const [status, setStatus] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const request = useRequestKey();

  const load = useCallback(async () => {
    try {
      setRoster(await apiFetch("/api/admin/v1/counter/roster", CounterRosterResponse));
    } catch (err) {
      fail(err);
    }
  }, [fail]);
  useEffect(() => void load(), [load]);

  if (!roster) return error ? <Alert color="danger" fade={false}>{error}</Alert> : <p>Carregando…</p>;
  const rows = rosterRows(roster, []);
  const visible = filterRoster(rows, { query, className: null }).sort((a, b) => a.firstName.localeCompare(b.firstName));
  const student = rows.find((r) => r.studentId === studentId) ?? null;
  const lines = cartLines(cart, roster.items);
  const total = cartTotal(cart, roster.items);
  const after = (student?.balanceCents ?? 0) - total;
  const overLimit = !!student?.guardianId && total > 0 && !canSell(student.balanceCents, total, roster.debtLimitCents);

  async function submit(allowOverLimit: boolean): Promise<void> {
    if (!student?.guardianId || lines.length === 0) return;
    const body = { studentId: student.studentId, items: lines.map(({ menuItemId, qty }) => ({ menuItemId, qty })), ...(allowOverLimit ? { allowOverLimit: true } : {}) };
    setError(null);
    try {
      const r = await apiFetch("/api/admin/v1/counter-sales", CounterSaleResult, { method: "POST", body, idempotencyKey: request.keyFor(body) });
      request.succeeded();
      const message = `Lançado para ${student.firstName}: ${formatCents(r.totalCents)}. Saldo de ${student.guardianFirstName}: ${formatCents(r.balanceCents)}.`;
      setStatus(message);
      announce(message);
      setCart({});
      setStudentId("");
      await load();
    } catch (err) {
      const limitHit = err instanceof ApiError && err.code === "insufficient_balance";
      if (!allowOverLimit && limitHit && window.confirm(`O saldo de ${student.guardianFirstName} passa do limite de débito (${formatCents(roster!.debtLimitCents)}). Lançar mesmo assim?`)) {
        await submit(true);
        return;
      }
      fail(err);
    }
  }

  return (
    <>
      <PageTitle
        heading="Lançar no balcão"
        subheading="Lançamento na conta do responsável financeiro do aluno."
        icon="pe-7s-cash"
        actions={<Link className="btn btn-outline-secondary" to="/admin/contas">Voltar</Link>}
      />
      <StatusNotice message={status} />
      {error && <Alert color="danger" fade={false}>{error}</Alert>}
      <Card className="main-card mb-3">
        <CardBody>
          <Row className="g-2">
            <Col md={6}>
              <FormGroup>
                <Label for="counter-query">Buscar aluno</Label>
                <Input id="counter-query" type="search" value={query} onChange={(e) => setQuery(e.target.value)} />
              </FormGroup>
            </Col>
            <Col md={6}>
              <FormGroup>
                <Label for="counter-student">Aluno</Label>
                <Input id="counter-student" type="select" value={studentId} onChange={(e) => { setStudentId(e.target.value); setCart({}); }}>
                  <option value="">Escolha…</option>
                  {visible.map((r) => (
                    <option key={r.studentId} value={r.studentId}>
                      {r.firstName} — {r.className} {r.guardianFirstName ? `(conta de ${r.guardianFirstName})` : "(sem responsável financeiro)"}
                    </option>
                  ))}
                </Input>
              </FormGroup>
            </Col>
          </Row>
          {student && (
            <>
              <Table responsive className="mb-3">
                <tbody>
                  {roster.items.map((i) => (
                    <tr key={i.id}>
                      <td><Label for={`qty-${i.id}`} className="mb-0">Quantidade de {i.name}</Label></td>
                      <td>{formatCents(i.priceCents)}</td>
                      <td style={{ width: "8rem" }}>
                        <Input id={`qty-${i.id}`} type="number" min={0} max={MAX_QTY} value={cart[i.id] ?? 0} onChange={(e) => setCart(setQty(cart, i.id, Number(e.target.value)))} />
                      </td>
                    </tr>
                  ))}
                </tbody>
              </Table>
              <p className="mb-1">Total: {formatCents(total)} · Saldo atual: {formatCents(student.balanceCents)} · Saldo depois: {formatCents(after)}</p>
              {!student.guardianId && <p className="text-danger">Este aluno não tem responsável financeiro. Defina um em "Alunos e responsáveis".</p>}
              {overLimit && <p className="text-danger">Passa do limite de débito: será pedida confirmação.</p>}
              <Button color="primary" type="button" disabled={!student.guardianId || lines.length === 0} onClick={() => void request.once(() => submit(false))}>
                Lançar
              </Button>
            </>
          )}
        </CardBody>
      </Card>
    </>
  );
}
```

```ts
export function adminNav(role: StaffRole | undefined): NavItem[] {
  const items: NavItem[] = [{ to: "/admin", label: "Início", icon: "pe-7s-home", isActive: home("") }];
  const accounts: NavItem = { to: "/admin/contas", label: "Contas", icon: "pe-7s-cash", isActive: under("/admin/contas") };
  if (role === "cozinha") return [...items, accounts];
  if (role !== "admin" && role !== "secretaria") return items;
  return [
    ...items,
    { to: "/admin/alunos", label: "Alunos e responsáveis", icon: "pe-7s-users", isActive: under("/admin/alunos") },
    accounts,
    { to: "/admin#cardapio", label: "Cardápio", icon: "pe-7s-note2", isActive: home("#cardapio") },
    { to: "/admin#dias", label: "Dias de serviço", icon: "pe-7s-date", isActive: home("#dias") },
    { to: "/admin/documentos", label: "Documentos", icon: "pe-7s-news-paper", isActive: under("/admin/documentos") },
    { to: "/admin#tablets", label: "Tablets da cozinha", icon: "pe-7s-display2", isActive: home("#tablets") },
  ];
}
```

```tsx
import { cleanup, fireEvent, render, screen, waitFor } from "@testing-library/react";
import { MemoryRouter, Route, Routes } from "react-router";
import { afterEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";
import { TopupPage } from "./TopupPage";
import { WalletPage } from "./WalletPage";

type Reply = { status: number; body: unknown };
function mockApi(replies: Record<string, Reply>) {
  const fn = vi.fn<(url: string, init?: RequestInit) => Promise<Response>>(async (url, init) => {
    const key = `${init?.method ?? "GET"} ${url}`;
    const reply = replies[key];
    if (!reply) throw new Error(`unexpected request ${key}`);
    return new Response(JSON.stringify(reply.body), { status: reply.status });
  });
  vi.stubGlobal("fetch", fn);
  return fn;
}
const ok = (body: unknown): Reply => ({ status: 200, body });
const U = (n: number) => `00000000-0000-4000-8000-${String(n).padStart(12, "0")}`;
const TOPUP = U(7);

function renderAt(path: string) {
  render(
    <MemoryRouter initialEntries={[path]}>
      <Routes>
        <Route path="/carteira" element={<WalletPage />} />
        <Route path="/carteira/recarga/:id" element={<TopupPage />} />
      </Routes>
    </MemoryRouter>,
  );
}

const WALLET = {
  balanceCents: -1350,
  debtLimitCents: 5000,
  financialFor: ["Bia", "Léo"],
  entries: [
    { id: U(1), kind: "counter_charge", amountCents: -900, createdAt: "2030-05-10T12:00:00.000Z", saleId: U(2), studentFirstName: "Léo", overLimit: false, voided: false },
    { id: U(3), kind: "topup_credit", amountCents: 2000, createdAt: "2030-05-09T12:00:00.000Z", saleId: null, studentFirstName: null, overLimit: false, voided: false },
  ],
  otherSales: [{ id: U(4), studentFirstName: "Léo", occurredAt: "2030-05-08T12:00:00.000Z", totalCents: 450, voided: false }],
};
const view = (status: string, pix: string | null) => ({
  id: TOPUP,
  status,
  amountCents: 1350,
  createdAt: "2030-05-10T12:01:00.000Z",
  paidAt: status === "paid" ? "2030-05-10T12:03:00.000Z" : null,
  balanceCents: status === "paid" ? 0 : -1350,
  payment: { status: status === "paid" ? "paid" : "pending", amountCents: 1350, pixCopyPaste: pix, pixQrPngBase64: null, expiresAt: "2030-05-10T13:01:00.000Z" },
});

afterEach(() => {
  cleanup();
  vi.unstubAllGlobals();
});

describe("guardian wallet", () => {
  it("highlights the debt, lists counter entries and other guardians' sales", async () => {
    mockApi({ "GET /api/v1/wallet": ok(WALLET) });
    renderAt("/carteira");
    expect(await screen.findByText("Débito: R$ 13,50")).toBeTruthy();
    expect(screen.getByText(/Lanche no balcão — Léo/)).toBeTruthy();
    expect(screen.getByText(/Recarga por Pix/)).toBeTruthy();
    expect(screen.getByText("Lanches lançados na conta de outro responsável")).toBeTruthy();
  });

  it("pays the debt with an idempotent request and opens the Pix", async () => {
    const fn = mockApi({
      "GET /api/v1/wallet": ok(WALLET),
      "POST /api/v1/topups": ok(view("pending_payment", "00020101FAKEx")),
      [`GET /api/v1/topups/${TOPUP}`]: ok(view("pending_payment", "00020101FAKEx")),
    });
    renderAt("/carteira");
    fireEvent.click(await screen.findByRole("button", { name: "Pagar débito (R$ 13,50)" }));
    expect(((await screen.findByLabelText("Código Pix")) as HTMLTextAreaElement).value).toBe("00020101FAKEx");
    const post = fn.mock.calls.find((c) => c[1]?.method === "POST")!;
    expect(JSON.parse(post[1]!.body as string)).toEqual({ purpose: "debt" });
    expect((post[1]!.headers as Record<string, string>)["idempotency-key"]).toBeTruthy();
  });

  it("validates free credits between R$ 10,00 and R$ 1.000,00", async () => {
    const fn = mockApi({ "GET /api/v1/wallet": ok({ ...WALLET, balanceCents: 500 }), "POST /api/v1/topups": ok(view("pending_payment", "x")), [`GET /api/v1/topups/${TOPUP}`]: ok(view("pending_payment", "x")) });
    renderAt("/carteira");
    const input = await screen.findByLabelText("Valor (R$ 10,00 a R$ 1.000,00)");
    expect(screen.queryByRole("button", { name: /Pagar débito/ })).toBeNull();
    fireEvent.change(input, { target: { value: "5" } });
    fireEvent.click(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Adicionar créditos" }));
    expect((await screen.findByRole("alert")).textContent).toBe("Informe um valor entre R$ 10,00 e R$ 1.000,00.");
    expect(fn.mock.calls.some((c) => c[1]?.method === "POST")).toBe(false);
    fireEvent.change(input, { target: { value: "25,50" } });
    fireEvent.click(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Adicionar créditos" }));
    await waitFor(() => expect(fn.mock.calls.some((c) => c[1]?.method === "POST")).toBe(true));
    const post = fn.mock.calls.find((c) => c[1]?.method === "POST")!;
    expect(JSON.parse(post[1]!.body as string)).toEqual({ purpose: "credit", amountCents: 2550 });
  });

  it("shows the confirmation with the new balance once paid", async () => {
    mockApi({ [`GET /api/v1/topups/${TOPUP}`]: ok(view("paid", "x")) });
    renderAt(`/carteira/recarga/${TOPUP}`);
    expect(await screen.findByText("Pagamento confirmado! Saldo: R$ 0,00")).toBeTruthy();
  });
});
```

`apps/web/src/areas/guardian/WalletPage.tsx` (substitua o arquivo; área do responsável: sem reactstrap):

```tsx
import { TOPUP_MAX_CENTS, TOPUP_MIN_CENTS, TopupView, WalletResponse, type GuardianTopupRequest } from "@app-lanche/contracts";
import { useEffect, useRef, useState, type FormEvent } from "react";
import { useNavigate } from "react-router";
import { ApiError, apiFetch } from "../../app/api";
import { errorMessage } from "../../ui/messages";
import { formatCents, parsePriceToCents } from "../../ui/money";
import { entryLabel } from "../../ui/walletLabels";

const day = (iso: string) => new Date(iso).toLocaleDateString("pt-BR", { day: "2-digit", month: "2-digit" });
const names = (list: string[]) => (list.length <= 1 ? list.join("") : `${list.slice(0, -1).join(", ")} e ${list[list.length - 1]}`);

export function WalletPage() {
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const [wallet, setWallet] = useState<WalletResponse | null>(null);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [amount, setAmount] = useState("");
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);
  // Mesma chave enquanto o pedido é o mesmo e não deu certo: repetir depois de uma falha não cria outra recarga.
  const held = useRef<{ body: string; key: string } | null>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    apiFetch("/api/v1/wallet", WalletResponse).then(setWallet, (err) => setError(errorMessage(err instanceof ApiError ? err.code : "internal_error")));
  }, []);

  async function pay(body: GuardianTopupRequest) {
    const fingerprint = JSON.stringify(body);
    if (held.current?.body !== fingerprint) held.current = { body: fingerprint, key: crypto.randomUUID() };
    setBusy(true);
    setError(null);
    try {
      const topup = await apiFetch("/api/v1/topups", TopupView, { method: "POST", body, idempotencyKey: held.current.key });
      held.current = null;
      navigate(`/carteira/recarga/${topup.id}`);
    } catch (err) {
      setError(errorMessage(err instanceof ApiError ? err.code : "internal_error"));
    } finally {
      setBusy(false);
    }
  }

  function addCredit(event: FormEvent) {
    event.preventDefault();
    const cents = parsePriceToCents(amount);
    if (cents === null || cents < TOPUP_MIN_CENTS || cents > TOPUP_MAX_CENTS) {
      setError("Informe um valor entre R$ 10,00 e R$ 1.000,00.");
      return;
    }
    void pay({ purpose: "credit", amountCents: cents });
  }

  if (!wallet) return <main>{error ? <p role="alert">{error}</p> : <p>Carregando…</p>}</main>;
  const debt = wallet.balanceCents < 0;

  return (
    <main>
      <h1>Carteira</h1>
      {debt ? (
        <p>
          <strong>Débito: {formatCents(-wallet.balanceCents)}</strong>
        </p>
      ) : (
        <p>Saldo: {formatCents(wallet.balanceCents)}</p>
      )}
      {wallet.financialFor.length > 0 && <p>Você é o responsável financeiro de {names(wallet.financialFor)}.</p>}
      {debt && (
        <button type="button" disabled={busy} onClick={() => void pay({ purpose: "debt" })}>
          Pagar débito ({formatCents(-wallet.balanceCents)})
        </button>
      )}
      <form onSubmit={addCredit}>
        <label htmlFor="credit">Valor (R$ 10,00 a R$ 1.000,00)</label>
        <input id="credit" inputMode="decimal" value={amount} onChange={(e) => setAmount(e.target.value)} />
        <button type="submit" disabled={busy}>Adicionar créditos</button>
      </form>
      {error && <p role="alert">{error}</p>}
      <h2>Extrato</h2>
      {wallet.entries.length === 0 ? (
        <p>Nenhum lançamento.</p>
      ) : (
        <ul>
          {wallet.entries.map((e) => (
            <li key={e.id}>
              {day(e.createdAt)} · {entryLabel(e.kind, e.studentFirstName)} · {e.amountCents > 0 ? "+" : ""}
              {formatCents(e.amountCents)}
              {e.overLimit ? " · acima do limite" : ""}
              {e.kind === "counter_charge" && e.voided ? " · estornado" : ""}
            </li>
          ))}
        </ul>
      )}
      {wallet.otherSales.length > 0 && (
        <>
          <h2>Lanches lançados na conta de outro responsável</h2>
          <ul>
            {wallet.otherSales.map((s) => (
              <li key={s.id}>
                {day(s.occurredAt)} · {s.studentFirstName} · {formatCents(s.totalCents)}
                {s.voided ? " · estornado" : ""}
              </li>
            ))}
          </ul>
        </>
      )}
      <button type="button" onClick={() => navigate(-1)}>Voltar</button>
    </main>
  );
}
```

`apps/web/src/areas/guardian/TopupPage.tsx`:

```tsx
import { TopupView } from "@app-lanche/contracts";
import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from "react";
import { Link, useParams } from "react-router";
import { ApiError, apiFetch } from "../../app/api";
import { errorMessage } from "../../ui/messages";
import { formatCents } from "../../ui/money";

const FINAL_TEXT: Partial<Record<TopupView["status"], string>> = {
  expired: "Este Pix expirou. Gere outro na carteira.",
  cancelled: "Este Pix foi substituído por outro mais recente.",
};

/** Pix da recarga, consultado a cada 3 s como no checkout; sem cobrança ainda, pede de novo ao servidor. */
export function TopupPage() {
  const { id } = useParams();
  const [topup, setTopup] = useState<TopupView | null>(null);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [copied, setCopied] = useState(false);
  const timer = useRef<ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | undefined>(undefined);

  useEffect(() => {
    let stopped = false;
    let needsPix = false;
    const load = () =>
      (needsPix ? apiFetch(`/api/v1/topups/${id}/charge`, TopupView, { method: "POST" }) : apiFetch(`/api/v1/topups/${id}`, TopupView)).then(
        (t) => {
          if (stopped) return;
          setError(null);
          setTopup(t);
          needsPix = t.status === "pending_payment" && !t.payment?.pixCopyPaste;
          if (t.status === "pending_payment") timer.current = setTimeout(() => void load(), 3000);
        },
        (err) => {
          if (stopped) return;
          setError(errorMessage(err instanceof ApiError ? err.code : "internal_error"));
          const transient = err instanceof ApiError && (err.status === 0 || err.status >= 500);
          if (transient) timer.current = setTimeout(() => void load(), 3000);
        },
      );
    void load();
    return () => {
      stopped = true;
      clearTimeout(timer.current);
    };
  }, [id]);

  if (!topup) return <main>{error ? <p role="alert">{error}</p> : <p>Carregando…</p>}</main>;
  const pix = topup.payment;
  return (
    <main>
      <h1>Pix de {formatCents(topup.amountCents)}</h1>
      {topup.status === "pending_payment" &&
        (pix?.pixCopyPaste ? (
          <section>
            {pix.pixQrPngBase64 && <img alt="QR Code Pix" src={`data:image/png;base64,${pix.pixQrPngBase64}`} />}
            <textarea readOnly value={pix.pixCopyPaste} aria-label="Código Pix" />
            <button type="button" onClick={() => navigator.clipboard.writeText(pix.pixCopyPaste!).then(() => setCopied(true), () => setCopied(false))}>
              {copied ? "Código copiado" : "Copiar código Pix"}
            </button>
            <p>Aguardando o pagamento…</p>
          </section>
        ) : (
          <p>Gerando o Pix… aguarde alguns segundos.</p>
        ))}
      {topup.status === "paid" && <p>Pagamento confirmado! Saldo: {formatCents(topup.balanceCents)}</p>}
      {FINAL_TEXT[topup.status] && <p>{FINAL_TEXT[topup.status]}</p>}
      {error && <p role="alert">{error}</p>}
      <Link to="/carteira">Voltar à carteira</Link>
    </main>
  );
}
```

**Interfaces:**

- [ ] **Step 1: Semente**

`db/seed-dev.ts`:
- `guardians` ganha `{ id: id(0xa4), first: "Diego", full: null, cpf: null, phone: "+5511900000004" }` (o tipo do array passa a aceitar `full: string | null` e `cpf: string | null`).
- O vínculo de cada aluno passa a `"INSERT INTO guardian_students (school_id, guardian_id, student_id, financial) VALUES ($1, $2, $3, true) ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING"` (o primeiro e único responsável de cada aluno da semente é o financeiro; o `ON CONFLICT` sem alvo também cobre um banco antigo em que a migração já marcou o financeiro).
- Depois do laço de alunos: `await db.query("INSERT INTO guardian_students (school_id, guardian_id, student_id, financial) VALUES ($1, $2, $3, false) ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING", [SCHOOL, guardians[3]!.id, students[0]!.id]);` (Diego vê os lanches de Léo, sem a carteira de Ana).
- Depois dos itens do cardápio:

```ts
  // Saldos de demonstração. Cada lançamento entra uma vez (ids fixos + ON CONFLICT) e o saldo só muda se ele entrou,
  // mantendo saldo = soma dos lançamentos.
  async function post(entryId: string, guardianId: string, kind: string, amountCents: number, sourceType: string, sourceId: string, note: string | null) {
    await db.query("INSERT INTO wallets (id, school_id, guardian_id) VALUES ($1, $2, $3) ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING", [id(0xd0 + guardians.findIndex((g) => g.id === guardianId)), SCHOOL, guardianId]);
    const { rowCount } = await db.query(
      `INSERT INTO wallet_entries (id, school_id, wallet_id, kind, amount_cents, source_type, source_id, note)
       SELECT $1, $2, w.id, $4, $5, $6, $7, $8 FROM wallets w WHERE w.school_id = $2 AND w.guardian_id = $3
       ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING`,
      [entryId, SCHOOL, guardianId, kind, amountCents, sourceType, sourceId, note],
    );
    if (rowCount === 1) {
      await db.query("UPDATE wallets SET balance_cents = balance_cents + $3 WHERE school_id = $1 AND guardian_id = $2", [SCHOOL, guardianId, amountCents]);
    }
  }
  // Ana: R$ 30,00 de crédito.
  await post(id(0x5a1), guardians[0]!.id, "manual_adjustment", 3000, "adjustment", id(0x5a2), "Saldo de demonstração");
  // Bruno: lanche de Caio lançado pela secretaria (misto quente + suco), saldo -R$ 15,00.
  const sale = id(0x5b1);
  await post(id(0x5b2), guardians[1]!.id, "counter_charge", -1500, "counter_sale", sale, null);
  await db.query(
    `INSERT INTO counter_sales (id, school_id, student_id, guardian_id, source, staff_user_id, occurred_at, total_cents, over_limit, wallet_entry_id)
     VALUES ($1, $2, $3, $4, 'staff', $5, now(), 1500, false, $6) ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING`,
    [sale, SCHOOL, students[2]!.id, guardians[1]!.id, id(0xf1), id(0x5b2)],
  );
  for (const [item, price] of [[items[0]!, 900], [items[1]!, 600]] as const) {
    await db.query(
      "INSERT INTO counter_sale_items (school_id, sale_id, menu_item_id, qty, unit_price_cents) VALUES ($1, $2, $3, 1, $4) ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING",
      [SCHOOL, sale, item.id, price],
    );
  }
```

`db/dev-link.ts`: `const scope = scopeArg === "order" || scopeArg === "documents" || scopeArg === "wallet" ? scopeArg : null;` e as mensagens de uso passam a `[order|documents|wallet]`.

Run: `pnpm app:up` (sobe com a migração 0010 e a semente); `docker compose exec -T db psql -U postgres app_lanche -tAc "SELECT g.first_name, w.balance_cents FROM wallets w JOIN guardians g ON g.id = w.guardian_id ORDER BY 1"` → `Ana|3000` e `Bruno|-1500`; `… -tAc "SELECT count(*) FROM guardian_students WHERE financial"` → `4`. Rode `pnpm app:up` de novo: os números não mudam. `pnpm app:down`.

- [ ] **Step 2: Cenário e2e**

`e2e/global-setup.ts`: o vínculo passa a `INSERT INTO guardian_students (school_id, guardian_id, student_id, financial) VALUES ($1, $2, $3, true)` (os três alunos têm Ana como financeira).

`e2e/counter.spec.ts`:

```ts
import { createHash } from "node:crypto";
import { expect, test } from "@playwright/test";
import pg from "pg";
import { SCHOOL_ID } from "./global-setup";

const DB = "postgres://postgres:postgres@localhost:5432/app_lanche_e2e";
const SYNCED = /^0 pendentes · última sincronização \d{2}:\d{2}$/;
const CODE = "KDSE2E67";

async function query<T extends pg.QueryResultRow>(sql: string, params: unknown[] = []): Promise<T[]> {
  const c = new pg.Client({ connectionString: DB });
  await c.connect();
  try {
    return (await c.query<T>(sql, params)).rows;
  } finally {
    await c.end();
  }
}

/** A secretaria debita no servidor enquanto o tablet está sem rede (o tablet não fica sabendo). */
async function serverDebit(guardianId: string, cents: number): Promise<void> {
  await query(`INSERT INTO wallets (id, school_id, guardian_id) VALUES (gen_random_uuid(), $1, $2) ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING`, [SCHOOL_ID, guardianId]);
  await query(
    `INSERT INTO wallet_entries (id, school_id, wallet_id, kind, amount_cents, source_type, source_id)
     SELECT gen_random_uuid(), $1, w.id, 'manual_adjustment', $3, 'adjustment', gen_random_uuid() FROM wallets w WHERE w.guardian_id = $2`,
    [SCHOOL_ID, guardianId, -cents],
  );
  await query(`UPDATE wallets SET balance_cents = balance_cents - $2 WHERE guardian_id = $1`, [guardianId, cents]);
}

test("sells offline at the counter, debits the wallet once and flags the limit", async ({ browser }) => {
  await query(`UPDATE schools SET debt_limit_cents = 1000 WHERE id = $1`, [SCHOOL_ID]);
  await query(
    `INSERT INTO devices (id, school_id, kind, pairing_code_hash, pairing_expires_at) VALUES (gen_random_uuid(), $1, 'kitchen', $2, now() + interval '30 minutes')`,
    [SCHOOL_ID, createHash("sha256").update(CODE).digest("hex")],
  );
  const [day] = await query<{ date: string }>(`SELECT service_date::text AS date FROM service_days WHERE school_id = $1`, [SCHOOL_ID]);
  const students = await query<{ id: string; first_name: string; guardian_id: string }>(
    `SELECT s.id, s.first_name, gs.guardian_id FROM students s JOIN guardian_students gs ON gs.student_id = s.id AND gs.financial WHERE s.school_id = $1`,
    [SCHOOL_ID],
  );
  const leo = students.find((s) => s.first_name === "Léo")!;
  const bia = students.find((s) => s.first_name === "Bia")!;
  const [item] = await query<{ id: string }>(`SELECT id FROM menu_items WHERE school_id = $1 AND name = 'Misto quente'`, [SCHOOL_ID]);
  const balance = async () =>
    (await query<{ b: number }>(`SELECT balance_cents::int AS b FROM wallets WHERE guardian_id = $1`, [leo.guardian_id]))[0]?.b ?? 0;
  const charges = () =>
    query<{ id: string; over_limit: boolean; total: number }>(
      `SELECT cs.id, cs.over_limit, cs.total_cents::int AS total FROM counter_sales cs WHERE cs.school_id = $1`,
      [SCHOOL_ID],
    );

  const context = await browser.newContext({ timezoneId: "America/Sao_Paulo" });
  const page = await context.newPage();
  await page.goto(`/kds?date=${day!.date}`);
  await page.evaluate(async () => {
    await navigator.serviceWorker.ready;
  });
  await page.getByTestId("kds-pair-code").fill(CODE);
  await page.getByTestId("kds-pair-submit").click();
  await page.getByTestId("kds-tab-counter").click();
  await expect(page.getByTestId(`kds-counter-student-${leo.id}`)).toBeVisible({ timeout: 20_000 });
  await expect(page.getByTestId("kds-badge")).toHaveText(SYNCED, { timeout: 20_000 });

  // 1. Sem rede. No servidor, a conta de Ana passa a -R$ 5,00; o tablet ainda acha que está zerada.
  await context.setOffline(true);
  await serverDebit(leo.guardian_id, 500);
  await page.getByTestId(`kds-counter-student-${leo.id}`).click();
  await page.getByTestId(`kds-counter-item-${item!.id}-plus`).click();
  await expect(page.getByTestId("kds-counter-balance-after")).toHaveText("-R$ 9,00");
  await page.getByTestId("kds-counter-submit").click();
  await expect(page.getByTestId("kds-badge")).toContainText("1 pendente");

  // 2. Com o saldo local (-R$ 9,00), outro lanche passaria do limite de R$ 10,00: botão desligado.
  await page.getByTestId(`kds-counter-student-${bia.id}`).click();
  await page.getByTestId(`kds-counter-item-${item!.id}-plus`).click();
  await expect(page.getByTestId("kds-counter-submit")).toBeDisabled();
  await expect(page.getByTestId("kds-counter-over-limit")).toHaveText("Saldo insuficiente: peça à secretaria");

  // 3. Recarrega offline (service worker + IndexedDB): o lançamento continua pendente; nada chegou ao servidor.
  await page.reload();
  await page.getByTestId("kds-tab-counter").click();
  await expect(page.getByTestId("kds-badge")).toContainText("1 pendente");
  expect(await charges()).toEqual([]);

  // 4. Rede de volta: o lançamento entra uma vez, acima do limite no servidor (-R$ 14,00), e fica marcado.
  await context.setOffline(false);
  await expect(page.getByTestId("kds-badge")).toHaveText(SYNCED, { timeout: 30_000 });
  const stored = await charges();
  expect(stored).toEqual([{ id: expect.any(String), over_limit: true, total: 900 }]);
  expect(await balance()).toBe(-1400);
  await expect(page.getByTestId(`kds-counter-student-${leo.id}`)).toContainText("Saldo: -R$ 14,00");

  // 5. Reenviar o mesmo lançamento (com o cookie do tablet) é aceito sem debitar de novo.
  const replay = await page.evaluate(async (body) => {
    const res = await fetch("/api/kds/v1/counter-sales", { method: "POST", headers: { "content-type": "application/json" }, body: JSON.stringify(body) });
    return { status: res.status, json: (await res.json()) as { accepted: string[]; rejected: string[] } };
  }, { sales: [{ id: stored[0]!.id, studentId: leo.id, items: [{ menuItemId: item!.id, qty: 1 }], occurredAt: new Date().toISOString() }] });
  expect(replay).toEqual({ status: 200, json: { accepted: [stored[0]!.id], rejected: [] } });
  expect(await balance()).toBe(-1400);
  const entries = await query<{ n: number }>(`SELECT count(*)::int AS n FROM wallet_entries WHERE school_id = $1 AND kind = 'counter_charge'`, [SCHOOL_ID]);
  expect(entries[0]!.n).toBe(1);
  await context.close();
});
```

## Verificação final da fase

- Com o compose desligado (`pnpm app:down; docker compose down`): `pnpm typecheck && pnpm lint && pnpm test` passando; `pnpm --filter @app-lanche/api build` e `pnpm --filter @app-lanche/web build` passando.
- `pnpm db:up && pnpm test:e2e` passando (entregas offline, segundo tablet e lançamento offline no balcão); depois `docker compose down`.
- Passeio manual no Docker (`pnpm app:up`):
  1. `/admin` com `admin@escola.local` (`pnpm app:otp`): "Contas" lista Bruno com "débito R$ 15,00" primeiro; abrir a conta mostra "Lanche no balcão — Caio"; "Gerar QR de pagamento" sugere 15,00 e mostra o copia-e-cola; "Enviar link de pagamento por WhatsApp" grava `wallet.payment_requested` (`docker compose exec -T db psql -U postgres app_lanche -tAc "SELECT type, data::text FROM outbox_events WHERE type LIKE 'wallet.%' ORDER BY occurred_at"` — só primeiro nome, telefone e valores).
  2. "Lançar no balcão" para Léo além do limite (baixe o limite para R$ 10,00 no cartão): aparece a confirmação; confirmado, o extrato de Ana mostra "acima do limite" e a "Contas" mostra o aviso de ultrapassagem; "Estornar" devolve o valor.
  3. "Alunos e responsáveis": Léo tem Ana "financeiro" e Diego com "Tornar financeiro".
  4. `/kds` num navegador separado, pareado por um código de "Tablets da cozinha": aba "Lançar", desligar a rede nas ferramentas do navegador, lançar, religar: o selo volta a "0 pendentes" e a conta aparece atualizada na "Contas".
  5. `pnpm app:link +5511900000002 wallet`: abre `/carteira` com "Débito", "Pagar débito" leva a `/carteira/recarga/<id>` com o Pix; `pnpm app:link +5511900000004 wallet` (Diego) mostra os lanches de Léo em "Lanches lançados na conta de outro responsável" e nenhum débito.
  6. `pnpm app:down`.
- `grep -rn "getDb" apps/api/src/modules/{counter,topups}` não encontra nada; `grep -rn "SECURITY DEFINER" db/migrations/0010_counter_wallet.sql` só encontra o `CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION list_due_actions()`.
- Spec principal 4.1, 4.3, 5.7, 6.2, 6.3 e 14, spec de UI (menu) e `docs/{local,deploy,n8n}.md` atualizados.
